===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\README.md =====
# Kingshot Discord Bot

Gift-code AUTOREDEEM + Event reminders + Interactive / versus games.

## Features

### Gift Codes (original)
- `/register player` / `/register alliance`
- `/codes`, `/roster`, `/show`, `/myregistrations`
- `/auto start|stop|status|scan` (owner)
- Automatic redeem loop for registered targets

### Event Reminders
- `/eventreminder setup` â€” dropdowns + form: one-time, daily, every X days, weekly, monthly (UTC)
- Events with 2 slots: Bear Trap 1/2, Swordland Legion 1/2, Viking Vengeance 1/2, Tri-Alliance Legion 1/2 (+ Arena, Castle Battle, KVK, Cesares, SG, Alliance Brawl, Custom â€” Custom asks for a name)
- The reminder is a plain message: `<event emoji> **Arena** @everyone` + **only the text you wrote** + small footer
- Each reminder has its own emoji (default âš”ï¸); admins can change it with `/eventreminder edit`. No generated text
- Multiple offsets (e.g. 60 + 10 min before). Footer: `PXD_BOT Â· Event: HH:MM UTC`
- **Auto-removal:** the 10-min reminder replaces the 60-min one; leftovers are deleted when the event starts
- `/eventreminder list|edit|delete|toggle|test` â€” edit name, UTC time, offsets, emoji, and reminder text without recreating the schedule

### Event Blogs
- `/eventblog post` / `quick` â€” preview, pick channel + mention, then **Post now** or **Scheduleâ€¦**
- Same schedule types as reminders; `/eventblog list|delete|toggle` to manage them

### Games
| Command | Type |
|---------|------|
| `/games` | Opens the full Arcade lobby; choose any of the nine games inside the Activity |

Trivia (all Kingshot: troops, resources, buildings, heroes, events, pets) and words live in `games/data.js` â€” edit freely.

## Setup

1. Copy `.env.example` â†’ `.env` and fill values
2. Enable **Message Content Intent** in Discord Developer Portal (Bot â†’ Privileged Gateway Intents)
3. Install & run:
```bash
npm install
npm run register
npm start
```

## Environment

```env
DISCORDTOKEN=
CLIENTID=
AUTOREDEEM=true
AUTOINTERVALMINUTES=15
NOTIFYCHANNELID=
ERRORADMINUSERID=
REDEEMDELAYMS=2500
MAXPLAYERSPERUSER=5
MAXALLIANCESPERUSER=3
DATADIR=./data
REMINDERSDIR=/tmp/kingshot-bot
```

## Permissions needed
- Send Messages
- Embed Links
- Mention @everyone (for reminders)
- Use Application Commands

## Reminder storage note

Reminder JSON is stored separately at `/tmp/kingshot-bot/reminders.json` by default. Existing
`/tmp/reminders.json` and project `data/reminders.json` files are migrated automatically on
first start if found. The bot does not delete reminder definitions when they fire; it only
removes sent Discord notification messages according to the configured auto-removal behavior.

**Important:** `/tmp` is temporary on many hosting providers and may be cleared on redeploy or
machine replacement. For reminders to survive those events, configure `REMINDERSDIR` to a
persistent mounted disk or use a database. Keeping data in `/tmp` cannot guarantee persistence
across a host that wipes temporary storage.

---

## Kingshot Discord Activity

This project includes the graphical Kingshot Arcade frontend in `activity/` and a same-process Activity server in `activity-server.js`. The single `/games` command launches this Activity; players choose any of the nine games from its in-Activity lobby.

### Required environment variables

Add these to your hosting environment (do not commit `.env`):

```env
CLIENTID=your_discord_application_id
DISCORD_CLIENT_SECRET=your_discord_oauth2_client_secret
VITE_CLIENT_ID=your_discord_application_id
```

`DISCORD_CLIENT_SECRET` must remain server-side. `VITE_CLIENT_ID` is a public application ID embedded in the frontend at build time; it is not a secret.

### Discord Developer Portal setup

1. Open your existing bot application at https://discord.com/developers/applications.
2. Open **Activities** and configure URL Mappings.
3. Under **OAuth2 â†’ General**, add `https://127.0.0.1` as a Redirect URI and save. The Embedded App SDK handles the in-Activity authorization return.
4. Under **Activities â†’ URL Mappings**, map prefix `/` to the public HTTPS hostname serving this bot (hostname only; no `https://`).
5. Under **Activities â†’ Settings**, enable Activities and save. Discord creates a default **Launch** Entry Point command.
6. Enable **Developer Mode** in Discord User Settings â†’ Advanced while testing an unpublished Activity.
7. Launch the Kingshot Activity from the App Launcher in a voice channel. A second player must join that same Activity instance.

The public hostname must serve this same Node process over HTTPS. Configure your hosting provider's HTTPS domain/proxy to forward to the app's `PORT`.

### Deploy on the same host as the bot

Build the frontend during deployment, before starting the bot:

```bash
npm install
npm run build
npm start
```

`npm run build` installs the Activity's small Vite dependency set and writes static assets to `activity/dist`. The existing Node bot process serves those assets, handles Discord's OAuth code exchange at `/api/activity/token`, and hosts the multiplayer WebSocket endpoint at `/ws` on the same port.

Do not expose `DISCORD_CLIENT_SECRET` in frontend variables or source files. Do not copy your real `.env` into a public repository or share it in a ZIP.

### Local development on Windows / PowerShell

1. In the project root, create or update `.env` with `CLIENTID`, `DISCORD_CLIENT_SECRET`, and `VITE_CLIENT_ID`.
2. In terminal 1, run the bot/API server:

   ```powershell
   npm install
   npm start
   ```

3. In terminal 2, run the Activity dev server:

   ```powershell
   cd activity
   npm install
   npm run dev
   ```

4. In terminal 3, expose the Activity dev server with a temporary HTTPS tunnel:

   ```powershell
   cloudflared tunnel --url http://localhost:5173
   ```

5. Set the Discord Activity URL Mapping prefix `/` target to the tunnel hostname (hostname only, without `https://`). Vite reads `VITE_CLIENT_ID` from the project-root `.env` and proxies `/api` and `/ws` to the bot server on port 8080. If your bot uses another port, set `BOT_API_ORIGIN` before starting Vite, for example `$env:BOT_API_ORIGIN='http://127.0.0.1:9000'`.
6. Launch the Activity from Discord's App Launcher / generated Launch Entry Point. Open the same Activity instance with a second Discord account to test the two-player game.

A normal browser tab can show the Vite page, but the Discord SDK authorization requires the Activity to be opened inside Discord.

### Current game behavior / limitations

- Two Discord users in the same Activity instance share one server-authoritative Tic-Tac-Toe board. The server verifies the Discord OAuth identity and checks the active Activity instance with Discord's Activity Instance API before allowing play.
- The server validates whose turn it is and whether a cell is already occupied.
- A third user is rejected for now; spectator mode is a later improvement.
- Rematch resets the board for both players.
- Rooms are in memory and are not persisted across bot restarts. This is appropriate for live game sessions; reminder persistence remains separate.
- The single `/games` command launches the graphical Activity. The Activity can also be opened from Discord's App Launcher.

## Kingshot Arcade Activity â€” all games

The graphical Discord Activity now includes these nine games:

- Tic-Tac-Toe
- Connect Four
- Rock Paper Scissors (first to two round wins)
- Reaction Duel
- Kingshot Trivia
- Guess the Number
- Hangman
- Word Scramble
- Daily Challenge (the question set and answer order are date-seeded)

### Launching the Activity in Discord

1. In Discord Developer Portal, open the same application used by the bot.
2. Under **Activities â†’ Settings**, enable Activities.
3. Add an Activity URL Mapping with prefix `/` pointing to the public HTTPS hostname serving this bot. The mapping target is the hostname only, without `https://`.
4. Under **OAuth2 â†’ General**, add `https://127.0.0.1` as a redirect URI for local development if required by the Discord Activity setup.
5. Put `CLIENTID`, `DISCORDTOKEN`, `DISCORD_CLIENT_SECRET`, and `VITE_CLIENT_ID` in the root `.env`. `VITE_CLIENT_ID` is the same application Client ID as `CLIENTID`.
6. Install and build from the project root:

   ```bash
   npm install
   npm run build
   ```

7. Register commands:

   ```bash
   npm run register
   ```

   The registration script preserves the existing chat-input commands and updates Discord's single global Entry Point command to **Kingshot Arcade**.

8. Start the bot:

   ```bash
   npm start
   ```

9. In Discord, run `/games` to launch the graphical Activity from the slash command, or open the App Launcher and choose **Kingshot Arcade**. Invite another participant to the same Activity instance for 1v1 play.

### Gameplay command behavior

There is now one game slash command: `/games`. It opens the complete Kingshot Arcade Activity, where players select from all nine games. Individual game subcommands have been removed. The PXD-themed interface uses a dark medieval war-room palette with stone, iron, parchment, and royal-gold accents.

### Game behavior and limitations

- Tic-Tac-Toe and Connect Four enforce alternating turns on the server.
- Rock Paper Scissors is a first-to-two-rounds match.
- Reaction Duel uses a server-side random start delay and determines the first valid tap.
- Trivia and Daily Challenge use the existing Kingshot trivia pool in `games/data.js`; Daily Challenge uses a date-based deterministic question set.
- Guess the Number, Hangman, and Word Scramble use the existing Kingshot word/question data.
- Rooms are scoped to the Discord Activity instance and kept in memory. A bot restart ends active matches; long-term match persistence is not implemented.
- The Activity supports up to two participants per instance. Hangman is cooperative; trivia, guess, and scramble support a solo participant as well.
- The existing `/games stats` and `/games leaderboard` continue to reflect the legacy slash-command game statistics; Activity match results are not yet written into that statistics store.



===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\register-commands.js =====
require('dotenv').config();
const {
	REST,
	Routes
} = require('discord.js');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const commands = [];
const dir = path.join(__dirname, 'commands');
for (const file of fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.js'))) {
	const command = require(path.join(dir, file));
	if (command.data) {
		commands.push(command.data.toJSON());
		console.log(`Prepared /${command.data.name}`);
	}
}
const rest = new REST({
	version: '10'
}).setToken(process.env.DISCORDTOKEN);
(async () => {
	try {
		if (!process.env.DISCORDTOKEN || !process.env.CLIENTID) {
			throw new Error('Need DISCORDTOKEN and CLIENTID in .env');
		}
		// Activities have one global PRIMARY_ENTRY_POINT command. Reuse Discord's
		// auto-created Launch command when present, so we do not create a duplicate.
		let current = [];
		try {
			current = await rest.get(Routes.applicationCommands(process.env.CLIENTID));
		} catch (error) {
			console.warn('[activity] Could not read existing commands before registration:', error.message);
		}
		const existingEntry = current.find((command) => command.type === 4);
		const entryPoint = existingEntry ? {
			name: 'kingshot-arcade',
			description: 'Launch the Kingshot Arcade Activity',
			type: 4,
			handler: 2,
			integration_types: existingEntry.integration_types || [0, 1],
			contexts: existingEntry.contexts || [0, 1, 2],
		} : {
			name: 'kingshot-arcade',
			description: 'Launch the Kingshot Arcade Activity',
			type: 4,
			handler: 2,
			integration_types: [0, 1],
			contexts: [0, 1, 2],
		};
		const body = [...commands, entryPoint];
		console.log(`Registering ${commands.length} chat command(s) + Kingshot Arcade entry pointâ€¦`);
		const data = await rest.put(Routes.applicationCommands(process.env.CLIENTID), {
			body
		});
		console.log(`Registered ${data.length} application command(s).`);
		console.log('âœ… Open Discord App Launcher â†’ Kingshot Arcade to launch the graphical Activity.');
	} catch (error) {
		console.error('âŒ Failed to register Discord commands:', error);
		process.exitCode = 1;
	}
})();



===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\start.js =====
const {
	spawnSync
} = require('child_process');
console.log('ðŸ”„ Registering Discord slash commands before bot startup...');
const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['register-commands.js'], {
	cwd: __dirname,
	stdio: 'inherit',
});
if (result.error) {
	console.error('âš ï¸ Could not run slash-command registration:', result.error.message);
	console.error('Starting bot anywayâ€¦');
} else if (result.status !== 0) {
	console.error('âš ï¸ Slash-command registration failed (rate limit?). Starting bot anywayâ€¦');
} else {
	console.log('âœ… Slash commands registered.');
}
console.log('Starting bot...');
require('./index.js');

===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\activity\vite.config.js =====
import {
	defineConfig,
	loadEnv
} from 'vite';
export default defineConfig(({
	mode
}) => {
	// Read environment variables from the project root.
	const env = loadEnv(mode, '..', '');
	// Support both variable names.
	const clientId = process.env.VITE_CLIENT_ID || process.env.CLIENTID || env.VITE_CLIENT_ID || env.CLIENTID || '1559205652205936762';
	if (!clientId) {
		console.warn('[Vite] Discord Application ID is missing during build!');
	} else {
		console.log('[Vite] Discord Application ID configured.');
	}
	return {
		envDir: '..',
		// Explicitly inject the ID into the frontend.
		define: {
			'import.meta.env.VITE_CLIENT_ID': JSON.stringify(clientId)
		},
		server: {
			host: '0.0.0.0',
			port: 5173,
			strictPort: true,
			proxy: {
				'/api': {
					target: process.env.BOT_API_ORIGIN || 'http://127.0.0.1:8080',
					changeOrigin: true
				},
				'/ws': {
					target: (process.env.BOT_API_ORIGIN || 'http://127.0.0.1:8080').replace(/^http/, 'ws'),
					ws: true
				}
			}
		},
		build: {
			outDir: 'dist',
			emptyOutDir: true
		}
	};
});



===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\activity\src\discord.js =====
import {
	DiscordSDK
} from '@discord/embedded-app-sdk';
const CLIENT_ID = import.meta.env.VITE_CLIENT_ID;
export async function connectDiscord() {
	if (!CLIENT_ID) {
		throw new Error('VITE_CLIENT_ID is missing. Set it in the hosting environment or project-root .env.');
	}
	const discordSdk = new DiscordSDK(CLIENT_ID);
	await discordSdk.ready();
	const {
		code
	} = await discordSdk.commands.authorize({
		client_id: CLIENT_ID,
		response_type: 'code',
		state: '',
		prompt: 'none',
		// guilds is required for server-side admin checks.
		scope: ['identify', 'guilds', 'applications.commands'],
	});
	const response = await fetch('/api/activity/token', {
		method: 'POST',
		headers: {
			'Content-Type': 'application/json'
		},
		body: JSON.stringify({
			code
		}),
	});
	const token = await response.json();
	if (!response.ok || !token.access_token) {
		throw new Error(token.error || 'Discord authorization failed.');
	}
	const auth = await discordSdk.commands.authenticate({
		access_token: token.access_token,
	});
	if (!auth?.user?.id) {
		throw new Error('Discord did not return your user identity.');
	}
	if (!discordSdk.instanceId) {
		throw new Error('Discord did not provide an Activity instance ID. Close and relaunch the Activity.');
	}
	return {
		sdk: discordSdk,
		user: auth.user,
		accessToken: token.access_token,
		instanceId: discordSdk.instanceId,
	};
}

            <footer class="footer">

                <span>Â·</span>
                PXD Â· For the glory of the kingdom
            </footer>
        </main>

	wireCommon();

function wireCommon() {
	app.querySelectorAll('[data-game]').forEach(b => {
		b.addEventListener('click', () => send({
			type: 'select_game',
			gameId: b.dataset.game
		}));
	});
	app.querySelectorAll('[data-action]').forEach(b => {
		b.addEventListener('click', () => {
			let payload = {};
			if (b.dataset.payload) {
				try {
					payload = JSON.parse(b.dataset.payload);
				} catch (e) {
					console.error('Invalid button payload:', e);
					return;
				}
			}
			send({
				type: b.dataset.action,
				...payload
			});
		});
	});
	app.querySelector('#back-menu')?.addEventListener('click', () => send({
		type: 'menu'
	}));
	app.querySelector('#rematch')?.addEventListener('click', () => send({
		type: 'rematch'
	}));
}
/* =====================================================================
 * LEADERBOARD PANEL
 * ===================================================================== */
async function showLeaderboard() {
	const detail = document.getElementById('event-detail');
	if (!detail) return;
	detail.innerHTML = '<p>Loading leaderboardâ€¦</p>';
	try {
		const res = await fetch('/api/leaderboard', {
			headers: {
				'Content-Type': 'application/json'
			},
			method: 'POST',
			body: JSON.stringify({
				accessToken: session?.accessToken,
				guildId: session?.guildId
			}),
		});
		const data = await res.json();
		if (!data.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Failed to load leaderboard');
		const players = data.leaderboard || [];
		if (!players.length) {
			detail.innerHTML = '<p>No players have competed yet.</p>';
			return;
		}
		const medals = ['ðŸ¥‡', 'ðŸ¥ˆ', 'ðŸ¥‰'];
		detail.innerHTML = `
            <div class="leaderboard">
                <h3>ðŸ† Kingshot Leaderboard</h3>
                ${players.map((p, i) => `
                    <div class="lb-row">
                        <span class="lb-rank">${medals[i] || `${i + 1}.`}</span>
                        <span class="lb-name">${esc(p.username || 'Unknown')}</span>
                        <span class="lb-points">${p.points} pts</span>
                        <span class="lb-wins">${p.wins}W</span>
                    </div>
                `).join('')}
            </div>
        `;
	} catch (e) {
		detail.innerHTML = `<p class="error">${esc(e.message)}</p>`;
	}
}
/* =====================================================================
 * INVITE SYSTEM
 * ===================================================================== */
function showInvitePanel() {
	const detail = document.getElementById('event-detail');
	if (!detail) return;
	detail.innerHTML = `
        <div class="invite-panel">
            <h3>ðŸŽ® Invite to Play</h3>
            <label>Game</label>
            <select id="invite-game">
                ${GAMES.map(g => `<option value="${g.id}">${g.icon} ${g.name}</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
            <label>Target</label>
            <div class="invite-targets">
                <button class="primary-button" id="invite-player-btn">Invite a Player</button>
                <button class="ghost-button" id="invite-channel-btn">Post to Channel</button>
            </div>
            <div id="invite-detail"></div>
        </div>
    `;
	document.getElementById('invite-player-btn')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
		const game = document.getElementById('invite-game')?.value || 'ttt';
		const user = prompt('Enter the Discord User ID of the player you want to invite:');
		if (!user) return;
		send({
			type: 'event',
			sub: 'invite',
			game,
			target: user,
			targetType: 'player'
		});
		document.getElementById('invite-detail').innerHTML = `<p>âœ… Invite sent to player <code>${esc(user)}</code> for <strong>${esc(game)}</strong>!</p>`;
	});
	document.getElementById('invite-channel-btn')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
		const game = document.getElementById('invite-game')?.value || 'ttt';
		const channel = prompt('Enter the Discord Channel ID to post the invite:');
		if (!channel) return;
		send({
			type: 'event',
			sub: 'invite',
			game,
			target: channel,
			targetType: 'channel'
		});
		document.getElementById('invite-detail').innerHTML = `<p>âœ… Invite posted to channel <code>${esc(channel)}</code> for <strong>${esc(game)}</strong>!</p>`;
	});
}

	shell(`

                <button class="ghost-button" data-action="event:invite">ðŸŽ® Invite</button>

	});
	app.querySelector('[data-action="event:invite"]')?.addEventListener('click', () => {

function showEventForm(mode) {
	const detail = document.getElementById('event-detail');
	if (!detail) return;
	detail.innerHTML = `
        <div class="event-form">
            <h3>${mode === 'post' ? 'Post announcement' : 'Quick LIVE post'}</h3>
            <label>Event name</label>
            <input id="ev-event" type="text" placeholder="Bear Trap / Arena / KVKâ€¦" maxlength="60" />
            <label>Title</label>
            <input id="ev-title" type="text" placeholder="Weekend Bear Trap schedule" maxlength="200" />
            <label>Content</label>
            <textarea id="ev-content" rows="4" placeholder="Write the announcementâ€¦" maxlength="2000"></textarea>
            <label>Image URL (optional)</label>
            <input id="ev-image" type="text" placeholder="https://â€¦" maxlength="400" />
            <div class="action-row">
                <button class="primary-button" id="ev-submit">Post now</button>
                <button class="ghost-button" id="ev-cancel">Cancel</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    `;
	detail.querySelector('#ev-cancel')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
		detail.innerHTML = '';
	});
	detail.querySelector('#ev-submit')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
		const event = detail.querySelector('#ev-event').value.trim();
		const title = detail.querySelector('#ev-title').value.trim();
		const content = detail.querySelector('#ev-content').value.trim();
		const image = detail.querySelector('#ev-image').value.trim();
		if (!event || !title || !content) {
			alert('Please fill in event name, title, and content.');
			return;
		}
		send({
			type: 'event',
			sub: mode,
			event,
			title,
			content,
			image
		});
		detail.innerHTML = '<p>âœ… Submitted! Check Discord for the result.</p>';
	});
}
async function quickEvent() {
	const event = prompt('Event name (e.g. Bear Trap):', 'Bear Trap');
	if (!event) return;
	send({
		type: 'event',
		sub: 'quick',
		event
	});
}
async function listEvents() {
	const detail = document.getElementById('event-detail');
	if (!detail) return;
	detail.innerHTML = '<p>Loading scheduled eventsâ€¦</p>';
	try {
		const res = await fetch('/api/event/list', {
			headers: {
				'Content-Type': 'application/json'
			},
			method: 'POST',
			body: JSON.stringify({
				accessToken: session?.accessToken,
				guildId: session?.guildId
			}),
		});
		const data = await res.json();
		if (!data.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Failed to load events');
		const blogs = data.blogs || [];
		if (!blogs.length) {
			detail.innerHTML = '<p>No scheduled events yet.</p>';
			return;
		}
		detail.innerHTML = blogs.map(b => `
            <div class="event-list-item">
                <strong>${esc(b.title)}</strong> <code>${esc(b.id)}</code>
                <div>${esc(b.event)} Â· ${esc(b.channelId ? `<#${b.channelId}>` : 'no channel')} Â· ${b.enabled ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled'}</div>
                <div class="action-row">
                    <button class="ghost-button" data-event-delete="${esc(b.id)}">Delete</button>
                    <button class="ghost-button" data-event-toggle="${esc(b.id)}" data-enabled="${b.enabled}">${b.enabled ? 'Disable' : 'Enable'}</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        `).join('');
		detail.querySelectorAll('[data-event-delete]').forEach(b => {
			b.addEventListener('click', async () => {
				const id = b.dataset.eventDelete;
				await fetch('/api/event/delete', {
					method: 'POST',
					headers: {
						'Content-Type': 'application/json'
					},
					body: JSON.stringify({
						accessToken: session?.accessToken,
						guildId: session?.guildId,
						id
					})
				});
				listEvents();
			});
		});
		detail.querySelectorAll('[data-event-toggle]').forEach(b => {
			b.addEventListener('click', async () => {
				const id = b.dataset.eventToggle;
				const enabled = b.dataset.enabled !== 'true';
				await fetch('/api/event/toggle', {
					method: 'POST',
					headers: {
						'Content-Type': 'application/json'
					},
					body: JSON.stringify({
						accessToken: session?.accessToken,
						guildId: session?.guildId,
						id,
						enabled
					})
				});
				listEvents();
			});
		});
	} catch (e) {
		detail.innerHTML = `<p class="error">${esc(e.message)}</p>`;
	}
}
/* =====================================================================
 * GAME RENDERING
 * ===================================================================== */
function statusLine(text) {
	return `
        <div class="game-status">
            <span class="status-dot playing"></span>
            ${esc(text)}
        </div>
    `;
}

function playersPanel(g) {
	return `
        <div class="players">
            ${playerCard(state.players?.[0], 0, g)}
            <div class="versus">VS</div>
            ${playerCard(state.players?.[1], 1, g)}
        </div>
    `;
}

function endControls(g) {
	if (!['won', 'draw', 'finished'].includes(g.status)) return '';
	let result = g.winner === -1 ? "It's a draw!" : g.winner === me() ? 'You win!' : g.winner >= 0 ? `${esc(state.players[g.winner]?.username || 'Opponent')} wins!` : 'Round complete';
	if (g.id === 'guess' && g.answer !== undefined) result += ` Â· Number: ${g.answer}`;
	if (g.id === 'hangman' && g.revealed) result += ` Â· Word: ${g.revealed.join('')}`;
	return `
        <div class="end-panel">
            <strong>${result}</strong>
            <div class="action-row">
                <button class="primary-button" id="rematch">Play again</button>
                <button class="ghost-button" id="back-menu">All games</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    `;
}

function boardTtt(g) {
	const win = new Set(g.winningLine || []);
	return `
        <div class="ttt-board">
            ${g.board.map((v, i) => `
                <button
                    class="ttt-cell ${v === 'X' ? 'x' : v === 'O' ? 'o' : ''} ${win.has(i) ? 'winning' : ''}"
                    data-action="move"
                    data-payload='{"index":${i}}'
                    ${state.players.length < 2 || g.status !== 'playing' || g.turn !== me() || v ? 'disabled' : ''}
                >
                    ${v === 'X' ? 'âœ•' : v === 'O' ? 'â—‹' : ''}
                </button>
            `).join('')}
        </div>
    `;
}

function boardConnect(g) {
	const win = new Set((g.winningCells || []).map(([r, c]) => `${r}-${c}`));
	return `
        <div class="c4-board">
            ${Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, c) => ` < button class = "drop-col"
	data - action = "drop"
	data - payload = '{"col":${c}}'
	$ {
		state.players.length < 2 || g.status !== 'playing' || g.turn !== me() ? 'disabled' : ''
	} > â–¼ < /button>`).join('')}
	$ {
		g.board.map((row, r) => row.map((v, c) => `
                    <div class="c4-hole ${v === 'R' ? 'red' : v === 'Y' ? 'yellow' : ''} ${win.has(`${r}-${c}`) ? 'winning' : ''}"></div>
                `).join('')).join('')
	} < /div>
	`;
}

function renderGame() {
    const g = state.game;
    const info = GAMES.find(x => x.id === g.id) || GAMES[0];
    let body = '';
    let status = '';

    // Show what other players are doing
    const otherPlayers = (state.players || []).filter(p => p.id !== session?.user?.id);
    const otherPlaying = otherPlayers.map(p => {
        // We don't have per-player game state from the server in this view,
        // but we can show who's in the room
        return ` < span class = "player-tag" > $ {
		esc(p.username)
	} < /span>`;
}).join(' ');
if (['ttt', 'connect4', 'rps', 'reaction'].includes(g.id) && state.players.length < 2) {
	status = 'Waiting for your opponent to join';
} else if (g.id === 'ttt') {
	status = g.status === 'playing' ? (g.turn === me() ? 'Your turn' : "Opponent's turn") : g.status === 'draw' ? 'Board is full' : g.winner === me() ? 'You connected three!' : 'Opponent connected three.';
	body = boardTtt(g);
} else if (g.id === 'connect4') {
	status = g.status === 'playing' ? (g.turn === me() ? 'Your turn â€” choose a column' : "Opponent's turn") : g.status === 'draw' ? 'The board is full' : g.winner === me() ? 'Four in a row!' : 'Opponent connected four.';
	body = boardConnect(g);
} else if (g.id === 'rps') {
	status = g.status === 'finished' ? 'Match complete' : g.revealed ? 'Round revealed â€” continue when ready' : g.picks[me()] ? 'Choice locked â€” waiting for opponent' : 'Choose your move';
	body = `
            <div class="score-strip">
                <div><small>${esc(state.players[0]?.username || 'P1')}</small><b>${g.wins[0]}</b></div>
                <span>ROUND ${g.round} / 3</span>
                <div><small>${esc(state.players[1]?.username || 'P2')}</small><b>${g.wins[1]}</b></div>
            </div>
            ${g.revealed ? `
                <div class="rps-reveal">
                    <div>${g.picks[0] === 'rock' ? 'âœŠ' : g.picks[0] === 'paper' ? 'âœ‹' : 'âœŒï¸'}<small>${esc(state.players[0]?.username || 'P1')}</small></div>
                    <strong>VS</strong>
                    <div>${g.picks[1] === 'rock' ? 'âœŠ' : g.picks[1] === 'paper' ? 'âœ‹' : 'âœŒï¸'}<small>${esc(state.players[1]?.username || 'P2')}</small></div>
                </div>
                ${g.status === 'playing' ? '<button class="primary-button" data-action="next_round">Next round</button>' : ''}
            ` : `
                <div class="choice-row">
                    ${[['rock','âœŠ','Rock'],['paper','âœ‹','Paper'],['scissors','âœŒï¸','Scissors']].map(([v,e,n]) => `
                        <button class="choice-button" data-action="pick" data-payload='{"choice":"${v}"}' ${state.players.length < 2 || g.picks[me()] ? 'disabled' : ''}>
                            <span>${e}</span>${n}
                        </button>
                    `).join('')}
                </div>
            `}
        `;
} else if (g.id === 'reaction') {
	status = g.phase === 'waiting' ? 'Waiting for game start' : g.phase === 'countdown' ? 'Wait for the green signalâ€¦' : g.phase === 'go' ? 'GO! TAP NOW!' : g.phase === 'false-start' ? 'False start!' : `Reaction time: ${g.reactionMs || 'â€”'} ms`;
	body = `
            <div class="reaction-zone ${g.phase === 'go' ? 'go' : ''}">
                <div class="reaction-symbol">${g.phase === 'go' ? 'âš¡' : g.phase === 'countdown' ? 'â³' : g.phase === 'false-start' ? 'â›”' : 'âš¡'}</div>
                <h3>${g.phase === 'go' ? 'TAP!' : g.phase === 'countdown' ? 'WAITâ€¦' : g.phase === 'false-start' ? 'Too early!' : g.phase === 'done' ? 'Finished' : 'Get ready'}</h3>
                <button class="primary-button reaction-button" data-action="tap" ${g.phase !== 'go' ? 'disabled' : ''}>${g.phase === 'go' ? 'TAP NOW' : 'WAIT FOR GO'}</button>
            </div>
        `;
} else if (g.id === 'trivia' || g.id === 'daily') {
	const q = g.question;
	status = `Question ${g.index + 1} of 5 Â· ${g.answers.filter(Boolean).length}/${state.players.length} answered`;
	body = `
            <div class="score-strip">
                <div><small>${esc(state.players[0]?.username || 'P1')}</small><b>${g.scores[0]}</b></div>
                <span>${g.id === 'daily' ? 'DAILY CHALLENGE' : 'TRIVIA'}</span>
                <div><small>${esc(state.players[1]?.username || 'P2')}</small><b>${g.scores[1]}</b></div>
            </div>
            ${q ? `
                <div class="question-card">
                    <small>${esc(q.cat || 'KINGSHOT')}</small>
                    <h3>${esc(q.q)}</h3>
                    <div class="answer-grid">
                        ${q.options.map((o, i) => `
                            <button class="answer-button" data-action="answer" data-payload='{"index":${i}}' ${g.myAnswer ? 'disabled' : ''}>
                                ${String.fromCharCode(65 + i)}. ${esc(o)}
                            </button>
                        `).join('')}
                    </div>
                    ${g.myAnswer ? `<p class="answer-feedback">${g.myAnswer.correct ? 'âœ… Correct Â· +10 points' : 'Not quite Â· keep going'}</p>` : ''}
                    ${(state.players.length < 2 || g.answers.every(Boolean)) ? '<button class="primary-button" data-action="next_question">Next question â†’</button>' : ''}
                </div>
            ` : ''}
        `;
} else if (g.id === 'guess') {
	status = `Possible range ${g.low}â€“${g.high} Â· ${g.myGuesses?.length || 0}/${g.maxGuesses} guesses`;
	body = `
            <div class="score-strip">
                <div><small>${esc(state.players[0]?.username || 'P1')}</small><b>${g.guesses[0].length}/${g.maxGuesses}</b></div>
                <span>FIND THE NUMBER</span>
                <div><small>${esc(state.players[1]?.username || 'P2')}</small><b>${g.guesses[1].length}/${g.maxGuesses}</b></div>
            </div>
            <div class="guess-card">
                <h3>Guess a number between ${g.low} and ${g.high}</h3>
                <form id="guess-form" class="inline-form">
                    <input id="guess-value" type="number" min="${g.low}" max="${g.high}" placeholder="Your guess" required>
                    <button class="primary-button" type="submit">Guess</button>
                </form>
                <div class="guess-history">
                    ${g.guesses.map((arr, i) => `
                        <div>
                            <b>${esc(state.players[i]?.username || `P${i + 1}`)}</b>
                            <span>${arr.map(n => g.answer === n ? 'ðŸŽ¯' : `${n} ${g.answer !== undefined ? (n < g.answer ? 'â†‘' : 'â†“') : 'Â·'}`).join(' Â· ') || 'No guesses yet'}</span>
                        </div>
                    `).join('')}
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
} else if (g.id === 'hangman') {
	const guessed = new Set(g.guessed || []);
	status = `${g.lives} lives remaining Â· ${(g.guessed || []).length} letters guessed`;
	const revealedLetters = g.revealed || Array.from({
		length: g.wordLength || 8
	}, () => '_');
	body = `
            <div class="hangman-card">
                <div class="hang-word">${revealedLetters.map(x => `<span>${esc(x)}</span>`).join('')}</div>
                <div class="letter-grid">
                    ${'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ'.split('').map(l => `
                        <button class="letter-button" data-action="letter" data-payload='{"letter":"${l}"}' ${guessed.has(l) || g.status !== 'playing' ? 'disabled' : ''}>${l}</button>
                    `).join('')}
                </div>
                <button class="ghost-button" data-action="hint" ${g.lives <= 1 || g.status !== 'playing' ? 'disabled' : ''}>Reveal a letter Â· âˆ’1 life</button>
            </div>
        `;
} else if (g.id === 'scramble') {
	status = `Word ${g.index + 1} of 5 Â· ${g.roundDone ? 'Round solved' : 'Race to solve it first'}`;
	body = `
            <div class="score-strip">
                <div><small>${esc(state.players[0]?.username || 'P1')}</small><b>${g.scores[0]}</b></div>
                <span>WORD SCRAMBLE</span>
                <div><small>${esc(state.players[1]?.username || 'P2')}</small><b>${g.scores[1]}</b></div>
            </div>
            <div class="scramble-card">
                <div class="scrambled">${esc(g.scrambled || '')}</div>
                <form id="scramble-form" class="inline-form">
                    <input id="scramble-answer" type="text" maxlength="24" placeholder="Unscramble the word" required ${g.roundDone ? 'disabled' : ''}>
                    <button class="primary-button" type="submit" ${g.roundDone ? 'disabled' : ''}>Submit</button>
                </form>
                ${g.roundDone ? `<p class="answer-feedback">${esc(state.players[g.roundWinner]?.username || 'Player')} solved it! +10 points</p><button class="primary-button" data-action="next_word">Next word â†’</button>` : ''}
            </div>
        `;
}
const gameHtml = `
        <section class="intro game-intro">
            <button class="back-button" id="back-menu">â† All games</button>
            <div class="eyebrow">KINGSHOT ARCADE</div>
            <h2>${info.icon} ${info.name}</h2>
            <p>${info.desc}</p>
        </section>
        <section class="match-card">
            ${playersPanel(g)}
            ${statusLine(status)}
            ${body}
            ${endControls(g)}
            <div class="game-footer">${state.players.length < 2 ? 'Invite a friend to this Activity to play together.' : 'Moves are validated by the game server.'}</div>
        </section>
    `;
shell(gameHtml);
app.querySelector('#guess-form')?.addEventListener('submit', e => {
	e.preventDefault();
	send({
		type: 'guess',
		value: Number(app.querySelector('#guess-value').value)
	});
});
app.querySelector('#scramble-form')?.addEventListener('submit', e => {
	e.preventDefault();
	send({
		type: 'solve',
		answer: app.querySelector('#scramble-answer').value
	});
});
}

function render() {
	if (!state?.selectedGame || !state?.game) {
		renderMenu();
	} else {
		renderGame();
	}
}

function renderMenu() {
	const players = state?.players || [];
	const activeGames = getActivePlayers();
	shell(`
        <section class="intro">
            <div class="eyebrow">PXD ALLIANCE Â· THE ROYAL GAME HALL</div>
            <h2>Choose your <span>battle</span></h2>
            <p>Gather your allies, choose a game, and claim victory for PXD.</p>
        </section>
        <section class="match-card lobby">
            <div class="players">
                ${playerCard(players[0], 0, null)}
                <div class="versus">VS</div>
                ${playerCard(players[1], 1, null)}
            </div>
            <div class="lobby-note">${players.length < 2 ? 'Waiting for a second player â€” Share this Activity with a friend' : 'Both players are in. Choose any game below.'}</div>
        </section>
        ${activeGames.length > 0 ? `
        <section class="match-card" style="margin-top:18px;">
            <h3 style="margin:0 0 10px;color:#e0b65f;">ðŸŽ® Players Currently Playing</h3>
            <div class="active-games">
                ${activeGames.map(p => `
                    <div class="active-game-item">
                        <span class="player-tag">${esc(p.username)}</span>
                        <span>is playing</span>
                        <strong>${esc(p.game)}</strong>
                    </div>
                `).join('')}
            </div>
        </section>
        ` : ''}
        <section class="game-grid">
            ${GAMES.map(g => `
                <button class="game-tile" data-game="${g.id}">
                    <span class="game-icon">${g.icon}</span>
                    <span class="game-tag">${g.tag}</span>
                    <strong>${g.name}</strong>
                    <small>${g.desc}</small>
                    <span class="tile-arrow">Play â†’</span>
                </button>
            `).join('')}
        </section>
        <section class="match-card" style="margin-top:18px;">
            <div class="action-row" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;">
                <button class="primary-button" data-action="event:desk">ðŸ“ Event Desk</button>
                <button class="ghost-button" data-action="event:leaderboard">ðŸ† Leaderboard</button>
                <button class="ghost-button" data-action="event:invite">ðŸŽ® Invite</button>
            </div>
        </section>
    `);
	// Wire Event Desk button
	app.querySelector('[data-action="event:desk"]')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
		send({
			type: 'event',
			sub: 'desk'
		});
		renderEventDesk();
	});
	app.querySelector('[data-action="event:leaderboard"]')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
		send({
			type: 'event',
			sub: 'leaderboard'
		});
		showLeaderboard();
	});
	app.querySelector('[data-action="event:invite"]')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
		send({
			type: 'event',
			sub: 'invite'
		});
		showInvitePanel();
	});
}

function connectSocket() {
	const protocol = location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:';
	socket = new WebSocket(`${protocol}//${location.host}/ws`);
	socket.addEventListener('open', () => {
		socket.send(JSON.stringify({
			type: 'auth',
			accessToken: session.accessToken,
			instanceId: session.instanceId
		}));
	});
	socket.addEventListener('message', e => {
		let m;
		try {
			m = JSON.parse(e.data);
		} catch {
			return;
		}
		if (m.type === 'error') {
			errorText = m.message || 'Game server error';
			render();
		}
		if (m.type === 'ready') {
			connected = true;
			errorText = '';
			render();
		}
		if (m.type === 'state') {
			connected = true;
			errorText = '';
			state = m.state;
			render();
		}
	});
	socket.addEventListener('close', () => {
		connected = false;
		render();
	});
	socket.addEventListener('error', () => {
		errorText = 'Could not connect to the game server.';
		render();
	});
}
async function boot() {
	shell(`
        <section class="intro">
            <div class="eyebrow">PXD ALLIANCE Â· KINGDOM ARCADE</div>
            <h2>Opening the <span>Royal Game Hall</span>â€¦</h2>
            <p>Connecting to Discord Activity.</p>
        </section>
    `);
	try {
		session = await connectDiscord();
		connectSocket();
	} catch (e) {
		errorText = e?.message || 'Could not start Activity.';
		shell(`
            <section class="intro">
                <h2>Unable to connect</h2>
                <p>${esc(errorText)}</p>
            </section>
        `);
	}
}
window.addEventListener('beforeunload', () => socket?.close());
boot();

const {
	SlashCommandBuilder,
	EmbedBuilder,
	PermissionFlagsBits,
} = require("discord.js");
const {
	denyIfNotAdmin
} = require("../lib/admin");
const {
	loadAutoState,
	loadSeen
} = require("../lib/store");
const {
	fetchActiveCodes
} = require("../lib/codes");
const auto = require("../lib/auto");
const {
	makeEmbed,
	resultEmbed,
	errorEmbed
} = require("../lib/ui");
module.exports = {
	data: new SlashCommandBuilder().setName("auto").setDescription("Automatic gift-code discovery and redeem (owner only)").setDefaultMemberPermissions(PermissionFlagsBits.Administrator).addSubcommand(s => s.setName("start").setDescription("Start auto: find codes + redeem for all members")).addSubcommand(s => s.setName("stop").setDescription("Stop auto loop")).addSubcommand(s => s.setName("status").setDescription("Show auto status + active codes")).addSubcommand(s => s.setName("scan").setDescription("Run one scan now (find + redeem new codes)")),
	async execute(interaction) {
		if (await denyIfNotAdmin(interaction)) return;
		const sub = interaction.options.getSubcommand();
		if (sub === "status") {
			await interaction.deferReply({
				ephemeral: true
			});
			const state = loadAutoState();
			const seen = loadSeen();
			let activeList = "â€”";
			let activeCount = 0;
			try {
				const active = await fetchActiveCodes();
				activeCount = active.length;
				activeList = active.map(c => `\`${c.code}\`${c.expiresAt?` (exp ${c.expiresAt.slice(0,10)})`:""}`).join("\n") || "_none_"
			} catch (e) {
				activeList = `Error: ${e.message}`
			}
			const done = Object.keys(seen.codes || {});
			const asDiscordTime = value => value && !Number.isNaN(new Date(value).getTime()) ? `<t:${Math.floor(new Date(value).getTime()/1e3)}:R>` : "No record yet";
			const embed = makeEmbed({
				title: "ðŸ¤– Automatic redemption status",
				color: auto.isAutoRunning() ? "success" : "neutral",
				description: auto.isAutoRunning() ? "The scanner is running and will check for newly discovered gift codes." : "The scanner is currently stopped.",
				fields: [{
					name: "Scanner",
					value: auto.isAutoRunning() ? "ðŸŸ¢ Running" : "â¹ Stopped",
					inline: true
				}, {
					name: "Scan interval",
					value: `${(auto.getIntervalMs()/6e4).toFixed(0)} minutes`,
					inline: true
				}, {
					name: "Notify channel",
					value: process.env.NOTIFYCHANNELID ? `<#${process.env.NOTIFYCHANNELID}>` : "Not configured",
					inline: true
				}, {
					name: "Last scan",
					value: asDiscordTime(state.lastScanAt),
					inline: true
				}, {
					name: "Last redemption",
					value: asDiscordTime(state.lastRedeemAt),
					inline: true
				}, {
					name: "Codes processed",
					value: String(done.length),
					inline: true
				}, {
					name: `Active codes (${activeCount})`,
					value: activeList.slice(0, 1e3)
				}],
				footer: "Automatic redemption targets come from explicit registrations"
			});
			return interaction.editReply({
				embeds: [embed]
			})
		}
		if (await denyIfNotAdmin(interaction)) return;
		if (sub === "start") {
			if (!process.env.NOTIFYCHANNELID) {
				await interaction.reply({
					embeds: [makeEmbed({
						title: "ðŸ¤– Automatic redemption starting",
						description: "The scanner will run, but no channel notifications will be posted because `NOTIFYCHANNELID` is not configured.",
						color: "warning",
						fields: [{
							name: "Scan interval",
							value: `${(auto.getIntervalMs()/6e4).toFixed(0)} minutes`,
							inline: true
						}, {
							name: "Target source",
							value: "Registered players and alliance members",
							inline: true
						}]
					})],
					ephemeral: true
				})
			} else {
				await interaction.reply({
					embeds: [resultEmbed("Automatic redemption enabled", "The bot will scan for new gift codes and redeem them for registered targets.", true, [{
						name: "Scan interval",
						value: `${(auto.getIntervalMs()/6e4).toFixed(0)} minutes`,
						inline: true
					}, {
						name: "Results channel",
						value: `<#${process.env.NOTIFYCHANNELID}>`,
						inline: true
					}, {
						name: "Targets",
						value: "Registered solo players and alliance members",
						inline: false
					}])],
					ephemeral: true
				})
			}
			auto.startAuto(interaction.client);
			return
		}
		if (sub === "stop") {
			auto.stopAuto();
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [makeEmbed({
					title: "â¹ Automatic redemption stopped",
					description: "The scheduled scanner has been stopped. You can restart it with `/auto start`.",
					color: "neutral",
					fields: [{
						name: "Manual scan",
						value: "Use `/auto scan` to run one scan on demand.",
						inline: false
					}]
				})],
				ephemeral: true
			})
		}
		if (sub === "scan") {
			await interaction.deferReply({
				ephemeral: true
			});
			await interaction.editReply({
				embeds: [makeEmbed({
					title: "ðŸ”„ Scan in progress",
					description: "Checking active gift codes and processing any new codes for registered targets.",
					color: "info",
					footer: "This may take a while depending on target count and rate limits"
				})]
			});
			await auto.runOnce();
			return interaction.editReply({
				embeds: [resultEmbed("Scan completed", "The scan routine has finished. Check `/auto status` or the configured notification channel for code results.", true)]
			})
		}
	}
};



===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\commands\codes.js =====
const {
	SlashCommandBuilder
} = require('discord.js');
const {
	fetchActiveCodes
} = require('../lib/codes');
const {
	makeEmbed,
	errorEmbed
} = require('../lib/ui');
const {
	isBlacklisted,
	loadSeen,
	loadBlacklist,
	getPendingMembers,
	countFidStatuses,
} = require('../lib/store');
const {
	buildAllRedeemTargets
} = require('../lib/registrations');
module.exports = {
	data: new SlashCommandBuilder().setName('codes').setDescription('List active gift codes and bot progress'),
	async execute(interaction) {
		await interaction.deferReply({
			ephemeral: true
		});
		let active = [];
		try {
			active = await fetchActiveCodes();
		} catch (e) {
			return interaction.editReply({
				embeds: [errorEmbed('Could not load gift codes', e.message)]
			});
		}
		let targets = [];
		try {
			targets = await buildAllRedeemTargets();
		} catch {
			targets = [];
		}
		const lines = active.map((c) => {
			const bl = isBlacklisted(c.code);
			if (bl) return `ðŸ”´ \`${c.code}\` â€” **Blacklisted**`;
			const pending = getPendingMembers(c.code, targets);
			const counts = countFidStatuses(c.code);
			const exp = c.expiresAt ? ` Â· exp ${String(c.expiresAt).slice(0, 10)}` : '';
			if (!targets.length) {
				return `ðŸŸ¡ \`${c.code}\` â€” **Awaiting** (no registrations)${exp}`;
			}
			if (pending.length === 0 && counts.total > 0) {
				return `ðŸŸ¢ \`${c.code}\` â€” **Done** for current roster (${counts.success + counts.already} ok)${exp}`;
			}
			if (pending.length > 0 && counts.total > 0) {
				return `ðŸŸ¡ \`${c.code}\` â€” **${pending.length} pending** (new members / retries)${exp}`;
			}
			return `ðŸŸ¡ \`${c.code}\` â€” **Not yet** for current roster${exp}`;
		});
		const seen = loadSeen();
		const bl = loadBlacklist();
		return interaction.editReply({
			embeds: [
				makeEmbed({
					title: 'ðŸŽ Active gift codes',
					color: 'info',
					description: lines.join('\n') || '_No active codes_',
					fields: [{
						name: 'Active',
						value: String(active.length),
						inline: true
					}, {
						name: 'Tracked codes',
						value: String(Object.keys(seen.codes || {}).length),
						inline: true
					}, {
						name: 'Blacklisted',
						value: String(bl.length),
						inline: true
					}, {
						name: 'Current roster',
						value: String(targets.length),
						inline: true
					}, {
						name: 'Legend',
						value: 'ðŸŸ¢ Done Â· ðŸŸ¡ Pending / not yet Â· ðŸ”´ Blacklisted',
					}, ],
					footer: 'New alliance members stay pending until redeemed',
				}),
			],
		});
	},
};



===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\commands\event.js =====
const {
	SlashCommandBuilder,
	PermissionFlagsBits,
	ModalBuilder,
	TextInputBuilder,
	TextInputStyle,
	ActionRowBuilder,
	ChannelSelectMenuBuilder,
	ChannelType,
	ButtonBuilder,
	ButtonStyle,
	StringSelectMenuBuilder,
	EmbedBuilder,
} = require('discord.js');
const {
	isAdmin
} = require('../lib/admin');
const {
	makeEmbed,
	errorEmbed,
	resultEmbed
} = require('../lib/ui');
const {
	createBlog,
	listBlogs,
	getBlog,
	deleteBlog,
	setBlogEnabled
} = require('../lib/blogs');
const {
	blogEmbed
} = require('../lib/eventembed');
const {
	fieldGetter
} = require('../lib/discordutil');
const {
	parseTime,
	parseDetails,
	formatSchedule,
	getNextOccurrence
} = require('../lib/schedule');

function canManage(interaction) {
	if (isAdmin(interaction)) return true;
	return interaction.memberPermissions?.has(PermissionFlagsBits.Administrator) || false;
}
const drafts = new Map();

function input(id, label, {
	style = TextInputStyle.Short,
	required = true,
	placeholder,
	max
} = {}) {
	const t = new TextInputBuilder().setCustomId(id).setLabel(label).setStyle(style).setRequired(required);
	if (placeholder) t.setPlaceholder(placeholder);
	if (max) t.setMaxLength(max);
	return new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(t);
}

function panel(userId, d) {
	const info = makeEmbed({
		title: 'ðŸ“ Announcement draft',
		description: 'Pick a channel, then **post now** or **schedule** it.',
		color: 'info',
		fields: [{
			name: 'Channel',
			value: d.channelId ? `<#${d.channelId}>` : '_not selected_',
			inline: true
		}, {
			name: 'Mention',
			value: d.mention === '@everyone' ? '@everyone' : 'none',
			inline: true
		}, ],
	});
	const channelMenu = new ChannelSelectMenuBuilder().setCustomId(`event_channel:${userId}`).setPlaceholder(d.channelId ? 'Change channelâ€¦' : 'Post to which channel?').addChannelTypes(ChannelType.GuildText, ChannelType.GuildAnnouncement);
	const buttons = new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`event_mention:${userId}`).setLabel(d.mention === '@everyone' ? '@everyone: ON' : '@everyone: OFF').setStyle(d.mention === '@everyone' ? ButtonStyle.Danger : ButtonStyle.Secondary), new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`event_now:${userId}`).setLabel('Post now').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Success).setDisabled(!d.channelId), new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`event_sched:${userId}`).setLabel('Scheduleâ€¦').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Primary).setDisabled(!d.channelId), new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`event_cancel:${userId}`).setLabel('Cancel').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary));
	return {
		embeds: [info, blogEmbed(d)],
		components: [new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(channelMenu), buttons]
	};
}
module.exports = {
	data: new SlashCommandBuilder().setName('event').setDescription('Post or schedule event announcements (Admin only)').setDefaultMemberPermissions(PermissionFlagsBits.Administrator).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('post').setDescription('Write an announcement (post now or schedule)')).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('quick').setDescription('Quick LIVE announcement').addStringOption((o) => o.setName('event').setDescription('Event').setRequired(true).addChoices(...require('../lib/reminders').PRESET_EVENTS.filter((e) => e !== 'Custom').slice(0, 24).map((e) => ({
		name: e,
		value: e
	}))))).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('list').setDescription('List scheduled announcements')).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('delete').setDescription('Delete a scheduled announcement').addStringOption((o) => o.setName('id').setDescription('Announcement ID').setRequired(true))).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('toggle').setDescription('Enable or disable a scheduled announcement').addStringOption((o) => o.setName('id').setDescription('Announcement ID').setRequired(true)).addBooleanOption((o) => o.setName('enabled').setDescription('Enable?').setRequired(true))),
	async execute(interaction) {
		if (!canManage(interaction)) {
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [errorEmbed('Permission denied', 'Admin only.')],
				ephemeral: true
			});
		}
		const sub = interaction.options.getSubcommand();
		const userId = interaction.user.id;
		const guildId = interaction.guildId;
		if (sub === 'post') {
			const modal = new ModalBuilder().setCustomId(`event_modal:${userId}`).setTitle('Event Announcement');
			modal.addComponents(input('event', 'Event name', {
				placeholder: 'Bear Trap / Arena / KVKâ€¦',
				max: 60
			}), input('title', 'Title', {
				placeholder: 'Weekend Bear Trap schedule',
				max: 200
			}), input('content', 'Content', {
				style: TextInputStyle.Paragraph,
				placeholder: 'Write the announcementâ€¦',
				max: 2000
			}), input('image', 'Image URL (optional)', {
				required: false,
				placeholder: 'https://â€¦',
				max: 400
			}));
			return interaction.showModal(modal);
		}
		if (sub === 'quick') {
			const eventName = interaction.options.getString('event');
			const d = {
				event: eventName,
				title: `${eventName} is LIVE!`,
				content: `**${eventName}** has started!\n\nGet ready, gather your troops, and fight for glory.\nGood luck, Governors! ðŸ†`,
				image: '',
				color: 0xed4245,
				mention: '@everyone',
				channelId: null
			};
			drafts.set(userId, d);
			return interaction.reply({
				...panel(userId, d),
				ephemeral: true
			});
		}
		if (sub === 'list') {
			const list = listBlogs(guildId);
			if (!list.length) {
				return interaction.reply({
					embeds: [makeEmbed({
						title: 'ðŸ“‹ Scheduled announcements',
						description: '_None yet. Use `/event post` â†’ Schedule._',
						color: 'info'
					})],
					ephemeral: true
				});
			}
			const lines = list.map((b) => {
				const next = b.enabled ? getNextOccurrence(b) : null;
				const nextStr = next ? `<t:${Math.floor(next.getTime() / 1000)}:R>` : 'â€”';
				return `${b.enabled ? 'âœ…' : 'âŒ'} **${b.title}** \`${b.id}\`\n${b.event} Â· ${formatSchedule(b)} â†’ <#${b.channelId}> Â· Next ${nextStr}`;
			});
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [makeEmbed({
					title: `ðŸ“‹ Scheduled announcements (${list.length})`,
					description: lines.join('\n\n').slice(0, 4000),
					color: 'info'
				})],
				ephemeral: true
			});
		}
		if (sub === 'delete') {
			const id = interaction.options.getString('id').trim();
			const b = getBlog(id);
			const ok = b && b.guildId === guildId ? deleteBlog(id) : false;
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [resultEmbed(ok ? 'Deleted' : 'Not found', `\`${id}\``, !!ok)],
				ephemeral: true
			});
		}
		if (sub === 'toggle') {
			const id = interaction.options.getString('id').trim();
			const enabled = interaction.options.getBoolean('enabled');
			const b = getBlog(id);
			const ok = b && b.guildId === guildId ? setBlogEnabled(id, enabled) : false;
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [resultEmbed(ok ? (enabled ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled') : 'Not found', `\`${id}\``, !!ok)],
				ephemeral: true
			});
		}
	},
};
async function handleEventUI(interaction) {
	const userId = interaction.user.id;
	const id = interaction.customId || '';
	if (!id.startsWith('event:')) return false;
	if (!canManage(interaction)) {
		await interaction.reply({
			content: 'Admin only.',
			ephemeral: true
		}).catch(() => {});
		return true;
	}
	const owner = id.split(':')[1];
	if (owner && owner !== userId) {
		await interaction.reply({
			content: 'This draft is not yours.',
			ephemeral: true
		}).catch(() => {});
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('event_modal:')) {
		const get = fieldGetter(interaction);
		const d = {
			event: get('event').trim(),
			title: get('title').trim(),
			content: get('content').trim(),
			image: get('image').trim(),
			color: 0x5865f2,
			mention: '@everyone',
			channelId: null
		};
		drafts.set(userId, d);
		await interaction.reply({
			...panel(userId, d),
			ephemeral: true
		});
		return true;
	}
	const d = drafts.get(userId);
	if (!d) {
		if (interaction.isRepliable()) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: 'Draft expired. Run the command again.',
				ephemeral: true
			}).catch(() => {});
		}
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('event_channel:')) {
		d.channelId = interaction.values[0];
		await interaction.update(panel(userId, d));
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('event_mention:')) {
		d.mention = d.mention === '@everyone' ? '' : '@everyone';
		await interaction.update(panel(userId, d));
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('event_cancel:')) {
		drafts.delete(userId);
		await interaction.update({
			embeds: [makeEmbed({
				title: 'Cancelled',
				description: 'Draft discarded.',
				color: 'neutral'
			})],
			components: []
		});
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('event_now:')) {
		try {
			const ch = await interaction.client.channels.fetch(d.channelId);
			if (!ch?.isTextBased()) throw new Error('That channel is not a text channel.');
			await ch.send({
				content: d.mention || undefined,
				embeds: [blogEmbed(d)]
			});
			drafts.delete(userId);
			await interaction.update({
				embeds: [resultEmbed('Posted', `Sent to <#${d.channelId}>`, true)],
				components: []
			});
		} catch (e) {
			await interaction.reply({
				embeds: [errorEmbed('Failed', e.message)],
				ephemeral: true
			}).catch(() => {});
		}
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('event_sched:') || id.startsWith('event_back:')) {
		if (id.startsWith('event_back:')) {
			await interaction.update(panel(userId, d));
			return true;
		}
		const typeMenu = new StringSelectMenuBuilder().setCustomId(`event_stype:${userId}`).setPlaceholder('Schedule type').addOptions({
			label: 'One-time',
			value: 'once',
			description: 'Single date + time'
		}, {
			label: 'Daily',
			value: 'daily',
			description: 'Every day at a fixed UTC time'
		}, {
			label: 'Every X days',
			value: 'every_x_days',
			description: 'Repeats every N days'
		}, {
			label: 'Weekly',
			value: 'weekly',
			description: 'Specific weekdays'
		}, {
			label: 'Monthly',
			value: 'monthly',
			description: 'Day of month'
		});
		await interaction.update({
			embeds: [makeEmbed({
				title: 'ðŸ“… Schedule announcement',
				description: `**${d.title}** â†’ <#${d.channelId}>\n\nChoose when it should be posted (UTC).`,
				color: 'info'
			})],
			components: [new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(typeMenu), new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`event_back:${userId}`).setLabel('â† Back').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary))],
		});
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('event_stype:')) {
		d.stype = interaction.values[0];
		const modal = new ModalBuilder().setCustomId(`event_modal_time:${userId}`).setTitle('Post time (UTC)');
		const rows = [input('time', 'Post time UTC (HH:MM)', {
			placeholder: '20:00',
			max: 5
		})];
		if (d.stype === 'once') rows.push(input('date', 'Date YYYY-MM-DD', {
			placeholder: '2026-11-20',
			max: 10
		}));
		if (d.stype === 'every_x_days') rows.push(input('interval', 'Every how many days?', {
			placeholder: '2',
			max: 3
		}));
		if (d.stype === 'weekly') rows.push(input('weekdays', 'Weekdays 0=Sunâ€¦6=Sat (e.g. 1,3,5)', {
			placeholder: '1,3,5',
			max: 20
		}));
		if (d.stype === 'monthly') rows.push(input('monthday', 'Day of month (1-31)', {
			placeholder: '15',
			max: 2
		}));
		modal.addComponents(...rows);
		await interaction.showModal(modal);
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('event_modal_time:')) {
		const get = fieldGetter(interaction);
		const t = parseTime(get('time'));
		const details = t ? parseDetails(d.stype, get) : null;
		const err = !t ? 'Invalid time. Use `HH:MM` in UTC, e.g. `20:00`.' : details.error;
		const schedule = !err ? {
			type: d.stype,
			hour: t.hour,
			minute: t.minute,
			date: null,
			intervalDays: null,
			weekdays: null,
			monthDay: null,
			...details.data
		} : null;
		if (!err && d.stype === 'once' && !getNextOccurrence(schedule)) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: 'âš ï¸ That date/time is already in the past.',
				ephemeral: true
			});
			return true;
		}
		if (err) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: `âš ï¸ ${err}`,
				ephemeral: true
			});
			return true;
		}
		const blog = createBlog({
			guildId: interaction.guildId,
			event: d.event,
			title: d.title,
			content: d.content,
			image: d.image,
			color: d.color,
			mention: d.mention,
			channelId: d.channelId,
			createdBy: userId,
			...schedule
		});
		drafts.delete(userId);
		const next = getNextOccurrence(blog);
		await interaction.update({
			embeds: [resultEmbed('Announcement scheduled', `**${blog.title}**\n${formatSchedule(blog)} â†’ <#${blog.channelId}>\nNext: ${next ? `<t:${Math.floor(next.getTime() / 1000)}:F>` : 'â€”'}\nID: \`${blog.id}\``, true)],
			components: [],
		});
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}
module.exports.handleEventUI = handleEventUI;

function canManage(interaction) {
	if (isAdmin(interaction)) return true;
	return interaction.memberPermissions?.has(PermissionFlagsBits.Administrator) || false;

const drafts = new Map();

function input(id, label, {
	style = TextInputStyle.Short,
	required = true,
	placeholder,
	max
} = {}) {
	const t = new TextInputBuilder().setCustomId(id).setLabel(label).setStyle(style).setRequired(required);
	if (placeholder) t.setPlaceholder(placeholder);
	if (max) t.setMaxLength(max);
	return new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(t);
}

function panel(userId, d) {
	const info = makeEmbed({
		title: 'ðŸ“ Announcement draft',
		description: 'Pick a channel, then **post now** or **schedule** it.',
		color: 'info',
		fields: [{
			name: 'Channel',
			value: d.channelId ? `<#${d.channelId}>` : '_not selected_',
			inline: true
		}, {
			name: 'Mention',
			value: d.mention === '@everyone' ? '@everyone' : 'none',
			inline: true
		}, ],
	});
	const channelMenu = new ChannelSelectMenuBuilder().setCustomId(`eb_channel:${userId}`).setPlaceholder(d.channelId ? 'Change channelâ€¦' : 'Post to which channel?').addChannelTypes(ChannelType.GuildText, ChannelType.GuildAnnouncement);
	const buttons = new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`eb_mention:${userId}`).setLabel(d.mention === '@everyone' ? '@everyone: ON' : '@everyone: OFF').setStyle(d.mention === '@everyone' ? ButtonStyle.Danger : ButtonStyle.Secondary), new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`eb_now:${userId}`).setLabel('Post now').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Success).setDisabled(!d.channelId), new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`eb_sched:${userId}`).setLabel('Scheduleâ€¦').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Primary).setDisabled(!d.channelId), new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`eb_cancel:${userId}`).setLabel('Cancel').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary));
	return {
		embeds: [info, blogEmbed(d)],
		components: [new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(channelMenu), buttons]
	};
}
module.exports = {
	data: new SlashCommandBuilder().setName('eventblog').setDescription('Post or schedule event announcements (Admin only)').setDefaultMemberPermissions(PermissionFlagsBits.Administrator).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('post').setDescription('Write an announcement (post now or schedule)')).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('quick').setDescription('Quick LIVE announcement').addStringOption((o) => o.setName('event').setDescription('Event').setRequired(true).addChoices(...PRESET_EVENTS.filter((e) => e !== 'Custom').slice(0, 24).map((e) => ({
		name: e,
		value: e
	}))))).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('list').setDescription('List scheduled announcements')).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('delete').setDescription('Delete a scheduled announcement').addStringOption((o) => o.setName('id').setDescription('Announcement ID').setRequired(true))).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('toggle').setDescription('Enable or disable a scheduled announcement').addStringOption((o) => o.setName('id').setDescription('Announcement ID').setRequired(true)).addBooleanOption((o) => o.setName('enabled').setDescription('Enable?').setRequired(true))),
	async execute(interaction) {
		if (!canManage(interaction)) {
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [errorEmbed('Permission denied', 'Admin only.')],
				ephemeral: true
			});
		}
		const sub = interaction.options.getSubcommand();
		const userId = interaction.user.id;
		const guildId = interaction.guildId;
		if (sub === 'post') {
			const modal = new ModalBuilder().setCustomId(`eb_modal:${userId}`).setTitle('Event Announcement');
			modal.addComponents(input('event', 'Event name', {
				placeholder: 'Bear Trap / Arena / KVKâ€¦',
				max: 60
			}), input('title', 'Title', {
				placeholder: 'Weekend Bear Trap schedule',
				max: 200
			}), input('content', 'Content', {
				style: TextInputStyle.Paragraph,
				placeholder: 'Write the announcementâ€¦',
				max: 2000
			}), input('image', 'Image URL (optional)', {
				required: false,
				placeholder: 'https://â€¦',
				max: 400
			}));
			return interaction.showModal(modal);
		}
		if (sub === 'quick') {
			const eventName = interaction.options.getString('event');
			const d = {
				event: eventName,
				title: `${eventName} is LIVE!`,
				content: `**${eventName}** has started!\n\nGet ready, gather your troops, and fight for glory.\nGood luck, Governors! ðŸ†`,
				image: '',
				color: 0xed4245,
				mention: '@everyone',
				channelId: null
			};
			drafts.set(userId, d);
			return interaction.reply({
				...panel(userId, d),
				ephemeral: true
			});
		}
		if (sub === 'list') {
			const list = listBlogs(guildId);
			if (!list.length) {
				return interaction.reply({
					embeds: [makeEmbed({
						title: 'ðŸ“‹ Scheduled announcements',
						description: '_None yet. Use `/eventblog post` â†’ Schedule._',
						color: 'info'
					})],
					ephemeral: true
				});
			}
			const lines = list.map((b) => {
				const next = b.enabled ? getNextOccurrence(b) : null;
				const nextStr = next ? `<t:${Math.floor(next.getTime() / 1000)}:R>` : 'â€”';
				return `${b.enabled ? 'âœ…' : 'âŒ'} **${b.title}** \`${b.id}\`\n${b.event} Â· ${formatSchedule(b)} â†’ <#${b.channelId}> Â· Next ${nextStr}`;
			});
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [makeEmbed({
					title: `ðŸ“‹ Scheduled announcements (${list.length})`,
					description: lines.join('\n\n').slice(0, 4000),
					color: 'info'
				})],
				ephemeral: true
			});
		}
		if (sub === 'delete') {
			const id = interaction.options.getString('id').trim();
			const b = getBlog(id);
			const ok = b && b.guildId === guildId ? deleteBlog(id) : false;
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [resultEmbed(ok ? 'Deleted' : 'Not found', `\`${id}\``, !!ok)],
				ephemeral: true
			});
		}
		if (sub === 'toggle') {
			const id = interaction.options.getString('id').trim();
			const enabled = interaction.options.getBoolean('enabled');
			const b = getBlog(id);
			const ok = b && b.guildId === guildId ? setBlogEnabled(id, enabled) : false;
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [resultEmbed(ok ? (enabled ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled') : 'Not found', `\`${id}\``, !!ok)],
				ephemeral: true
			});
		}
	},
};
async function handleEventBlogUI(interaction) {
	const userId = interaction.user.id;
	const id = interaction.customId || '';
	if (!id.startsWith('eb_')) return false;
	if (!canManage(interaction)) {
		await interaction.reply({
			content: 'Admin only.',
			ephemeral: true
		}).catch(() => {});
		return true;
	}
	const owner = id.split(':')[1];
	if (owner && owner !== userId) {
		await interaction.reply({
			content: 'This draft is not yours.',
			ephemeral: true
		}).catch(() => {});
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('eb_modal:')) {
		const get = fieldGetter(interaction);
		const d = {
			event: get('event').trim(),
			title: get('title').trim(),
			content: get('content').trim(),
			image: get('image').trim(),
			color: 0x5865f2,
			mention: '@everyone',
			channelId: null
		};
		drafts.set(userId, d);
		await interaction.reply({
			...panel(userId, d),
			ephemeral: true
		});
		return true;
	}
	const d = drafts.get(userId);
	if (!d) {
		if (interaction.isRepliable()) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: 'Draft expired. Run the command again.',
				ephemeral: true
			}).catch(() => {});
		}
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('eb_channel:')) {
		d.channelId = interaction.values[0];
		await interaction.update(panel(userId, d));
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('eb_mention:')) {
		d.mention = d.mention === '@everyone' ? '' : '@everyone';
		await interaction.update(panel(userId, d));
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('eb_cancel:')) {
		drafts.delete(userId);
		await interaction.update({
			embeds: [makeEmbed({
				title: 'Cancelled',
				description: 'Draft discarded.',
				color: 'neutral'
			})],
			components: []
		});
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('eb_now:')) {
		try {
			const ch = await interaction.client.channels.fetch(d.channelId);
			if (!ch?.isTextBased()) throw new Error('That channel is not a text channel.');
			await ch.send({
				content: d.mention || undefined,
				embeds: [blogEmbed(d)]
			});
			drafts.delete(userId);
			await interaction.update({
				embeds: [resultEmbed('Posted', `Sent to <#${d.channelId}>`, true)],
				components: []
			});
		} catch (e) {
			await interaction.reply({
				embeds: [errorEmbed('Failed', e.message)],
				ephemeral: true
			}).catch(() => {});
		}
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('eb_sched:') || id.startsWith('eb_back:')) {
		if (id.startsWith('eb_back:')) {
			await interaction.update(panel(userId, d));
			return true;
		}
		const typeMenu = new StringSelectMenuBuilder().setCustomId(`eb_stype:${userId}`).setPlaceholder('Schedule type').addOptions({
			label: 'One-time',
			value: 'once',
			description: 'Single date + time'
		}, {
			label: 'Daily',
			value: 'daily',
			description: 'Every day at a fixed UTC time'
		}, {
			label: 'Every X days',
			value: 'every_x_days',
			description: 'Repeats every N days'
		}, {
			label: 'Weekly',
			value: 'weekly',
			description: 'Specific weekdays'
		}, {
			label: 'Monthly',
			value: 'monthly',
			description: 'Day of month'
		});
		await interaction.update({
			embeds: [makeEmbed({
				title: 'ðŸ“… Schedule announcement',
				description: `**${d.title}** â†’ <#${d.channelId}>\n\nChoose when it should be posted (UTC).`,
				color: 'info'
			})],
			components: [new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(typeMenu), new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`eb_back:${userId}`).setLabel('â† Back').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary))],
		});
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('eb_stype:')) {
		d.stype = interaction.values[0];
		const modal = new ModalBuilder().setCustomId(`eb_modal_time:${userId}`).setTitle('Post time (UTC)');
		const rows = [input('time', 'Post time UTC (HH:MM)', {
			placeholder: '20:00',
			max: 5
		})];
		if (d.stype === 'once') rows.push(input('date', 'Date YYYY-MM-DD', {
			placeholder: '2026-11-20',
			max: 10
		}));
		if (d.stype === 'every_x_days') rows.push(input('interval', 'Every how many days?', {
			placeholder: '2',
			max: 3
		}));
		if (d.stype === 'weekly') rows.push(input('weekdays', 'Weekdays 0=Sunâ€¦6=Sat (e.g. 1,3,5)', {
			placeholder: '1,3,5',
			max: 20
		}));
		if (d.stype === 'monthly') rows.push(input('monthday', 'Day of month (1-31)', {
			placeholder: '15',
			max: 2
		}));
		modal.addComponents(...rows);
		await interaction.showModal(modal);
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('eb_modal_time:')) {
		const get = fieldGetter(interaction);
		const t = parseTime(get('time'));
		const details = t ? parseDetails(d.stype, get) : null;
		const err = !t ? 'Invalid time. Use `HH:MM` in UTC, e.g. `20:00`.' : details.error;
		const schedule = !err ? {
			type: d.stype,
			hour: t.hour,
			minute: t.minute,
			date: null,
			intervalDays: null,
			weekdays: null,
			monthDay: null,
			...details.data
		} : null;
		if (!err && d.stype === 'once' && !getNextOccurrence(schedule)) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: 'âš ï¸ That date/time is already in the past.',
				ephemeral: true
			});
			return true;
		}
		if (err) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: `âš ï¸ ${err}`,
				ephemeral: true
			});
			return true;
		}
		const blog = createBlog({
			guildId: interaction.guildId,
			event: d.event,
			title: d.title,
			content: d.content,
			image: d.image,
			color: d.color,
			mention: d.mention,
			channelId: d.channelId,
			createdBy: userId,
			...schedule
		});
		drafts.delete(userId);
		const next = getNextOccurrence(blog);
		await interaction.update({
			embeds: [resultEmbed('Announcement scheduled', `**${blog.title}**\n${formatSchedule(blog)} â†’ <#${blog.channelId}>\nNext: ${next ? `<t:${Math.floor(next.getTime() / 1000)}:F>` : 'â€”'}\nID: \`${blog.id}\``, true)],
			components: [],
		});
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}
module.exports.handleEventBlogUI = handleEventBlogUI;

	SlashCommandBuilder,

} = require('discord.js');

	PRESET_EVENTS,
	createReminder,
	listReminders,
	deleteReminder,
	updateReminder,
	setEnabled,
	getNextOccurrence,
	getReminder
} = require('../lib/reminders');

function canManage(interaction) {
	if (isAdmin(interaction)) return true;
	return interaction.memberPermissions?.has(PermissionFlagsBits.Administrator) || false;
