const wizards = new Map();
module.exports = {
	data: new SlashCommandBuilder().setName('eventreminder').setDescription('Event reminder setup (Admin only)').setDefaultMemberPermissions(PermissionFlagsBits.Administrator).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('setup').setDescription('Interactive setup with dropdowns')).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('list').setDescription('List all reminders')).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('delete').setDescription('Delete a reminder by ID').addStringOption((o) => o.setName('id').setDescription('Reminder ID').setRequired(true))).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('edit').setDescription('Edit an existing reminder').addStringOption((o) => o.setName('id').setDescription('Reminder ID').setRequired(true))).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('toggle').setDescription('Enable or disable a reminder').addStringOption((o) => o.setName('id').setDescription('Reminder ID').setRequired(true)).addBooleanOption((o) => o.setName('enabled').setDescription('Enable?').setRequired(true))).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('test').setDescription('Send a test message for a reminder').addStringOption((o) => o.setName('id').setDescription('Reminder ID').setRequired(true))),
	async execute(interaction) {
		if (!canManage(interaction)) {
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [errorEmbed('Permission denied', 'Admin only.')],
				ephemeral: true
			});
		}
		const sub = interaction.options.getSubcommand();
		const guildId = interaction.guildId;
		const userId = interaction.user.id;
		if (sub === 'list') {
			const list = listReminders(guildId);
			if (!list.length) {
				return interaction.reply({
					embeds: [makeEmbed({
						title: 'ðŸ”” Reminders',
						description: '_None yet. Use `/eventreminder setup`_',
						color: 'info'
					})],
					ephemeral: true
				});
			}
			const lines = list.map((r) => {
				const next = getNextOccurrence(r);
				const nextStr = next ? `<t:${Math.floor(next.getTime() / 1000)}:R>` : 'â€”';
				const note = r.note ? ' Â· ðŸ“ note' : '';
				return `${r.enabled ? 'âœ…' : 'âŒ'} ${r.emoji || 'ðŸ””'} **${r.name}** \`${r.id}\`\n${formatSchedule(r)} Â· Next ${nextStr}${note}`;
			});
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [makeEmbed({
					title: `ðŸ”” Reminders (${list.length})`,
					description: lines.join('\n\n').slice(0, 4000),
					color: 'info'
				})],
				ephemeral: true
			});
		}
		if (sub === 'delete') {
			const id = interaction.options.getString('id').trim();
			const r = getReminder(id);
			const ok = r && r.guildId === guildId ? deleteReminder(id) : false;
			if (ok) {
				for (const s of r.sent || []) await deleteMessage(interaction.client, s.channelId, s.messageId);
			}
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [resultEmbed(ok ? 'Deleted' : 'Not found', `\`${id}\``, !!ok)],
				ephemeral: true
			});
		}
		if (sub === 'edit') {
			const id = interaction.options.getString('id').trim();
			const r = getReminder(id);
			if (!r || r.guildId !== guildId) {
				return interaction.reply({
					embeds: [errorEmbed('Not found', `No reminder found with ID \`${id}\`.`)],
					ephemeral: true
				});
			}
			const modal = new ModalBuilder().setCustomId(`er_edit_modal:${userId}:${id}`).setTitle('Edit event reminder');
			modal.addComponents(input('name', 'Event name', {
				value: r.name || '',
				max: 60
			}), input('time', 'Event time UTC (HH:MM)', {
				value: `${pad(r.hour)}:${pad(r.minute)}`,
				max: 5
			}), input('offsets', 'Minutes before (e.g. 60,10)', {
				value: (r.offsets || [60, 10]).join(','),
				max: 30
			}), input('emoji', 'Event emoji', {
				value: r.emoji || 'ðŸ””',
				max: 100
			}), input('note', 'Reminder text / rules', {
				style: TextInputStyle.Paragraph,
				required: false,
				value: r.note || '',
				max: 700
			}));
			return interaction.showModal(modal);
		}
		if (sub === 'toggle') {
			const id = interaction.options.getString('id').trim();
			const enabled = interaction.options.getBoolean('enabled');
			const r = getReminder(id);
			const ok = r && r.guildId === guildId ? setEnabled(id, enabled) : false;
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [resultEmbed(ok ? (enabled ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled') : 'Not found', `\`${id}\``, !!ok)],
				ephemeral: true
			});
		}
		if (sub === 'test') {
			const id = interaction.options.getString('id').trim();
			const r = getReminder(id);
			if (!r || r.guildId !== guildId) {
				return interaction.reply({
					embeds: [errorEmbed('Not found', `\`${id}\``)],
					ephemeral: true
				});
			}
			const eventTime = getNextOccurrence(r) || new Date();
			try {
				const ch = await interaction.client.channels.fetch(r.channelId);
				if (ch?.isTextBased()) await ch.send({
					content: reminderMessage(r, eventTime, {
						test: true
					})
				});
				return interaction.reply({
					embeds: [resultEmbed('Test sent', `<#${r.channelId}>`, true)],
					ephemeral: true
				});
			} catch (e) {
				return interaction.reply({
					embeds: [errorEmbed('Failed', e.message)],
					ephemeral: true
				});
			}
		}
		if (sub === 'setup') {
			wizards.delete(userId);
			wizards.set(userId, {
				guildId,
				step: 'event'
			});
			const eventMenu = new StringSelectMenuBuilder().setCustomId(`er_event:${userId}`).setPlaceholder('Select event').addOptions(PRESET_EVENTS.slice(0, 24).map((e) => ({
				label: e,
				value: e
			})));
			await interaction.reply({
				embeds: [makeEmbed({
					title: 'ðŸ”” Event Reminder Setup',
					description: '**Step 1/4** â€” Choose the event',
					color: 'info'
				})],
				components: [new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(eventMenu)],
				ephemeral: true,
			});
		}
	},
};

function input(id, label, {
	style = TextInputStyle.Short,
	required = true,
	placeholder,
	value,
	max
} = {}) {
	const t = new TextInputBuilder().setCustomId(id).setLabel(label).setStyle(style).setRequired(required);
	if (placeholder) t.setPlaceholder(placeholder);
	if (value) t.setValue(value);
	if (max) t.setMaxLength(max);
	return new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(t);
}
async function handleEventReminderUI(interaction) {
	const userId = interaction.user.id;
	const id = interaction.customId || '';
	if (!id.startsWith('er_')) return false;
	if (!canManage(interaction)) {
		await interaction.reply({
			content: 'Admin only.',
			ephemeral: true
		}).catch(() => {});
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('er_edit_modal:')) {
		const [, ownerId, reminderId] = id.split(':');
		if (ownerId !== userId) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: 'This edit form is not yours.',
				ephemeral: true
			}).catch(() => {});
			return true;
		}
		const existing = getReminder(reminderId);
		if (!existing || existing.guildId !== interaction.guildId) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: 'That reminder no longer exists.',
				ephemeral: true
			}).catch(() => {});
			return true;
		}
		const get = fieldGetter(interaction);
		const name = get('name').trim();
		const time = parseTime(get('time'));
		const emoji = get('emoji').trim();
		if (!name || !time || !emoji) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: 'Please provide an event name, valid UTC time (HH:MM), and emoji.',
				ephemeral: true
			});
			return true;
		}
		const updated = updateReminder(reminderId, {
			name,
			hour: time.hour,
			minute: time.minute,
			offsets: parseOffsets(get('offsets')),
			emoji,
			note: get('note').trim()
		});
		await interaction.reply({
			embeds: [resultEmbed('Reminder updated', `**${updated.name}** ${updated.emoji}\n${formatSchedule(updated)}\nID: \`${updated.id}\``, true)],
			ephemeral: true
		});
		return true;
	}
	const parts = id.split(':');
	if (parts.length >= 2 && /^\d{15,}$/.test(parts[1]) && parts[1] !== userId) {
		await interaction.reply({
			content: 'This setup is not yours.',
			ephemeral: true
		}).catch(() => {});
		return true;
	}
	/* Step 1 */
	if (id.startsWith('er_event:')) {
		const eventName = interaction.values[0];
		const wiz = wizards.get(userId) || {
			guildId: interaction.guildId
		};
		wiz.event = eventName;
		wiz.step = 'type';
		wizards.set(userId, wiz);
		const typeMenu = new StringSelectMenuBuilder().setCustomId(`er_type:${userId}`).setPlaceholder('Schedule type').addOptions({
			label: 'Daily',
			value: 'daily',
			description: 'Every day at a fixed UTC time'
		}, {
			label: 'One-time',
			value: 'once',
			description: 'Single date + time'
		}, {
			label: 'Every X days',
			value: 'every_x_days',
			description: 'Repeats every N days'
		}, {
			label: 'Weekly',
			value: 'weekly',
			description: 'Specific weekdays'
		}, {
			label: 'Monthly',
			value: 'monthly',
			description: 'Day of month'
		});
		await interaction.update({
			embeds: [makeEmbed({
				title: 'ðŸ”” Event Reminder Setup',
				description: `Event: **${eventName}**${eventName === 'Custom' ? ' _(you\'ll name it in the next form)_' : ''}\n\n**Step 2/4** â€” Choose schedule type`,
				color: 'info'
			})],
			components: [new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(typeMenu)],
		});
		return true;
	}
	/* Step 2 */
	if (id.startsWith('er_type:')) {
		const type = interaction.values[0];
		const wiz = wizards.get(userId);
		if (!wiz) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: 'Setup expired. Run `/eventreminder setup` again.',
				ephemeral: true
			});
			return true;
		}
		wiz.type = type;
		wiz.step = 'time';
		wizards.set(userId, wiz);
		const modal = new ModalBuilder().setCustomId(`er_modal_time:${userId}`).setTitle('Reminder details (UTC)');
		const rows = [];
		if (wiz.event === 'Custom') rows.push(input('name', 'Event name', {
			placeholder: 'e.g. Guild Raid',
			max: 60
		}));
		rows.push(input('time', 'Event time UTC (HH:MM)', {
			placeholder: '20:00',
			max: 5
		}));
		if (type === 'once') rows.push(input('date', 'Date YYYY-MM-DD', {
			placeholder: '2026-11-20',
			max: 10
		}));
		if (type === 'every_x_days') rows.push(input('interval', 'Every how many days?', {
			placeholder: '2',
			max: 3
		}));
		if (type === 'weekly') rows.push(input('weekdays', 'Weekdays 0=Sunâ€¦6=Sat (e.g. 1,3,5)', {
			placeholder: '1,3,5',
			max: 20
		}));
		if (type === 'monthly') rows.push(input('monthday', 'Day of month (1-31)', {
			placeholder: '15',
			max: 2
		}));
		rows.push(input('offsets', 'Remind minutes before (e.g. 60,10)', {
			required: false,
			value: '60,10',
			max: 30
		}));
		rows.push(input('note', 'Reminder text / rules (optional)', {
			style: TextInputStyle.Paragraph,
			required: false,
			placeholder: 'This text is the reminder message â€” rules, rally timesâ€¦',
			max: 700
		}));
		modal.addComponents(...rows);
		await interaction.showModal(modal);
		return true;
	}
	/* Step 3 */
	if (id.startsWith('er_modal_time:')) {
		const wiz = wizards.get(userId);
		if (!wiz) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: 'Setup expired. Run `/eventreminder setup` again.',
				ephemeral: true
			});
			return true;
		}
		const get = fieldGetter(interaction);
		const t = parseTime(get('time'));
		if (!t) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: 'âš ï¸ Invalid time. Use `HH:MM` in UTC, e.g. `20:00`. Run `/eventreminder setup` again.',
				ephemeral: true
			});
			return true;
		}
		if (wiz.event === 'Custom') {
			const name = get('name').trim();
			if (!name) {
				await interaction.reply({
					content: 'âš ï¸ Please enter an event name. Run `/eventreminder setup` again.',
					ephemeral: true
				});
				return true;
			}
			wiz.name = name;
		} else {
			wiz.name = wiz.event;
		}
		const details = parseDetails(wiz.type, get);
		if (details.error) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: `âš ï¸ ${details.error} Run \`/eventreminder setup\` again.`,
				ephemeral: true
			});
			return true;
		}
		Object.assign(wiz, {
			hour: t.hour,
			minute: t.minute,
			date: null,
			intervalDays: null,
			weekdays: null,
			monthDay: null
		}, details.data);
		if (wiz.type === 'once' && !getNextOccurrence(wiz)) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: 'âš ï¸ That date/time is already in the past. Run `/eventreminder setup` again.',
				ephemeral: true
			});
			return true;
		}
		wiz.offsets = parseOffsets(get('offsets'));
		wiz.note = get('note').trim();
		wiz.step = 'channel';
		wizards.set(userId, wiz);
		const channelMenu = new ChannelSelectMenuBuilder().setCustomId(`er_channel:${userId}`).setPlaceholder('Select channel for reminders').addChannelTypes(ChannelType.GuildText, ChannelType.GuildAnnouncement).setMaxValues(1);
		await interaction.reply({
			embeds: [makeEmbed({
				title: 'ðŸ”” Event Reminder Setup',
				description: `Event: **${wiz.name}**\nSchedule: ${formatSchedule(wiz)}\n\n**Step 3/4** â€” Choose the channel`,
				color: 'info'
			})],
			components: [new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(channelMenu)],
			ephemeral: true,
		});
		return true;
	}
	/* Step 4 */
	if (id.startsWith('er_channel:')) {
		const wiz = wizards.get(userId);
		if (!wiz) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: 'Setup expired.',
				ephemeral: true
			});
			return true;
		}
		wiz.channelId = interaction.values[0];
		wiz.step = 'confirm';
		wizards.set(userId, wiz);
		const row = new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`er_confirm:${userId}`).setLabel('Create reminder').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Success), new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`er_cancel:${userId}`).setLabel('Cancel').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary));
		const previewTime = getNextOccurrence(wiz) || new Date();
		await interaction.update({
			embeds: [
				makeEmbed({
					title: 'ðŸ”” Confirm reminder',
					description: `**${wiz.name}**\nSchedule: ${formatSchedule(wiz)}\nChannel: <#${wiz.channelId}>\nRemind: ${wiz.offsets.map((o) => `${o}m`).join(', ')} before\nMention: @everyone\n\n**Preview** (shows only what you wrote):`,
					color: 'warning'
				}),
				makeEmbed({
					title: 'ðŸ“ Preview',
					description: reminderMessage(wiz, previewTime, {
						test: true
					}).replace('@everyone', '`@everyone`'),
					color: 'neutral'
				}),
			],
			components: [row],
		});
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('er_cancel:')) {
		wizards.delete(userId);
		await interaction.update({
			embeds: [makeEmbed({
				title: 'Cancelled',
				description: 'Reminder setup cancelled.',
				color: 'neutral'
			})],
			components: []
		});
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('er_confirm:')) {
		const wiz = wizards.get(userId);
		if (!wiz || !wiz.channelId) {
			await interaction.update({
				content: 'Setup expired.',
				embeds: [],
				components: []
			});
			return true;
		}
		const reminder = createReminder({
			guildId: wiz.guildId || interaction.guildId,
			name: wiz.name || wiz.event,
			note: wiz.note || '',
			type: wiz.type,
			hour: wiz.hour,
			minute: wiz.minute,
			date: wiz.date || null,
			intervalDays: wiz.intervalDays || null,
			weekdays: wiz.weekdays || null,
			monthDay: wiz.monthDay || null,
			channelId: wiz.channelId,
			mention: '@everyone',
			offsets: wiz.offsets || [60, 10],
			createdBy: userId,
		});
		wizards.delete(userId);
		const next = getNextOccurrence(reminder);
		const nextStr = next ? `<t:${Math.floor(next.getTime() / 1000)}:F>` : 'â€”';
		await interaction.update({
			embeds: [resultEmbed('Reminder created', `**${reminder.name}**\n${formatSchedule(reminder)}\nNext: ${nextStr}\nID: \`${reminder.id}\``, true)],
			components: []
		});
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}
module.exports.handleEventReminderUI = handleEventReminderUI;

const {
	SlashCommandBuilder,
	PermissionFlagsBits,
	ActionRowBuilder,
	ButtonBuilder,
	ButtonStyle,
	StringSelectMenuBuilder,
	ModalBuilder,
	TextInputBuilder,
	TextInputStyle,
	ChannelSelectMenuBuilder,
	ChannelType
} = require('discord.js');
const core = require('../games/core');
const stats = require('../lib/gamestats');
const {
	embed
} = core;
const {
	isAdmin
} = require('../lib/admin');
const {
	makeEmbed,
	resultEmbed
} = require('../lib/ui');
async function launchActivity(interaction) {
	const response = await fetch(`https://discord.com/api/v10/interactions/${interaction.id}/${interaction.token}/callback`, {
		method: 'POST',
		headers: {
			'Content-Type': 'application/json'
		},
		body: JSON.stringify({
			type: 12
		}),
	});
	if (!response.ok) {
		const detail = await response.text().catch(() => '');
		console.error('[games] Activity launch failed:', response.status, detail);
		throw new Error('Discord could not launch Kingshot Arcade. Make sure Activities are enabled for this application and try the App Launcher.');
	}
}
async function showStats(interaction) {
	const user = interaction.options.getUser('user') || interaction.user;
	const st = stats.getStats(user.id);
	const rate = st.played ? Math.round((st.wins / st.played) * 100) : 0;
	const per = Object.entries(st.games || {}).map(([k, g]) => `â€¢ **${k}** â€” ${g.wins}/${g.played} wins`).join('\n') || '_No games played yet_';
	return interaction.reply({
		embeds: [embed({
			title: `ðŸŽ® Game stats â€” ${user.username}`,
			color: 'info',
			fields: [{
				name: 'ðŸ† Points',
				value: `**${st.points}**`,
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: 'Played',
				value: String(st.played),
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: 'Win rate',
				value: `${rate}%`,
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: 'W / L / D',
				value: `${st.wins} / ${st.losses} / ${st.draws}`,
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: 'ðŸ… Win streak',
				value: `${st.streak} (best ${st.bestStreak})`,
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: 'ðŸ“… Daily streak',
				value: `${st.daily?.streak || 0} (best ${st.daily?.best || 0})`,
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: 'By game',
				value: per
			}, ],
		})],
		flags: core.EPH,
	});
}
async function showLeaderboard(interaction) {
	const top = stats.leaderboard(10);
	const medals = ['ðŸ¥‡', 'ðŸ¥ˆ', 'ðŸ¥‰'];
	const lines = top.map(
		(u, i) => `${medals[i] || `**${i + 1}.**`} <@${u.id}> â€” **${u.points}** pts Â· ${u.wins}W ${u.losses}L ${u.draws}D`);
	return interaction.reply({
		embeds: [embed({
			title: 'ðŸ† Games leaderboard',
			description: lines.join('\n') || '_No games played yet â€” be the first!_',
			color: 'gold',
			footer: 'Win = 5 pts Â· Draw = 2 Â· Loss = 1 Â· solo games score by performance',
		})],
		allowedMentions: {
			parse: []
		},
	});
}
/* Invite system */
const activeInvites = new Map();
async function handleInvite(interaction) {
	const userId = interaction.user.id;
	const id = interaction.customId || '';
	if (!id.startsWith('invite:')) return false;
	const parts = id.split(':');
	const action = parts[1]; // accept | decline | create
	const invId = parts[2];
	if (action === 'create') {
		// Open modal to create invite
		const modal = new ModalBuilder().setCustomId(`invite_modal:${userId}`).setTitle('Create Game Invite');
		modal.addComponents(new TextInputBuilder().setCustomId('invite_game').setLabel('Game (ttt, connect4, rps, reaction, trivia, guess, hangman, scramble, daily)').setStyle(TextInputStyle.Short).setRequired(true).setPlaceholder('ttt'), new TextInputBuilder().setCustomId('invite_channel').setLabel('Channel ID (optional â€” leave blank for current channel)').setStyle(TextInputStyle.Short).setRequired(false), new TextInputBuilder().setCustomId('invite_message').setLabel('Custom invite message (optional)').setStyle(TextInputStyle.Paragraph).setRequired(false).setPlaceholder('Come challenge me in Kingshot Arcade!'), );
		await interaction.showModal(modal);
		return true;
	}
	if (id.startsWith('invite_modal:')) {
		const get = (fid) => {
			try {
				return interaction.fields.getTextInputValue(fid) || '';
			} catch {
				return '';
			}
		};
		const game = get('invite_game').trim().toLowerCase();
		const channelId = get('invite_channel').trim() || interaction.channelId;
		const message = get('invite_message').trim() || `ðŸŽ® **${game.toUpperCase()}** invite! Come challenge me in Kingshot Arcade!`;
		const invId = `inv_${Date.now().toString(36)}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`;
		activeInvites.set(invId, {
			game,
			channelId,
			creator: userId,
			message,
			createdAt: Date.now()
		});
		// Send invite to target channel
		try {
			const ch = await interaction.client.channels.fetch(channelId);
			if (ch?.isTextBased()) {
				const row = new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`invite:accept:${invId}`).setLabel('ðŸŽ® Accept Invite').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Success), new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`invite:decline:${invId}`).setLabel('Decline').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary));
				await ch.send({
					content: message,
					components: [row]
				});
			}
		} catch (e) {
			console.error('Invite send failed:', e.message);
		}
		await interaction.reply({
			content: `âœ… Invite sent to <#${channelId}>! Code: \`${invId}\``,
			ephemeral: true
		});
		return true;
	}
	if (action === 'accept') {
		const inv = activeInvites.get(invId);
		if (!inv) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: 'âš ï¸ This invite has expired.',
				ephemeral: true
			});
			return true;
		}
		if (interaction.user.id === inv.creator) {
			await interaction.reply({
				content: 'âš ï¸ You cannot accept your own invite.',
				ephemeral: true
			});
			return true;
		}
		// Launch activity with game pre-selected
		await interaction.reply({
			content: `ðŸŽ® Accepting invite â€” launching **${inv.game}**! Opening Kingshot Arcadeâ€¦`,
			ephemeral: false
		});
		// Try to launch the activity
		try {
			await launchActivity(interaction);
		} catch (e) {
			console.error('Activity launch error:', e.message);
		}
		activeInvites.delete(invId);
		return true;
	}
	if (action === 'decline') {
		activeInvites.delete(invId);
		await interaction.reply({
			content: 'âŒ Invite declined.',
			ephemeral: true
		});
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}
/* Game activity tracker â€” shows who is playing what */
const playerGames = new Map(); // userId -> { game, instanceId, username }
function getActivePlayers() {
	const list = [];
	for (const [uid, info] of playerGames) {
		list.push({
			id: uid,
			...info
		});
	}
	return list;
}

function updatePlayerGame(userId, username, game, instanceId) {
	if (game && instanceId) {
		playerGames.set(userId, {
			game,
			instanceId,
			username
		});
	} else {
		playerGames.delete(userId);
	}
}
// One command opens the complete Activity
const builder = new SlashCommandBuilder().setName('games').setDescription('Open the PXD Kingshot Arcade game hub');
module.exports = {
	data: builder,
	async execute(interaction) {
		return launchActivity(interaction);
	},
	handleInvite,
	updatePlayerGame,
	getActivePlayers,
};

const {
	SlashCommandBuilder,
	PermissionFlagsBits
} = require('discord.js');
const {
	makeEmbed
} = require('../lib/ui');
module.exports = {
	data: new SlashCommandBuilder().setName('help').setDescription('How to use the bot'),
	async execute(interaction) {
		const isAdmin = interaction.memberPermissions?.has(PermissionFlagsBits.Administrator) || false;
		const fields = [{
			name: 'ðŸ“ Account',
			value: '`/register` Â· `/myregistrations` Â· `/roster` Â· `/show` Â· `/codes`',
			inline: false
		}, {
			name: 'ðŸŽ® Games',
			value: '`/games` â€” Opens Kingshot Arcade (all 9 games)\n`/stats` â€” Your personal game stats\n`/leaderboard` â€” Top 10 players',
			inline: false
		}, {
			name: 'ðŸ“… Events',
			value: '`/eventreminder setup` â€” Dropdowns + form (with rules/note field)\n`/eventreminder list|edit|delete|toggle|test`\n`/eventblog post` â€” Write it, then **Post now** or **Schedule**\n`/eventblog quick` â€” Quick LIVE post (now or scheduled)\n`/eventblog list|delete|toggle` â€” Scheduled announcements',
			inline: false
		}, {
			name: 'ðŸ”— Invite',
			value: 'Use the **ðŸŽ® Invite** button in the Activity lobby to invite specific players or post invites to channels.',
			inline: false
		}, {
			name: 'Other',
			value: '`/ping` Â· `/help`',
			inline: false
		}, ];
		if (isAdmin) {
			fields.push({
				name: 'âš™ï¸ Admin (hidden from members)',
				value: '`/auto` Â· `/stats` (bot-wide stats + user breakdown)',
			});
		}
		await interaction.reply({
			embeds: [makeEmbed({
				title: 'ðŸ° Kingshot Bot',

module.exports = {
	data: new SlashCommandBuilder().setName('myregistrations').setDescription('View or remove your registered players and alliances').addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('list').setDescription('Show your registrations (with remove buttons)')).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('remove-player').setDescription('Remove a solo player by ID').addStringOption((o) => o.setName('uid').setDescription('Player ID').setRequired(true))).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('remove-alliance').setDescription('Remove an alliance').addIntegerOption((o) => o.setName('server').setDescription('Kingdom ID').setRequired(true)).addStringOption((o) => o.setName('tag').setDescription('Alliance tag/slug').setRequired(true))),
	async execute(interaction) {
		const sub = interaction.options.getSubcommand();
		const userId = interaction.user.id;
		if (sub === 'list') {
			const u = listUser(userId);
			const players = (u.players || []).map((p) => `â€¢ **${p.name}** \`${p.fid}\` K${p.kid}`).join('\n') || '_None_';
			const alliances = (u.alliances || []).map((a) => `â€¢ **${a.name}** [${a.abbr}] K${a.kid}`).join('\n') || '_None_';
			const embed = makeEmbed({
				title: 'ðŸ“‹ Your registrations',
				color: 'info',
				description: 'AUTOREDEEM runs for these accounts and all alliance members (refreshed each cycle).',
				fields: [{
					name: `ðŸ‘¤ Players Â· ${(u.players || []).length}/${MAX_PLAYERS}`,
					value: players,
				}, {
					name: `ðŸ›¡ï¸ Alliances Â· ${(u.alliances || []).length}/${MAX_ALLIANCES}`,
					value: alliances,
				}, ],
				footer: 'Use the buttons below to remove an entry',
			});
			const rows = [];
			const buttons = [];
			for (const p of (u.players || []).slice(0, 10)) {
				buttons.push(new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`rmreg:player:${p.fid}:${userId}`).setLabel(`Remove ${(p.name || p.fid).slice(0, 60)}`.slice(0, 80)).setStyle(ButtonStyle.Danger));
			}
			for (const a of (u.alliances || []).slice(0, 5)) {
				buttons.push(new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`rmreg:ally:${a.kid}:${a.slug}:${userId}`).setLabel(`Remove ${a.abbr || a.slug} K${a.kid}`.slice(0, 80)).setStyle(ButtonStyle.Danger));
			}
			// Discord max 5 buttons per row, 5 rows
			for (let i = 0; i < buttons.length && rows.length < 5; i += 5) {
				rows.push(new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(buttons.slice(i, i + 5)));
			}
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [embed],
				components: rows,
				ephemeral: true,
			});
		}
		if (sub === 'remove-player') {
			const uid = interaction.options.getString('uid').trim();
			const ok = removePlayer(userId, uid);
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [resultEmbed(ok ? 'Player removed' : 'Not found', `ID: \`${uid}\``, ok)],
				ephemeral: true,
			});
		}
		if (sub === 'remove-alliance') {
			const server = interaction.options.getInteger('server');
			const tag = interaction.options.getString('tag').trim();
			const ok = removeAlliance(userId, server, tag);
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [
					resultEmbed(ok ? 'Alliance removed' : 'Not found', `**${tag}** Â· K${server}`, ok),
				],
				ephemeral: true,
			});
		}
	},
};

const {
	SlashCommandBuilder
} = require("discord.js");
const {
	makeEmbed
} = require("../lib/ui");
module.exports = {
	data: (new SlashCommandBuilder).setName("ping").setDescription("Bot latency"),
	async execute(interaction) {
		const sent = await interaction.reply({
			content: "ðŸ“ Measuring latencyâ€¦",
			fetchReply: true,
			ephemeral: true
		});
		const responseMs = Math.max(0, sent.createdTimestamp - interaction.createdTimestamp);
		const wsMs = Math.round(interaction.client.ws.ping);
		const quality = wsMs < 150 ? "Excellent" : wsMs < 300 ? "Good" : "High latency";
		const embed = makeEmbed({
			title: "ðŸ“ Bot connection status",
			color: wsMs < 300 ? "success" : "warning",
			fields: [{
				name: "Response time",
				value: `\`${responseMs} ms\``,
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: "Discord gateway",
				value: `\`${wsMs} ms\``,
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: "Connection quality",
				value: quality,
				inline: true
			}],
			footer: "Latency can fluctuate with network conditions"
		});
		return interaction.editReply({
			content: null,
			embeds: [embed]
		})
	}
};

const {
	SlashCommandBuilder,
	EmbedBuilder,
	ActionRowBuilder,
	StringSelectMenuBuilder,
	ButtonBuilder,
	ButtonStyle,
	ComponentType,
} = require('discord.js');
const {
	searchPlayers,
	searchAlliances
} = require('../lib/search');
const {
	addPlayer,
	addAlliance,
	listUser,
	MAX_PLAYERS,
	MAX_ALLIANCES,
} = require('../lib/registrations');
const {
	errorEmbed
} = require('../lib/ui');

function slotsFooter(userId) {
	const u = listUser(userId);
	const pLeft = Math.max(0, MAX_PLAYERS - (u.players || []).length);
	const aLeft = Math.max(0, MAX_ALLIANCES - (u.alliances || []).length);
	return `Slots left: ${pLeft}/${MAX_PLAYERS} players Â· ${aLeft}/${MAX_ALLIANCES} alliances Â· AUTOREDEEM ON`;
}

function playerEmbed(p, userId) {
	return new EmbedBuilder().setTitle('âœ… Player registered').setColor(0x57f287).setDescription('This account will receive gift codes automatically.').addFields({
		name: 'Name',
		value: p.name || 'â€”',
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: 'Player ID',
		value: `\`${p.fid}\``,
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: 'Kingdom',
		value: p.kid != null ? String(p.kid) : 'â€”',
		inline: true
	}).setFooter({
		text: slotsFooter(userId)
	}).setTimestamp();
}

function allianceEmbed(a, userId) {
	return new EmbedBuilder().setTitle('âœ… Alliance registered').setColor(0x57f287).setDescription('Gift codes will be redeemed for **all members**.\nNew members are picked up every auto cycle.').addFields({
		name: 'Alliance',
		value: `**${a.name}** [${a.abbr || a.slug}]`,
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: 'Kingdom',
		value: String(a.kid),
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: 'Members',
		value: a.members != null ? String(a.members) : 'â€”',
		inline: true
	}).setFooter({
		text: slotsFooter(userId)
	}).setTimestamp();
}
async function confirmPlayer(interaction, pick, userId) {
	const row = new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId('reg_confirm_yes').setLabel('Confirm').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Success), new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId('reg_confirm_no').setLabel('Cancel').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary));
	const preview = new EmbedBuilder().setTitle('Confirm player registration').setColor(0xfee75c).setDescription(`Register **${pick.name}** \`${pick.fid}\` (K${pick.kid ?? '?'})?`).setFooter({
		text: 'You have 60 seconds'
	});
	const msg = await interaction.editReply({
		embeds: [preview],
		components: [row],
		content: null
	});
	try {
		const btn = await msg.awaitMessageComponent({
			componentType: ComponentType.Button,
			time: 60_000,
			filter: (i) => i.user.id === userId,
		});
		if (btn.customId === 'reg_confirm_no') {
			return btn.update({
				content: 'Cancelled.',
				embeds: [],
				components: []
			});
		}
		const r = addPlayer(userId, pick);
		if (!r.ok) {
			return btn.update({
				embeds: [errorEmbed('Could not register', r.error)],
				components: []
			});
		}
		return btn.update({
			embeds: [playerEmbed(pick, userId)],
			components: []
		});
	} catch {
		return interaction.editReply({
			content: 'â° Timed out.',
			embeds: [],
			components: []
		});
	}
}
async function confirmAlliance(interaction, pick, userId) {
	const row = new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId('reg_confirm_yes').setLabel('Confirm').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Success), new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId('reg_confirm_no').setLabel('Cancel').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary));
	const preview = new EmbedBuilder().setTitle('Confirm alliance registration').setColor(0xfee75c).setDescription(`Register **${pick.name}** [${pick.abbr}] Â· K${pick.kid}` + (pick.members != null ? ` Â· ~${pick.members} members` : '') + '?').setFooter({
		text: 'Codes redeem for all members Â· 60s to confirm'
	});
	const msg = await interaction.editReply({
		embeds: [preview],
		components: [row],
		content: null
	});
	try {
		const btn = await msg.awaitMessageComponent({
			componentType: ComponentType.Button,
			time: 60_000,
			filter: (i) => i.user.id === userId,
		});
		if (btn.customId === 'reg_confirm_no') {
			return btn.update({
				content: 'Cancelled.',
				embeds: [],
				components: []
			});
		}
		const r = addAlliance(userId, pick);
		if (!r.ok) {
			return btn.update({
				embeds: [errorEmbed('Could not register', r.error)],
				components: []
			});
		}
		return btn.update({
			embeds: [allianceEmbed(pick, userId)],
			components: []
		});
	} catch {
		return interaction.editReply({
			content: 'â° Timed out.',
			embeds: [],
			components: []
		});
	}
}
async function handlePlayer(interaction) {
	const userId = interaction.user.id;
	const uid = interaction.options.getString('uid', true).trim();
	const server = interaction.options.getInteger('server');
	await interaction.deferReply({
		ephemeral: true
	});
	let results;
	try {
		results = await searchPlayers(uid);
	} catch (e) {
		return interaction.editReply({
			embeds: [errorEmbed('Search failed', e.message)]
		});
	}
	if (server != null) {
		results = results.filter((p) => p.kid == null || Number(p.kid) === Number(server));
	}
	if (!results.length) {
		return interaction.editReply({
			embeds: [
				errorEmbed('Not found', server != null ? `No player \`${uid}\` found on kingdom **${server}**.` : `No player found for \`${uid}\`.`),
			],
		});
	}
	if (results.length === 1) return confirmPlayer(interaction, results[0], userId);
	const menu = new StringSelectMenuBuilder().setCustomId(`reg_player:${userId}`).setPlaceholder('Select your player account').addOptions(results.slice(0, 25).map((p, i) => ({
		label: String(p.name || p.fid).slice(0, 100),
		description: `ID ${p.fid} Â· K${p.kid ?? '?'} Â· TC${p.tc ?? '?'}${p.alliance ? ` Â· ${p.alliance}` : ''}`.slice(0, 100),
		value: String(i),
	})));
	const msg = await interaction.editReply({
		content: `Found **${results.length}** matches. Select **your** account:`,
		components: [new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(menu)],
	});
	try {
		const sel = await msg.awaitMessageComponent({
			componentType: ComponentType.StringSelect,
			time: 60_000,
			filter: (i) => i.user.id === userId,
		});
		const pick = results[Number(sel.values[0])];
		await sel.update({
			content: 'Confirm belowâ€¦',
			components: []
		});
		return confirmPlayer(interaction, pick, userId);
	} catch {
		return interaction.editReply({
			content: 'â° Timed out.',
			components: []
		});
	}
}
async function handleAlliance(interaction) {
	const userId = interaction.user.id;
	const server = interaction.options.getInteger('server', true);
	// Option is registered as "name"
	const allianceQ = (interaction.options.getString('name') || '').trim();
	if (!allianceQ) {
		return interaction.reply({
			embeds: [errorEmbed('Alliance name required', 'Use `/register alliance` and fill **server** and **name**.')],
			ephemeral: true,
		});
	}
	await interaction.deferReply({
		ephemeral: true
	});
	let results;
	try {
		results = await searchAlliances(allianceQ, server);
	} catch (e) {
		return interaction.editReply({
			embeds: [errorEmbed('Search failed', e.message)]
		});
	}
	if (!results.length) {
		return interaction.editReply({
			embeds: [errorEmbed('Not found', `No alliance **${allianceQ}** on kingdom **${server}**.`)],
		});
	}
	if (results.length === 1) return confirmAlliance(interaction, results[0], userId);
	const menu = new StringSelectMenuBuilder().setCustomId(`reg_ally:${userId}`).setPlaceholder('Select the correct alliance').addOptions(results.slice(0, 25).map((a, i) => ({
		label: `${a.name} [${a.abbr}]`.slice(0, 100),
		description: `K${a.kid} Â· ${a.members ?? '?'} members Â· ${a.leader || 'â€”'}`.slice(0, 100),
		value: String(i),
	})));
	const msg = await interaction.editReply({
		content: `Found **${results.length}** alliances on K${server}. Select one:`,
		components: [new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(menu)],
	});
	try {
		const sel = await msg.awaitMessageComponent({
			componentType: ComponentType.StringSelect,
			time: 60_000,
			filter: (i) => i.user.id === userId,
		});
		const pick = results[Number(sel.values[0])];
		await sel.update({
			content: 'Confirm belowâ€¦',
			components: []
		});
		return confirmAlliance(interaction, pick, userId);
	} catch {
		return interaction.editReply({
			content: 'â° Timed out.',
			components: []
		});
	}
}
module.exports = {
	data: new SlashCommandBuilder().setName('register').setDescription('Register a player or alliance for auto gift-code redeem').addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('player').setDescription('Register a solo player account').addStringOption((o) => o.setName('uid').setDescription('Your Player ID').setRequired(true)).addIntegerOption((o) => o.setName('server').setDescription('Kingdom / server ID (optional)').setRequired(false))).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('alliance').setDescription('Register a whole alliance (all members)').addIntegerOption((o) => o.setName('server').setDescription('Kingdom / server ID').setRequired(true)).addStringOption((o) => o.setName('name').setDescription('Alliance tag or name').setRequired(true))),
	async execute(interaction) {
		const sub = interaction.options.getSubcommand(false);
		if (sub === 'player') return handlePlayer(interaction);
		if (sub === 'alliance') return handleAlliance(interaction);
		// Old command shape still cached by Discord â€” clear guidance
		return interaction.reply({
			embeds: [
				errorEmbed('Update required', 'Please use the new commands:\n' + 'â€¢ `/register player` â†’ options: **uid**, **server** (optional)\n' + 'â€¢ `/register alliance` â†’ options: **server**, **name**\n\n' + 'If you still see the old form, wait a minute or ask the owner to re-register slash commands.'),
			],
			ephemeral: true,
		});
	},
};



===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\commands\roster.js =====
const {
	SlashCommandBuilder
} = require('discord.js');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const {
	fetchAlliance
} = require('../lib/mightpulse');
const {
	searchPlayers
} = require('../lib/search');
const {
	listUser,
	MAX_PLAYERS,
	MAX_ALLIANCES
} = require('../lib/registrations');
const {
	makeEmbed,
	errorEmbed
} = require('../lib/ui');

function saveUserRegistrations(userId, userData) {
	const dataDir = process.env.DATADIR || path.join(__dirname, '..', 'data');
	const regPath = path.join(dataDir, 'registrations.json');
	if (!fs.existsSync(dataDir)) fs.mkdirSync(dataDir, {
		recursive: true
	});
	let all = {
		users: {}
	};
	if (fs.existsSync(regPath)) {
		all = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(regPath, 'utf8'));
	}
	all.users = all.users || {};
	all.users[String(userId)] = userData;
	fs.writeFileSync(regPath, JSON.stringify(all, null, 2));
}
module.exports = {
	data: new SlashCommandBuilder().setName('roster').setDescription('Refresh and view your registered players and alliances').addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('sync').setDescription('Refresh live data for your registered players and alliances')).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('list').setDescription('Show your registered players and alliances')).addSubcommand((s) => s.setName('count').setDescription('How many players / alliances you registered')),
	async execute(interaction) {
		const sub = interaction.options.getSubcommand();
		const userId = interaction.user.id;
		const u = listUser(userId);
		if (sub === 'count') {
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [
					makeEmbed({
						title: 'ðŸ“Š Your roster count',
						color: 'info',
						fields: [{
							name: 'Solo players',
							value: `${(u.players || []).length} / ${MAX_PLAYERS}`,
							inline: true,
						}, {
							name: 'Alliances',
							value: `${(u.alliances || []).length} / ${MAX_ALLIANCES}`,
							inline: true,
						}, ],
					}),
				],
				ephemeral: true,
			});
		}
		if (sub === 'list') {
			const players = (u.players || []).map((p) => `â€¢ **${p.name || 'Player'}** \`${p.fid}\` Â· K${p.kid ?? '?'}`).join('\n') || '_None â€” `/register player`_';
			const alliances = (u.alliances || []).map((a) => `â€¢ **${a.name}** [${a.abbr || a.slug}] Â· K${a.kid}`).join('\n') || '_None â€” `/register alliance`_';
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [
					makeEmbed({
						title: 'ðŸ“‹ Your registrations',
						color: 'info',
						fields: [{
							name: `Players (${(u.players || []).length}/${MAX_PLAYERS})`,
							value: players,
						}, {
							name: `Alliances (${(u.alliances || []).length}/${MAX_ALLIANCES})`,
							value: alliances,
						}, ],
						footer: 'Use /roster sync to refresh live alliance members',
					}),
				],
				ephemeral: true,
			});
		}
		// sync
		if (!(u.players || []).length && !(u.alliances || []).length) {
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [
					errorEmbed('Nothing registered', 'Register first:\n`/register player uid:â€¦`\n`/register alliance server:â€¦ name:â€¦`'),
				],
				ephemeral: true,
			});
		}
		await interaction.deferReply({
			ephemeral: true
		});
		const lines = [];
		let totalAllianceMembers = 0;
		const players = [...(u.players || [])];
		const alliances = [...(u.alliances || [])];
		for (let i = 0; i < players.length; i++) {
			const p = {
				...players[i]
			};
			try {
				const found = await searchPlayers(String(p.fid));
				const match = found.find((x) => String(x.fid) === String(p.fid)) || found[0];
				if (match) {
					p.name = match.name || p.name;
					if (match.kid != null) p.kid = match.kid;
					lines.push(`âœ… Player **${p.name}** \`${p.fid}\` Â· K${p.kid ?? '?'}`);
				} else {
					lines.push(`âš ï¸ Player \`${p.fid}\` â€” kept as registered`);
				}
			} catch (e) {
				lines.push(`âš ï¸ Player \`${p.fid}\` â€” ${e.message}`);
			}
			players[i] = p;
		}
		for (let i = 0; i < alliances.length; i++) {
			const a = {
				...alliances[i]
			};
			try {
				const {
					alliance,
					members
				} = await fetchAlliance(a.kid, a.slug);
				a.name = alliance.name || a.name;
				a.abbr = alliance.abbr || a.abbr;
				totalAllianceMembers += members.length;
				lines.push(`âœ… Alliance **${a.name}** [${a.abbr || a.slug}] Â· K${a.kid} Â· **${members.length}** members`);
			} catch (e) {
				lines.push(`âš ï¸ Alliance **${a.name || a.slug}** K${a.kid} â€” ${e.message}`);
			}
			alliances[i] = a;
		}
		saveUserRegistrations(userId, {
			...u,
			players,
			alliances,
			lastSyncedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
		});
		return interaction.editReply({
			embeds: [
				makeEmbed({
					title: 'ðŸ”„ Roster synced',
					color: 'success',
					description: lines.join('\n').slice(0, 4000) || '_Done_',
					fields: [{
						name: 'Solo players',
						value: String(players.length),
						inline: true
					}, {
						name: 'Alliances',
						value: String(alliances.length),
						inline: true
					}, {
						name: 'Alliance members (live)',
						value: String(totalAllianceMembers),
						inline: true
					}, ],
					footer: 'AUTOREDEEM uses updated data on the next cycle',
				}),
			],
		});
	},
};



===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\commands\show.js =====
const {
	SlashCommandBuilder,
	EmbedBuilder,
	ActionRowBuilder,
	StringSelectMenuBuilder,
	ButtonBuilder,
	ButtonStyle,
	ComponentType,
} = require('discord.js');
const {
	listUser
} = require('../lib/registrations');
const {
	fetchAlliance
} = require('../lib/mightpulse');
const {
	errorEmbed,
	makeEmbed
} = require('../lib/ui');
const MEMBERS_PER_EMBED = 25;
const MAX_MEMBERS = 100;

function memberLines(members, startIndex) {
	if (!members.length) return 'No members were returned for this alliance.';
	return members.map((m, i) => {
		const n = startIndex + i + 1;
		return `${n}. **${m.name || m.fid}** â€” \`${m.fid}\` Â· K${m.kid ?? '?'}${
        m.tc != null ? ` Â· TC${m.tc}` : ''
      }`;
	}).join('\n');
}

function statsEmbed(alliance, members) {
	const knownTc = members.filter((m) => m.tc != null);
	const averageTc = knownTc.length ? (knownTc.reduce((sum, m) => sum + Number(m.tc), 0) / knownTc.length).toFixed(1) : 'â€”';
	return new EmbedBuilder().setTitle(`ðŸ“Š ${alliance.name || alliance.slug} â€” summary`).setColor(0x57f287).addFields({
		name: 'Total members',
		value: String(members.length),
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: 'TC available',
		value: `${knownTc.length}/${members.length}`,
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: 'Average TC',
		value: averageTc,
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: 'Kingdom',
		value: `K${alliance.kid}`,
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: 'Alliance tag',
		value: alliance.abbr || alliance.slug || 'â€”',
		inline: true
	}).setFooter({
		text: 'Summary shown after the full member list'
	}).setTimestamp();
}
/**
 * Member list embeds only (no stats mixed in).
 * Stats embed is appended only after the last member chunk.
 */
function buildRosterMessage(alliance, members, page, pageSize) {
	const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(members.length / pageSize));
	const pageIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(page, totalPages - 1));
	const start = pageIndex * pageSize;
	const pageMembers = members.slice(start, start + pageSize);
	const embeds = [];
	for (let offset = 0; offset < pageMembers.length; offset += MEMBERS_PER_EMBED) {
		const chunk = pageMembers.slice(offset, offset + MEMBERS_PER_EMBED);
		const abs = start + offset;
		const isFirstOverall = abs === 0;
		embeds.push(new EmbedBuilder().setTitle(isFirstOverall ? `ðŸ‘¥ ${alliance.name || alliance.slug} [${alliance.abbr || alliance.slug}]` : `ðŸ‘¥ ${alliance.name || alliance.slug} (continued)`).setDescription(memberLines(chunk, abs)).setColor(0x5865f2).setFooter({
			text: `Members ${abs + 1}â€“${abs + chunk.length} of ${members.length} Â· Page ${
            pageIndex + 1
          }/${totalPages}`,
		}));
	}
	// Stats ONLY after all members on the last page
	const isLastPage = pageIndex >= totalPages - 1;
	if (isLastPage) {
		embeds.push(statsEmbed(alliance, members));
	}
	if (!embeds.length) {
		embeds.push(new EmbedBuilder().setTitle(`ðŸ‘¥ ${alliance.name || alliance.slug}`).setDescription('No members found.').setColor(0x5865f2));
	}
	return {
		embeds,
		page: pageIndex,
		totalPages
	};
}

function pageButtons(userId, page, totalPages) {
	if (totalPages <= 1) return [];
	return [
		new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`show_prev:${userId}`).setLabel('Previous').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary).setDisabled(page === 0), new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`show_next:${userId}`).setLabel('Next').setStyle(ButtonStyle.Primary).setDisabled(page >= totalPages - 1)),
	];
}
async function displayRoster(interaction, userId, alliance, members) {
	const safe = (members || []).slice(0, MAX_MEMBERS);
	// One page of up to 50 list lines â†’ fewer split messages; stats only on last page
	const pageSize = 50;
	let page = 0;
	let view = buildRosterMessage(alliance, safe, page, pageSize);
	await interaction.editReply({
		content: null,
		embeds: view.embeds,
		components: pageButtons(userId, page, view.totalPages),
	});
	if (view.totalPages <= 1) return;
	const message = await interaction.fetchReply();
	while (true) {
		try {
			const click = await message.awaitMessageComponent({
				componentType: ComponentType.Button,
				time: 120_000,
				filter: (c) => c.user.id === userId && [`show_prev:${userId}`, `show_next:${userId}`].includes(c.customId),
			});
			if (click.customId === `show_prev:${userId}`) page = Math.max(0, page - 1);
			if (click.customId === `show_next:${userId}`) page = Math.min(view.totalPages - 1, page + 1);
			view = buildRosterMessage(alliance, safe, page, pageSize);
			await click.update({
				content: null,
				embeds: view.embeds,
				components: pageButtons(userId, page, view.totalPages),
			});
		} catch {
			await interaction.editReply({
				components: []
			}).catch(() => {});
			return;
		}
	}
}
async function loadAndDisplayRoster(interaction, userId, selected) {
	try {
		const result = await fetchAlliance(selected.kid, selected.slug);
		return displayRoster(interaction, userId, result.alliance || selected, result.members || []);
	} catch (error) {
		return interaction.editReply({
			content: null,
			embeds: [
				errorEmbed('Could not load alliance roster', `Alliance: **${selected.name || selected.slug}** Â· K${selected.kid}\n${error.message}`),
			],
			components: [],
		});
	}
}
module.exports = {
	data: new SlashCommandBuilder().setName('show').setDescription('Show members of one of your registered alliances'),
	async execute(interaction) {
		const userId = interaction.user.id;
		const u = listUser(userId);
		const alliances = u.alliances || [];
		if (!alliances.length) {
			return interaction.reply({
				embeds: [
					errorEmbed('No alliances registered', 'Use `/register alliance server:â€¦ name:â€¦` first.'),
				],
				ephemeral: true,
			});
		}
		await interaction.deferReply({
			ephemeral: true
		});
		if (alliances.length === 1) {
			return loadAndDisplayRoster(interaction, userId, alliances[0]);
		}
		const menu = new StringSelectMenuBuilder().setCustomId(`show_pick:${userId}`).setPlaceholder('Select an alliance').addOptions(alliances.slice(0, 25).map((a, i) => ({
			label: `${a.name || a.slug} [${a.abbr || a.slug}]`.slice(0, 100),
			description: `K${a.kid}`.slice(0, 100),
			value: String(i),
		})));
		const msg = await interaction.editReply({
			embeds: [
				makeEmbed({
					title: 'Select alliance',
					description: 'Choose which registered alliance to show.',
					color: 'info',
				}),
			],
			components: [new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(menu)],
		});
		try {
			const sel = await msg.awaitMessageComponent({
				componentType: ComponentType.StringSelect,
				time: 60_000,
				filter: (i) => i.user.id === userId,
			});
			const selected = alliances[Number(sel.values[0])];
			await sel.update({
				content: 'Loading rosterâ€¦',
				embeds: [],
				components: []
			});
			return loadAndDisplayRoster(interaction, userId, selected);
		} catch {
			return interaction.editReply({
				content: 'Timed out.',
				embeds: [],
				components: []
			});
		}
	},
};

module.exports = {
	data: new SlashCommandBuilder().setName('stats').setDescription('Bot stats and registered users (owner only)').setDefaultMemberPermissions(PermissionFlagsBits.Administrator),
	async execute(interaction) {
		if (await denyIfNotAdmin(interaction)) return;
		await interaction.deferReply({
			flags: MessageFlags.Ephemeral
		});
		const s = stats();
		const targets = await buildAllRedeemTargets();
		const st = loadAutoState();
		const seen = loadSeen();
		const users = listAllUsers();
		// Overview embed
		const overview = makeEmbed({
			title: 'ðŸ“Š Bot stats',
			color: 'info',
			fields: [{
				name: 'Discord users registered',
				value: String(s.users),
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: 'Solo players (total)',
				value: String(s.players),
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: 'Alliances (total)',
				value: String(s.alliances),
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: 'Unique redeem targets',
				value: String(targets.length),
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: 'Auto running',
				value: auto.isAutoRunning() ? 'Yes' : 'No',
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: 'Tracked codes',
				value: String(Object.keys(seen.codes || {}).length),
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: 'Last scan',
				value: st.lastScanAt ? st.lastScanAt.slice(0, 19).replace('T', ' ') : 'â€”',
				inline: true
			}, {
				name: 'Last redeem',
				value: st.lastRedeemAt ? st.lastRedeemAt.slice(0, 19).replace('T', ' ') : 'â€”',
				inline: true
			}, ],
			footer: 'Only you can see this Â· roster refreshes each auto cycle',
		});
		// User breakdown (ephemeral, owner only)
		const lines = [];
		for (const u of users.slice(0, 20)) {
			let mention = `\`${u.discordId}\``;
			try {
				const user = await interaction.client.users.fetch(u.discordId);
				mention = `**${user.tag}** (\`${u.discordId}\`)`;
			} catch {
				/* keep id */
			}
			const players = (u.players || []).map((p) => `${p.name || 'Player'} \`${p.fid}\` K${p.kid ?? '?'}`).join(', ') || '_none_';
			const alliances = (u.alliances || []).map((a) => `${a.name || a.slug} [${a.abbr || a.slug}] K${a.kid}`).join(', ') || '_none_';
			lines.push(`${mention}\nðŸ‘¤ ${players}\nðŸ›¡ï¸ ${alliances}`);
		}
		const userEmbed = new EmbedBuilder().setTitle('ðŸ‘¥ Registered users').setColor(0x5865f2).setDescription(lines.length ? lines.join('\n\n').slice(0, 4000) : '_No users have registered yet._').setFooter({
			text: users.length > 20 ? `Showing 20 of ${users.length} users` : `${users.length} user(s)`,
		}).setTimestamp();
		return interaction.editReply({
			embeds: [overview, userEmbed]
		});
	},
};

/**
 * Shared game plumbing: sessions, embed/button helpers.
 */
const crypto = require('crypto');
const {
	EmbedBuilder,
	ActionRowBuilder,
	ButtonBuilder,
	ButtonStyle,
	ModalBuilder,
	TextInputBuilder,
	TextInputStyle,
	MessageFlags,
} = require('discord.js');
const BRAND = 'PXD_BOT';
const EPH = MessageFlags.Ephemeral;
const COLORS = {
	info: 0x5865f2,
	success: 0x57f287,
	danger: 0xed4245,
	warn: 0xfee75c,
	gold: 0xf1c40f,
	neutral: 0x99aab5
};
/* ---------- sessions ---------- */
const sessions = new Map();

function createSession(type, data) {
	const id = crypto.randomBytes(4).toString('hex'); // 8 chars â€” keeps customIds short
	const s = {
		id,
		type,
		createdAt: Date.now(),
		timers: {},
		...data
	};
	sessions.set(id, s);
	return s;
}
const getSession = (id) => sessions.get(id) || null;

function clearTimers(s) {
	for (const t of Object.values(s.timers)) clearTimeout(t);
	s.timers = {};
}

function endSession(id) {
	const s = sessions.get(id);
	if (!s) return;
	clearTimers(s);
	sessions.delete(id);
}

function setTimer(s, name, ms, fn) {
	clearTimeout(s.timers[name]);
	s.timers[name] = setTimeout(async () => {
		try {
			await fn();
		} catch (e) {
			console.error(`[games] timer ${name} failed:`, e.message);
		}
	}, ms);
}
// sweep abandoned sessions
setInterval(() => {
	const cutoff = Date.now() - 60 * 60 * 1000;
	for (const [id, s] of sessions)
		if (s.createdAt < cutoff) endSession(id);
}, 10 * 60 * 1000).unref();
/* ---------- UI helpers ---------- */
function embed({
	title,
	description,
	color = 'info',
	fields,
	footer
} = {}) {
	const e = new EmbedBuilder().setColor(COLORS[color] ?? color);
	if (title) e.setTitle(title);
	if (description) e.setDescription(description);
	if (fields && fields.length) e.addFields(fields);
	e.setFooter({
		text: footer ? `${BRAND} Â· ${footer}` : `${BRAND} Â· Games`
	});
	return e;
}

function btn(id, label, style = ButtonStyle.Secondary, {
	emoji,
	disabled
} = {}) {
	const b = new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(id).setStyle(style);
	if (label) b.setLabel(label);
	if (emoji) b.setEmoji(emoji);
	if (disabled) b.setDisabled(true);
	return b;
}
const row = (...components) => new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(...components);

function modal(id, title, inputId, label, {
	placeholder,
	max,
	style = TextInputStyle.Short
} = {}) {
	const t = new TextInputBuilder().setCustomId(inputId).setLabel(label.slice(0, 45)).setStyle(style).setRequired(true);
	if (placeholder) t.setPlaceholder(placeholder);
	if (max) t.setMaxLength(max);
	return new ModalBuilder().setCustomId(id).setTitle(title.slice(0, 45)).addComponents(row(t));
}
const bar = (v, max, len = 10) => {
	const f = Math.max(0, Math.min(len, Math.round((v / max) * len)));
	return 'â–°'.repeat(f) + 'â–±'.repeat(len - f);
};
const hearts = (n, max) => 'â¤ï¸'.repeat(Math.max(0, n)) + 'ðŸ–¤'.repeat(Math.max(0, max - n));
const mention = (id) => `<@${id}>`;
const ts = (ms) => Math.floor(ms / 1000);

function shuffle(arr) {
	const a = [...arr];
	for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
		const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
		[a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];
	}
	return a;
}
const randInt = (min, max) => Math.floor(Math.random() * (max - min + 1)) + min;
const pick = (arr) => arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)];
/** reply (new ephemeral message) or update (edit the clicked message) */
const respond = (interaction, payload, update = false) => update ? interaction.update(payload) : interaction.reply({
	...payload,
	flags: EPH
});
const expired = (interaction) => interaction.reply({
	embeds: [embed({
		title: 'âŒ› Game expired',
		description: 'That game has ended. Start a new one with `/games`.',
		color: 'neutral'
	})],
	flags: EPH,
});
module.exports = {
	BRAND,
	EPH,
	COLORS,
	ButtonStyle,
	createSession,
	getSession,
	endSession,
	clearTimers,
	setTimer,
	embed,
	btn,
	row,
	modal,
	bar,
	hearts,
	mention,
	ts,
	shuffle,
	randInt,
	pick,
	respond,
	expired,
};

/** Stable short id for "already seen" tracking (a word is its own id). */
const idOf = (x) => typeof x === 'string' ? x : crypto.createHash('md5').update(x.q).digest('hex').slice(0, 8);

/**
 * Solo games (private / ephemeral): trivia, guess, hangman, scramble, daily.
 * customId format: g:<key>:<sessionId>:<action>[:arg]
 */
const crypto = require('crypto');
const {
	ButtonStyle,
	StringSelectMenuBuilder
} = require('discord.js');
const core = require('./core');
const data = require('./data');
const stats = require('../lib/gamestats');
const {
	embed,
	btn,
	row,
	bar,
	hearts,
	ts,
	respond,
	EPH
} = core;
/** Pick `count` items this player hasn't seen yet; when the pool runs dry, start a new cycle. */
function pickFresh(userId, kind, pool, count) {
	const seen = new Set(stats.getSeen(userId, kind));
	let chosen = core.shuffle(pool.filter((x) => !seen.has(data.idOf(x)))).slice(0, count);
	if (chosen.length < count) {
		stats.forgetSeen(userId, kind, new Set(pool.map(data.idOf)));
		const rest = core.shuffle(pool.filter((x) => !chosen.includes(x))).slice(0, count - chosen.length);
		chosen = chosen.concat(rest);
	}
	stats.markSeen(userId, kind, chosen.map(data.idOf));
	return chosen;
}
const notYours = (i) => i.reply({
	content: 'ðŸš« This is not your game.',
	flags: EPH
});
const again = (key, label = 'ðŸ” Play again') => row(btn(`g:${key}:0:again`, label, ButtonStyle.Primary));
/* =====================================================================
 * TRIVIA â€” 5 questions, multiple choice, timer, streak bonus
 * ===================================================================== */
const TRIVIA_ROUND = 5;
const TRIVIA_TIME = 20000;
const LETTERS = ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D'];

function triviaStart(interaction, update = false) {
	// 5 fresh Kingshot questions (mixed categories), never repeating until the pool is used up
	const picked = pickFresh(interaction.user.id, 'trivia', data.TRIVIA, TRIVIA_ROUND);
	const qs = picked.map((item) => {
		const opts = core.shuffle([item.a, ...item.w]);
		return {
			q: item.q,
			cat: item.cat,
			opts,
			correct: opts.indexOf(item.a)
		};
	});
	const s = core.createSession('trivia', {
		userId: interaction.user.id,
		qs,
		i: 0,
		score: 0,
		correct: 0,
		streak: 0,
		answered: null,
		deadline: Date.now() + TRIVIA_TIME,
	});
	return respond(interaction, triviaView(s), update);
}

function triviaView(s) {
	const q = s.qs[s.i];
	const a = s.answered;
	const lines = q.opts.map((o, i) => `**${LETTERS[i]}.** ${o}`).join('\n');
	let status;
	let color = 'info';
	if (!a) status = `â³ Time left: <t:${ts(s.deadline)}:R>`;
	else if (a.ok) {
		color = 'success';
		status = `âœ… **Correct!** +${a.gain} pts${s.streak >= 2 ? ` Â· ðŸ”¥ ${s.streak} streak` : ''}`;
	} else {
		color = 'danger';
		status = `${a.timeout ? "âŒ› **Time's up!**" : 'âŒ **Wrong!**'} Answer: **${q.opts[q.correct]}**`;
	}
	const buttons = q.opts.map((o, i) => btn(`g:trivia:${s.id}:ans:${i}`, LETTERS[i], a ? (i === q.correct ? ButtonStyle.Success : i === a.idx && !a.ok ? ButtonStyle.Danger : ButtonStyle.Secondary) : ButtonStyle.Primary, {
		disabled: !!a
	}));
	buttons.push(a ? btn(`g:trivia:${s.id}:next`, s.i === s.qs.length - 1 ? 'ðŸ Results' : 'Next â–¶', ButtonStyle.Primary) : btn(`g:trivia:${s.id}:quit`, 'âœ–', ButtonStyle.Secondary));
	return {
		embeds: [
			embed({
				title: `ðŸ§  Kingshot Â· ${q.cat} Â· Question ${s.i + 1}/${s.qs.length}`,
				description: `**${q.q}**\n\n${lines}\n\n${status}`,
				color,
				fields: [{
					name: 'Score',
					value: `**${s.score}**`,
					inline: true
				}, {
					name: 'Streak',
					value: `ðŸ”¥ ${s.streak}`,
					inline: true
				}, {
					name: 'Progress',
					value: bar(s.i + (a ? 1 : 0), s.qs.length, 10),
					inline: true
				}, ],
			}),
		],
		components: [row(...buttons)],
	};
}

function triviaFinish(s) {
	core.endSession(s.id);
	stats.record(s.userId, 'trivia', s.correct >= 3 ? 'win' : 'loss', s.score);
	const total = s.qs.length;
	const tier = s.correct === total ? 'ðŸ‘‘ Kingshot Legend' : s.correct >= 4 ? 'ðŸ¥‡ Veteran Governor' : s.correct >= 3 ? 'ðŸ¥ˆ Rising Lord' : s.correct >= 1 ? 'ðŸ¥‰ Recruit' : 'ðŸªµ Fresh Settler';
	return {
		embeds: [
			embed({
				title: 'ðŸ Trivia complete',
				description: `**${tier}**\n\nâœ… Correct: **${s.correct}/${total}**\nâ­ Points earned: **${s.score}**`,
				color: s.correct >= 3 ? 'success' : 'warn',
			}),
		],
		components: [again('trivia')],
	};
}
async function triviaAction(interaction, s, action, arg) {
	if (action === 'quit') return interaction.update(triviaFinish(s));
	if (action === 'next') {
		if (!s.answered) return interaction.deferUpdate();
		if (s.i >= s.qs.length - 1) return interaction.update(triviaFinish(s));
		s.i++;
		s.answered = null;
		s.deadline = Date.now() + TRIVIA_TIME;
		return interaction.update(triviaView(s));
	}
	if (action === 'ans') {
		if (s.answered) return interaction.deferUpdate();
		const idx = Number(arg);
		const q = s.qs[s.i];
		const late = Date.now() > s.deadline + 1500;
		const ok = !late && idx === q.correct;
		let gain = 0;
		if (ok) {
			gain = 10 + Math.min(s.streak, 5) * 2;
			s.streak++;
			s.correct++;
			s.score += gain;
		} else s.streak = 0;
		s.answered = {
			idx,
			ok,
			gain,
			timeout: late
		};
		return interaction.update(triviaView(s));
	}
}
/* =====================================================================
 * GUESS THE NUMBER â€” narrowing range, hint, hearts
 * ===================================================================== */
function guessStart(interaction, update = false, max = 100) {
	const maxTries = Math.ceil(Math.log2(max)) + 2;
	const s = core.createSession('guess', {
		userId: interaction.user.id,
		max,
		secret: core.randInt(1, max),
		tries: 0,
		maxTries,
		lo: 1,
		hi: max,
		log: [],
		hint: null,
		over: null,
	});
	s.max = max;
	return respond(interaction, guessView(s), update);
}

function rangeBar(lo, hi, max, len = 24) {
	let out = '';
	for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
		const a = 1 + Math.floor((i * max) / len);
		const b = Math.max(a, Math.floor(((i + 1) * max) / len));
		out += b >= lo && a <= hi ? 'â–ˆ' : 'â–‘';
	}
	return out;
}

function guessView(s) {
	const left = s.maxTries - s.tries;
	let title = 'ðŸŽ¯ Guess the Number';
	let color = 'info';
	let desc = `I'm thinking of a number between **1** and **${s.max}**.\n\n**Possible range:** ${s.lo} â€“ ${s.hi}\n\`${rangeBar(s.lo, s.hi, s.max)}\``;
	if (s.over === 'win') {
		title = 'ðŸŽ‰ You got it!';
		color = 'success';
		desc = `The number was **${s.secret}** â€” found in **${s.tries}** ${s.tries === 1 ? 'try' : 'tries'}!`;
	} else if (s.over === 'lose') {
		title = 'ðŸ’€ Out of tries';
		color = 'danger';
		desc = `The number was **${s.secret}**. Better luck next time!`;
	} else if (s.over === 'quit') {
		title = 'ðŸ³ï¸ Gave up';
		color = 'neutral';
		desc = `The number was **${s.secret}**.`;
	}
	const fields = [{
		name: 'Tries left',
		value: hearts(left, s.maxTries),
		inline: false
	}, ];
	if (s.log.length) fields.push({
		name: 'Your guesses',
		value: s.log.slice(-8).join('\n'),
		inline: false
	});
	if (s.hint) fields.push({
		name: 'ðŸ’¡ Hint',
		value: s.hint,
		inline: false
	});
	const components = s.over ? [row(btn(`g:guess:0:again:${s.max}`, 'ðŸ” Play again', ButtonStyle.Primary))] : [
		row(btn(`g:guess:${s.id}:ask`, 'ðŸŽ¯ Make a guess', ButtonStyle.Primary), btn(`g:guess:${s.id}:hint`, 'ðŸ’¡ Hint (âˆ’1 try)', ButtonStyle.Secondary, {
			disabled: !!s.hint || left <= 1
		}), btn(`g:guess:${s.id}:quit`, 'ðŸ³ï¸ Give up', ButtonStyle.Danger)),
	];
	return {
		embeds: [embed({
			title,
			description: desc,
			color,
			fields
		})],
		components
	};
}

function guessFinish(s, result) {
	s.over = result;
	core.endSession(s.id);
	if (result === 'win') stats.record(s.userId, 'guess', 'win', 5 + (s.maxTries - s.tries) * 2);
	else stats.record(s.userId, 'guess', 'loss', 0);
}
async function guessAction(interaction, s, action) {
	if (action === 'ask') {
		return interaction.showModal(core.modal(`g:guess:${s.id}:modal`, `Guess 1â€“${s.max}`, 'n', `Your guess (1-${s.max})`, {
			placeholder: 'e.g. 42',
			max: 5
		}));
	}
	if (action === 'hint') {
		s.hint = `The number is **${s.secret % 2 === 0 ? 'even' : 'odd'}**.`;
		s.tries++;
		return interaction.update(guessView(s));
	}
	if (action === 'quit') {
		guessFinish(s, 'quit');
		return interaction.update(guessView(s));
	}
	if (action === 'modal') {
		const n = parseInt(interaction.fields.getTextInputValue('n'), 10);
		if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 1 || n > s.max) {
			return interaction.reply({
				content: `Enter a whole number between 1 and ${s.max}.`,
				flags: EPH
			});
		}
		s.tries++;
		if (n === s.secret) {
			s.log.push(`**${n}** âžœ ðŸŽ¯ Correct!`);
			guessFinish(s, 'win');
			return interaction.update(guessView(s));
		}
		const diff = Math.abs(n - s.secret);
		const close = diff <= Math.max(2, Math.round(s.max * 0.05)) ? ' Â· ðŸ”¥ very close!' : diff <= Math.round(s.max * 0.15) ? ' Â· ðŸŒ¶ï¸ warm' : '';
		if (n < s.secret) {
			s.lo = Math.max(s.lo, n + 1);
			s.log.push(`**${n}** âžœ â¬†ï¸ Higher${close}`);
		} else {
			s.hi = Math.min(s.hi, n - 1);
			s.log.push(`**${n}** âžœ â¬‡ï¸ Lower${close}`);
		}
		if (s.tries >= s.maxTries) guessFinish(s, 'lose');
		return interaction.update(guessView(s));
	}
}
/* =====================================================================
 * HANGMAN â€” gallows art + Aâ€“M / Nâ€“Z letter menus
 * ===================================================================== */
const GALLOWS = [
	['  â”Œâ”€â”€â”€â”', '  â”‚', '  â”‚', '  â”‚', '  â”‚', 'â”€â”€â”´â”€â”€'],
	['  â”Œâ”€â”€â”€â”', '  â”‚   O', '  â”‚', '  â”‚', '  â”‚', 'â”€â”€â”´â”€â”€'],
	['  â”Œâ”€â”€â”€â”', '  â”‚   O', '  â”‚   â”‚', '  â”‚', '  â”‚', 'â”€â”€â”´â”€â”€'],
	['  â”Œâ”€â”€â”€â”', '  â”‚   O', '  â”‚  /â”‚', '  â”‚', '  â”‚', 'â”€â”€â”´â”€â”€'],
	['  â”Œâ”€â”€â”€â”', '  â”‚   O', '  â”‚  /â”‚\\', '  â”‚', '  â”‚', 'â”€â”€â”´â”€â”€'],
	['  â”Œâ”€â”€â”€â”', '  â”‚   O', '  â”‚  /â”‚\\', '  â”‚  /', '  â”‚', 'â”€â”€â”´â”€â”€'],
	['  â”Œâ”€â”€â”€â”', '  â”‚   O', '  â”‚  /â”‚\\', '  â”‚  / \\', '  â”‚', 'â”€â”€â”´â”€â”€'],
].map((l) => l.join('\n'));
const MAX_LIVES = 6;

function hangStart(interaction, update = false) {
	const s = core.createSession('hang', {
		userId: interaction.user.id,
		word: pickFresh(interaction.user.id, 'words', data.WORDS, 1)[0],
		guessed: [],
		lives: MAX_LIVES,
		over: null,
		hintUsed: false,
	});
	return respond(interaction, hangView(s), update);
}

function letterMenu(s, group, n) {
	const free = group.split('').filter((l) => !s.guessed.includes(l));
	const m = new StringSelectMenuBuilder().setCustomId(`g:hang:${s.id}:pick${n}`).setPlaceholder(`Letters ${group[0]}â€“${group[group.length - 1]}`);
	if (!free.length) m.setDisabled(true).addOptions({
		label: 'â€”',
		value: 'none'
	});
	else m.addOptions(free.map((l) => ({
		label: l,
		value: l
	})));
	return row(m);
}

function hangView(s) {
	const wrong = s.guessed.filter((l) => !s.word.includes(l));
	const display = s.word.split('').map((c) => (s.guessed.includes(c) || s.over ? c : '_')).join(' ');
	let title = 'ðŸª Hangman';
	let color = 'info';
	let status = `Kingshot term Â· **${s.word.length}** letters`;
	if (s.over === 'win') {
		title = 'ðŸŽ‰ You saved him!';
		color = 'success';
		status = `The word was **${s.word}**.`;
	}
	if (s.over === 'lose') {
		title = 'ðŸ’€ Game over';
		color = 'danger';
		status = `The word was **${s.word}**.`;
	}
	if (s.over === 'quit') {
		title = 'ðŸ³ï¸ Gave up';
		color = 'neutral';
		status = `The word was **${s.word}**.`;
	}
	const components = s.over ? [again('hang')] : [
		letterMenu(s, 'ABCDEFGHIJKLM', 1),
		letterMenu(s, 'NOPQRSTUVWXYZ', 2),
		row(btn(`g:hang:${s.id}:hint`, 'ðŸ’¡ Reveal a letter (âˆ’1 life)', ButtonStyle.Secondary, {
			disabled: s.hintUsed || s.lives <= 1
		}), btn(`g:hang:${s.id}:quit`, 'ðŸ³ï¸ Give up', ButtonStyle.Danger)),
	];
	return {
		embeds: [
			embed({
				title,
				description: `\`\`\`\n${GALLOWS[MAX_LIVES - s.lives]}\n\`\`\`\n# \`${display}\`\n${status}`,
				color,
				fields: [{
					name: 'Lives',
					value: hearts(s.lives, MAX_LIVES),
					inline: true
				}, {
					name: 'Wrong letters',
					value: wrong.length ? wrong.join(' ') : 'â€”',
					inline: true
				}, ],
			}),
		],
		components,
	};
}

function hangCheck(s) {
	if (s.word.split('').every((c) => s.guessed.includes(c))) {
		s.over = 'win';
		stats.record(s.userId, 'hangman', 'win', 5 + s.lives * 2);
	} else if (s.lives <= 0) {
		s.over = 'lose';
		stats.record(s.userId, 'hangman', 'loss', 0);
	}
	if (s.over) core.endSession(s.id);
}
async function hangAction(interaction, s, action) {
	if (action === 'quit') {
		s.over = 'quit';
		core.endSession(s.id);
		stats.record(s.userId, 'hangman', 'loss', 0);
		return interaction.update(hangView(s));
	}
	if (action === 'hint') {
		const hidden = [...new Set(s.word.split('').filter((c) => !s.guessed.includes(c)))];
		if (hidden.length) {
			s.guessed.push(core.pick(hidden));
			s.hintUsed = true;
			s.lives--;
		}
		hangCheck(s);
		return interaction.update(hangView(s));
	}
	if (action === 'pick1' || action === 'pick2') {
		const letter = interaction.values[0];
		if (!/^[A-Z]$/.test(letter) || s.guessed.includes(letter)) return interaction.update(hangView(s));
		s.guessed.push(letter);
		if (!s.word.includes(letter)) s.lives--;
		hangCheck(s);
		return interaction.update(hangView(s));
	}
}
/* =====================================================================
 * SCRAMBLE â€” rounds, hints, speed-based points
 * ===================================================================== */
function scrambleWord(word) {
	let s = word;
	for (let i = 0; i < 10 && s === word; i++) s = core.shuffle(word.split('')).join('');
	return s;
}

function scrambleNewWord(s) {
	s.word = pickFresh(s.userId, 'words', data.WORDS.filter((w) => w !== s.word), 1)[0];
	s.mixed = scrambleWord(s.word);
	s.hints = 0;
	s.attempts = 0;
	s.startedAt = Date.now();
	s.result = null;
}

function scrambleStart(interaction, update = false) {
	const s = core.createSession('scr', {
		userId: interaction.user.id,
		round: 1,
		score: 0,
		solved: 0,
		word: '',
		result: null
	});
	scrambleNewWord(s);
	return respond(interaction, scrambleView(s), update);
}

function scrambleView(s) {
	const spaced = s.mixed.split('').join(' ');
	const hintLine = s.hints ? `\nðŸ’¡ \`${s.word.slice(0, s.hints).split('').join(' ')} ${'_ '.repeat(s.word.length - s.hints).trim()}\`` : '';
	let status = `Attempts: **${s.attempts}** Â· Hints: **${s.hints}**`;
	let color = 'info';
	if (s.result) {
		color = s.result.ok ? 'success' : 'neutral';
		status = s.result.ok ? `âœ… **${s.word}** â€” +${s.result.gain} pts` : `â­ï¸ It was **${s.word}**`;
	}
	const components = s.result ? [row(btn(`g:scr:${s.id}:next`, 'Next word â–¶', ButtonStyle.Primary), btn(`g:scr:${s.id}:finish`, 'ðŸ Finish', ButtonStyle.Secondary))] : [
		row(btn(`g:scr:${s.id}:ask`, 'âœï¸ Answer', ButtonStyle.Primary), btn(`g:scr:${s.id}:hint`, 'ðŸ’¡ Hint', ButtonStyle.Secondary, {
			disabled: s.hints >= Math.min(3, s.word.length - 2)
		}), btn(`g:scr:${s.id}:skip`, 'â­ï¸ Skip', ButtonStyle.Secondary), btn(`g:scr:${s.id}:finish`, 'ðŸ Finish', ButtonStyle.Danger)),
	];
	return {
		embeds: [
			embed({
				title: `ðŸ”¤ Word Scramble Â· Round ${s.round}`,
				description: `Unscramble this word:\n# \`${spaced}\`${hintLine}\n${s.word.length} letters\n\n${status}`,
				color,
				fields: [{
					name: 'Score',
					value: `**${s.score}**`,
					inline: true
				}, {
					name: 'Solved',
					value: `**${s.solved}**`,
					inline: true
				}, ],
			}),
		],
		components,
	};
}

function scrambleFinish(s) {
	core.endSession(s.id);
	stats.record(s.userId, 'scramble', s.solved > 0 ? 'win' : 'loss', s.score);
	return {
		embeds: [embed({
			title: 'ðŸ Scramble finished',
			description: `Words solved: **${s.solved}**\nâ­ Points earned: **${s.score}**`,
			color: s.solved ? 'success' : 'warn'
		})],
		components: [again('scr')],
	};
}
async function scrambleAction(interaction, s, action) {
	if (action === 'finish') return interaction.update(scrambleFinish(s));
	if (action === 'ask') {
		return interaction.showModal(core.modal(`g:scr:${s.id}:modal`, 'Word Scramble', 'a', `Unscramble: ${s.mixed}`, {
			placeholder: 'Your answer',
			max: 30
		}));
	}
	if (action === 'hint') {
		s.hints++;
		return interaction.update(scrambleView(s));
	}
	if (action === 'skip') {
		s.result = {
			ok: false
		};
		return interaction.update(scrambleView(s));
	}
	if (action === 'next') {
		s.round++;
		scrambleNewWord(s);
		return interaction.update(scrambleView(s));
	}
	if (action === 'modal') {
		if (s.result) return interaction.deferUpdate();
		const ans = interaction.fields.getTextInputValue('a').toUpperCase().trim();
		s.attempts++;
		if (ans === s.word) {
			const elapsed = (Date.now() - s.startedAt) / 1000;
			const gain = Math.max(2, 10 - s.hints * 3 - Math.floor(elapsed / 15));
			s.score += gain;
			s.solved++;
			s.result = {
				ok: true,
				gain
			};
		}
		return interaction.update(scrambleView(s));
	}
}
/* =====================================================================
 * DAILY â€” same number for everyone each UTC day, 7 tries, persistent streak
 * ===================================================================== */
const DAILY_MAX = 100;
const DAILY_TRIES = 7;
const dailyNumber = (date) => (crypto.createHash('md5').update(`pxd-daily-${date}`).digest().readUInt32BE(0) % DAILY_MAX) + 1;
const nextResetTs = () => {
	const d = new Date();
	return ts(Date.UTC(d.getUTCFullYear(), d.getUTCMonth(), d.getUTCDate() + 1));
};

function temperature(diff) {
	if (diff <= 2) return 'ðŸ”¥ Burning';
	if (diff <= 5) return 'ðŸŒ¶ï¸ Hot';
	if (diff <= 10) return 'ðŸ˜Š Warm';
	if (diff <= 20) return 'â„ï¸ Cold';
	return 'ðŸ§Š Freezing';
}

function dailyDoneView(userId) {
	const d = stats.getDaily(userId);
	const r = d.result;
	return {
		embeds: [
			embed({
				title: 'ðŸ“… Daily Challenge â€” done!',
				description: (r?.solved ? `âœ… You solved today's number in **${r.tries}** ${r.tries === 1 ? 'try' : 'tries'}!` : 'âŒ You didn\'t crack today\'s number.') + `\n\nðŸ”¥ Streak: **${d.streak}** Â· ðŸ† Best: **${d.best}**\nâ³ New challenge <t:${nextResetTs()}:R>`,
				color: r?.solved ? 'success' : 'neutral',
			}),
		],
		components: [],
	};
}

function dailyStart(interaction) {
	const date = stats.todayStr();
	const d = stats.getDaily(interaction.user.id);
	if (d.result && d.result.date === date) return interaction.reply({
		...dailyDoneView(interaction.user.id),
		flags: EPH
	});
	const guesses = d.progress && d.progress.date === date ? d.progress.guesses : [];
	const s = core.createSession('daily', {
		userId: interaction.user.id,
		date,
		secret: dailyNumber(date),
		guesses,
		over: null
	});
	return interaction.reply({
		...dailyView(s),
		flags: EPH
	});
}

function dailyView(s) {
	const left = DAILY_TRIES - s.guesses.length;
	const log = s.guesses.map((n) => {
		if (n === s.secret) return `**${n}** âžœ ðŸŽ¯ Correct!`;
		return `**${n}** âžœ ${n < s.secret ? 'â¬†ï¸ Higher' : 'â¬‡ï¸ Lower'} Â· ${temperature(Math.abs(n - s.secret))}`;
	});
	const d = stats.getDaily(s.userId);
	let title = 'ðŸ“… Daily Challenge';
	let color = 'info';
	let desc = `Everyone gets the **same number** today (1â€“${DAILY_MAX}). **${DAILY_TRIES} tries.**\nðŸ”¥ Streak: **${d.streak}**\nâ³ Resets <t:${nextResetTs()}:R>`;
	if (s.over === 'win') {
		title = 'ðŸŽ‰ Daily solved!';
		color = 'success';
		desc = `Solved in **${s.guesses.length}** ${s.guesses.length === 1 ? 'try' : 'tries'}!\nðŸ”¥ Streak: **${d.streak}** Â· â­ +${s.points} pts\nâ³ New challenge <t:${nextResetTs()}:R>`;
	}
	if (s.over === 'lose') {
		title = 'ðŸ’€ Daily failed';
		color = 'danger';
		desc = `The number was **${s.secret}**.\nâ³ New challenge <t:${nextResetTs()}:R>`;
	}
	const fields = [{
		name: 'Tries left',
		value: hearts(left, DAILY_TRIES),
		inline: false
	}];
	if (log.length) fields.push({
		name: 'Your guesses',
		value: log.join('\n'),
		inline: false
	});
	return {
		embeds: [embed({
			title,
			description: desc,
			color,
			fields
		})],
		components: s.over ? [] : [row(btn(`g:daily:${s.id}:ask`, 'ðŸŽ¯ Make a guess', ButtonStyle.Primary))],
	};
}
async function dailyAction(interaction, s, action) {
	if (action === 'ask') {
		return interaction.showModal(core.modal(`g:daily:${s.id}:modal`, 'Daily Challenge', 'n', `Guess a number (1-${DAILY_MAX})`, {
			placeholder: 'e.g. 42',
			max: 3
		}));
	}
	if (action === 'modal') {
		const n = parseInt(interaction.fields.getTextInputValue('n'), 10);
		if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 1 || n > DAILY_MAX) {
			return interaction.reply({
				content: `Enter a whole number between 1 and ${DAILY_MAX}.`,
				flags: EPH
			});
		}
		if (s.guesses.includes(n)) return interaction.reply({
			content: 'You already tried that number.',
			flags: EPH
		});
		s.guesses.push(n);
		if (n === s.secret || s.guesses.length >= DAILY_TRIES) {
			const solved = n === s.secret;
			const prev = stats.getDaily(s.userId);
			const points = solved ? 15 - (s.guesses.length - 1) * 2 + Math.min(prev.streak, 7) : 0;
			s.points = points;
			s.over = solved ? 'win' : 'lose';
			stats.finishDaily(s.userId, s.date, {
				solved,
				tries: s.guesses.length,
				points
			});
			core.endSession(s.id);
		} else {
			stats.saveDailyProgress(s.userId, s.date, s.guesses);
		}
		return interaction.update(dailyView(s));
	}
}
/* =====================================================================
 * Router
 * ===================================================================== */
const STARTERS = {
	trivia: triviaStart,
	guess: guessStart,
	hang: hangStart,
	scr: scrambleStart,
	daily: (i) => dailyStart(i),
};
const ACTIONS = {
	trivia: triviaAction,
	guess: guessAction,
	hang: hangAction,
	scr: scrambleAction,
	daily: dailyAction
};
const KEYS = new Set(Object.keys(ACTIONS));
/** /games <sub> â†’ key */
const SUB_TO_KEY = {
	trivia: 'trivia',
	guess: 'guess',
	hangman: 'hang',
	scramble: 'scr',
	daily: 'daily'
};
async function handle(interaction, parts) {
	const [, key, gid, action] = parts;
	if (action === 'again' && gid === '0') return STARTERS[key](interaction, true, Number(parts[4]) || undefined);
	const s = core.getSession(gid);
	if (!s || s.type !== key) return core.expired(interaction);
	if (s.userId !== interaction.user.id) return notYours(interaction);
	return ACTIONS[key](interaction, s, action, parts[4]);
}
module.exports = {
	KEYS,
	SUB_TO_KEY,
	STARTERS,
	handle,
	// exported for tests
	_t: {
		triviaStart,
		triviaView,
		guessView,
		hangView,
		scrambleView,
		dailyView,
		dailyNumber,
		rangeBar
	},
};
