/**
 * 1v1 games: Tic-Tac-Toe, Connect Four, Rock-Paper-Scissors (best of 3), Reaction Duel (best of 3).
 * customId format: g:<key>:<sessionId>:<action>[:arg]
 */
const {
	ButtonStyle
} = require('discord.js');
const core = require('./core');
const stats = require('../lib/gamestats');
const {
	embed,
	btn,
	row,
	mention,
	ts,
	EPH
} = core;
const TURN_MS = 90 * 1000;
const INVITE_MS = 60 * 1000;
/* ---------- shared lifecycle ---------- */
const rematchBtn = (s) => btn(`g:${s.type}:${s.id}:rematch`, `ðŸ” Rematch (${s.rematch.size}/2)`, ButtonStyle.Primary);

function inviteView(s, g) {
	const [a, b] = s.players;
	return {
		content: mention(b),
		embeds: [
			embed({
				title: `${g.icon} ${g.label} â€” challenge!`,
				description: `${mention(a)} challenges ${mention(b)}!\n\n${g.rules}\n\nâ³ Invite expires <t:${ts(Date.now() + INVITE_MS)}:R>`,
				color: 'warn',
			}),
		],
		components: [
			row(btn(`g:${s.type}:${s.id}:accept`, 'Accept', ButtonStyle.Success, {
				emoji: 'âœ…'
			}), btn(`g:${s.type}:${s.id}:decline`, 'Decline', ButtonStyle.Danger, {
				emoji: 'âœ–ï¸'
			}), btn(`g:${s.type}:${s.id}:cancel`, 'Cancel', ButtonStyle.Secondary)),
		],
	};
}
async function challenge(interaction, key) {
	const g = GAMES[key];
	const opp = interaction.options.getUser('opponent', true);
	if (opp.bot || opp.id === interaction.user.id) {
		return interaction.reply({
			embeds: [embed({
				title: 'âŒ Invalid opponent',
				description: 'Pick a real member â€” not yourself or a bot.',
				color: 'danger'
			})],
			flags: EPH,
		});
	}
	const s = core.createSession(key, {
		players: [interaction.user.id, opp.id],
		phase: 'invite',
		rematch: new Set(),
		winner: null,
		reason: null
	});
	await interaction.reply(inviteView(s, g));
	s.message = await interaction.fetchReply();
	core.setTimer(s, 'invite', INVITE_MS, async () => {
		if (s.phase !== 'invite') return;
		core.endSession(s.id);
		await s.message.edit({
			content: null,
			embeds: [embed({
				title: `${g.icon} ${g.label}`,
				description: 'âŒ› Challenge expired â€” nobody answered.',
				color: 'neutral'
			})],
			components: []
		}).catch(() => {});
	});
}
/** Mark game as over, record stats, keep session briefly for rematch. */
function finish(s, {
	winner = null,
	reason = null
} = {}) {
	s.phase = 'over';
	s.winner = winner;
	s.reason = reason;
	core.clearTimers(s);
	const [a, b] = s.players;
	if (winner) {
		const loser = winner === a ? b : a;
		stats.record(winner, s.type, 'win', 5);
		stats.record(loser, s.type, 'loss', 1);
	} else {
		stats.record(a, s.type, 'draw', 2);
		stats.record(b, s.type, 'draw', 2);
	}
	core.setTimer(s, 'cleanup', 5 * 60 * 1000, () => core.endSession(s.id));
}

function armTurn(s, onTimeout) {
	core.setTimer(s, 'turn', TURN_MS, async () => {
		if (s.phase !== 'play') return;
		onTimeout();
		await s.message.edit(GAMES[s.type].view(s)).catch(() => {});
	});
}
const resultLine = (s) => {
	if (s.winner) return `ðŸ† ${mention(s.winner)} wins!${s.reason === 'timeout' ? ' _(opponent timed out)_' : s.reason === 'forfeit' ? ' _(opponent forfeited)_' : ''}`;
	return 'ðŸ¤ **It\'s a draw!**';
};
const other = (s, uid) => (uid === s.players[0] ? s.players[1] : s.players[0]);
/* =====================================================================
 * TIC-TAC-TOE
 * ===================================================================== */
const T_LINES = [
	[0, 1, 2],
	[3, 4, 5],
	[6, 7, 8],
	[0, 3, 6],
	[1, 4, 7],
	[2, 5, 8],
	[0, 4, 8],
	[2, 4, 6]
];
const tttWinner = (b) => {
	for (const l of T_LINES)
		if (b[l[0]] && b[l[0]] === b[l[1]] && b[l[0]] === b[l[2]]) return {
			mark: b[l[0]],
			line: l
		};
	return null;
};

function tttReset(s) {
	s.board = Array(9).fill(null);
	s.turn = 0;
	s.winLine = null;
	s.phase = 'play';
	s.winner = null;
	s.reason = null;
}

function tttBegin(s, interaction) {
	tttReset(s);
	armTurn(s, () => finish(s, {
		winner: other(s, s.players[s.turn]),
		reason: 'timeout'
	}));
	return interaction.update(tttView(s));
}

function tttView(s) {
	const [a, b] = s.players;
	const over = s.phase === 'over';
	const status = over ? resultLine(s) : `ðŸŽ¯ ${mention(s.players[s.turn])}'s turn (${s.turn === 0 ? 'âŒ' : 'â­•'})\nâ³ Move <t:${ts(Date.now() + TURN_MS)}:R>`;
	const rows = [];
	for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) {
		const comps = [];
		for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) {
			const i = r * 3 + c;
			const cell = s.board[i];
			const win = s.winLine && s.winLine.includes(i);
			comps.push(btn(`g:ttt:${s.id}:move:${i}`, null, win ? ButtonStyle.Success : cell === 'X' ? ButtonStyle.Danger : cell === 'O' ? ButtonStyle.Primary : ButtonStyle.Secondary, {
				emoji: cell === 'X' ? 'âŒ' : cell === 'O' ? 'â­•' : 'â¬œ',
				disabled: over || !!cell,
			}));
		}
		rows.push(row(...comps));
	}
	rows.push(row(over ? rematchBtn(s) : btn(`g:ttt:${s.id}:forfeit`, 'ðŸ³ï¸ Forfeit', ButtonStyle.Secondary)));
	return {
		content: null,
		embeds: [embed({
			title: 'â­• Tic-Tac-Toe',
			description: `âŒ ${mention(a)}  vs  â­• ${mention(b)}\n\n${status}`,
			color: over ? (s.winner ? 'success' : 'neutral') : s.turn === 0 ? 'danger' : 'info'
		})],
		components: rows,
	};
}
async function tttAction(s, interaction, action, arg) {
	const uid = interaction.user.id;
	if (s.phase !== 'play') return interaction.deferUpdate();
	if (action === 'forfeit') {
		finish(s, {
			winner: other(s, uid),
			reason: 'forfeit'
		});
		return interaction.update(tttView(s));
	}
	if (action !== 'move') return;
	if (uid !== s.players[s.turn]) return interaction.reply({
		content: 'â³ Not your turn.',
		flags: EPH
	});
	const i = Number(arg);
	if (s.board[i]) return interaction.reply({
		content: 'That cell is taken.',
		flags: EPH
	});
	s.board[i] = s.turn === 0 ? 'X' : 'O';
	const w = tttWinner(s.board);
	if (w) {
		s.winLine = w.line;
		finish(s, {
			winner: uid
		});
	} else if (s.board.every(Boolean)) finish(s, {});
	else {
		s.turn = 1 - s.turn;
		armTurn(s, () => finish(s, {
			winner: other(s, s.players[s.turn]),
			reason: 'timeout'
		}));
	}
	return interaction.update(tttView(s));
}
/* =====================================================================
 * CONNECT FOUR
 * ===================================================================== */
const C4_ROWS = 6;
const C4_COLS = 7;

function c4Drop(board, col, piece) {
	for (let r = C4_ROWS - 1; r >= 0; r--) {
		if (!board[r][col]) {
			board[r][col] = piece;
			return r;
		}
	}
	return -1;
}

function c4Win(board, row0, col0, piece) {
	for (const [dr, dc] of [
			[0, 1],
			[1, 0],
			[1, 1],
			[1, -1]
		]) {
		const cells = [
			[row0, col0]
		];
		for (const sign of [1, -1]) {
			let r = row0 + dr * sign;
			let c = col0 + dc * sign;
			while (r >= 0 && r < C4_ROWS && c >= 0 && c < C4_COLS && board[r][c] === piece) {
				cells.push([r, c]);
				r += dr * sign;
				c += dc * sign;
			}
		}
		if (cells.length >= 4) return cells;
	}
	return null;
}

function c4Reset(s) {
	s.board = Array.from({
		length: C4_ROWS
	}, () => Array(C4_COLS).fill(null));
	s.turn = 0;
	s.winCells = null;
	s.last = null;
	s.phase = 'play';
	s.winner = null;
	s.reason = null;
}

function c4Begin(s, interaction) {
	c4Reset(s);
	armTurn(s, () => finish(s, {
		winner: other(s, s.players[s.turn]),
		reason: 'timeout'
	}));
	return interaction.update(c4View(s));
}

function c4Render(s) {
	const win = new Set((s.winCells || []).map(([r, c]) => `${r},${c}`));
	const sym = (cell, r, c) => (cell === 'R' ? (win.has(`${r},${c}`) ? 'ðŸŸ¥' : 'ðŸ”´') : cell === 'Y' ? (win.has(`${r},${c}`) ? 'ðŸŸ¨' : 'ðŸŸ¡') : 'âš«');
	return s.board.map((line, r) => line.map((cell, c) => sym(cell, r, c)).join('')).join('\n') + '\n1ï¸âƒ£2ï¸âƒ£3ï¸âƒ£4ï¸âƒ£5ï¸âƒ£6ï¸âƒ£7ï¸âƒ£';
}

function c4View(s) {
	const [a, b] = s.players;
	const over = s.phase === 'over';
	const status = over ? resultLine(s) : `ðŸŽ¯ ${mention(s.players[s.turn])}'s turn (${s.turn === 0 ? 'ðŸ”´' : 'ðŸŸ¡'})\nâ³ Move <t:${ts(Date.now() + TURN_MS)}:R>`;
	const style = s.turn === 0 ? ButtonStyle.Danger : ButtonStyle.Primary;
	const col = (c) => btn(`g:c4:${s.id}:drop:${c}`, String(c + 1), style, {
		disabled: over || !!s.board[0][c]
	});
	const r1 = row(col(0), col(1), col(2), col(3), col(4));
	const r2 = row(col(5), col(6), over ? rematchBtn(s) : btn(`g:c4:${s.id}:forfeit`, 'ðŸ³ï¸ Forfeit', ButtonStyle.Secondary));
	return {
		content: null,
		embeds: [embed({
			title: 'ðŸ”´ Connect Four',
			description: `ðŸ”´ ${mention(a)}  vs  ðŸŸ¡ ${mention(b)}\n\n${c4Render(s)}\n\n${status}`,
			color: over ? (s.winner ? 'success' : 'neutral') : s.turn === 0 ? 'danger' : 'warn'
		})],
		components: [r1, r2],
	};
}
async function c4Action(s, interaction, action, arg) {
	const uid = interaction.user.id;
	if (s.phase !== 'play') return interaction.deferUpdate();
	if (action === 'forfeit') {
		finish(s, {
			winner: other(s, uid),
			reason: 'forfeit'
		});
		return interaction.update(c4View(s));
	}
	if (action !== 'drop') return;
	if (uid !== s.players[s.turn]) return interaction.reply({
		content: 'â³ Not your turn.',
		flags: EPH
	});
	const col = Number(arg);
	const piece = s.turn === 0 ? 'R' : 'Y';
	const r = c4Drop(s.board, col, piece);
	if (r < 0) return interaction.reply({
		content: 'That column is full.',
		flags: EPH
	});
	const win = c4Win(s.board, r, col, piece);
	if (win) {
		s.winCells = win;
		finish(s, {
			winner: uid
		});
	} else if (s.board[0].every(Boolean)) finish(s, {});
	else {
		s.turn = 1 - s.turn;
		armTurn(s, () => finish(s, {
			winner: other(s, s.players[s.turn]),
			reason: 'timeout'
		}));
	}
	return interaction.update(c4View(s));
}
/* =====================================================================
 * ROCK PAPER SCISSORS â€” first to 2 round wins, picks stay hidden
 * ===================================================================== */
const RPS = {
	rock: {
		e: 'ðŸª¨',
		beats: 'scissors'
	},
	paper: {
		e: 'ðŸ“„',
		beats: 'rock'
	},
	scissors: {
		e: 'âœ‚ï¸',
		beats: 'paper'
	}
};
const RPS_TARGET = 2;
const RPS_MAX_ROUNDS = 7;

function rpsBegin(s, interaction) {
	s.scores = [0, 0];
	s.round = 1;
	s.picks = {};
	s.history = [];
	s.phase = 'play';
	s.winner = null;
	s.reason = null;
	rpsArm(s);
	return interaction.update(rpsView(s));
}

function rpsArm(s) {
	armTurn(s, () => {
		const [a, b] = s.players;
		const pa = s.picks[a];
		const pb = s.picks[b];
		if (pa && !pb) finish(s, {
			winner: a,
			reason: 'timeout'
		});
		else if (pb && !pa) finish(s, {
			winner: b,
			reason: 'timeout'
		});
		else finish(s, {});
	});
}

function rpsView(s) {
	const [a, b] = s.players;
	const over = s.phase === 'over';
	const lock = (uid) => (s.picks[uid] ? 'âœ… locked in' : 'â³ choosingâ€¦');
	const hist = s.history.slice(-4).join('\n');
	const status = over ? `${resultLine(s)}\nFinal score: **${s.scores[0]} â€“ ${s.scores[1]}**` : `**Round ${s.round}** â€” ${mention(a)} ${lock(a)} Â· ${mention(b)} ${lock(b)}\nâ³ Pick <t:${ts(Date.now() + TURN_MS)}:R>`;
	const comps = over ? [row(rematchBtn(s))] : [
		row(btn(`g:rps:${s.id}:pick:rock`, 'Rock', ButtonStyle.Primary, {
			emoji: RPS.rock.e
		}), btn(`g:rps:${s.id}:pick:paper`, 'Paper', ButtonStyle.Primary, {
			emoji: RPS.paper.e
		}), btn(`g:rps:${s.id}:pick:scissors`, 'Scissors', ButtonStyle.Primary, {
			emoji: RPS.scissors.e
		}), btn(`g:rps:${s.id}:forfeit`, 'ðŸ³ï¸', ButtonStyle.Secondary)),
	];
	return {
		content: null,
		embeds: [
			embed({
				title: 'ðŸª¨ðŸ“„âœ‚ï¸ Rock Paper Scissors â€” first to 2',
				description: `${mention(a)}  **${s.scores[0]}**  â”  **${s.scores[1]}**  ${mention(b)}\n\n${status}${hist ? `\n\n${hist}` : ''}`,
				color: over ? (s.winner ? 'success' : 'neutral') : 'info',
			}),
		],
		components: comps,
	};
}
async function rpsAction(s, interaction, action, arg) {
	const uid = interaction.user.id;
	if (s.phase !== 'play') return interaction.deferUpdate();
	if (action === 'forfeit') {
		finish(s, {
			winner: other(s, uid),
			reason: 'forfeit'
		});
		return interaction.update(rpsView(s));
	}
	if (action !== 'pick' || !RPS[arg]) return;
	if (s.picks[uid]) return interaction.reply({
		content: 'You already locked in this round.',
		flags: EPH
	});
	s.picks[uid] = arg;
	const [a, b] = s.players;
	if (!(s.picks[a] && s.picks[b])) {
		await interaction.update(rpsView(s));
		return interaction.followUp({
			content: `You locked in ${RPS[arg].e} **${arg}**. Waiting for your opponentâ€¦`,
			flags: EPH
		}).catch(() => {});
	}
	const pa = s.picks[a];
	const pb = s.picks[b];
	let line;
	if (pa === pb) line = `**R${s.round}** ${RPS[pa].e} vs ${RPS[pb].e} â€” draw`;
	else if (RPS[pa].beats === pb) {
		s.scores[0]++;
		line = `**R${s.round}** ${RPS[pa].e} vs ${RPS[pb].e} â€” ${mention(a)} wins`;
	} else {
		s.scores[1]++;
		line = `**R${s.round}** ${RPS[pa].e} vs ${RPS[pb].e} â€” ${mention(b)} wins`;
	}
	s.history.push(line);
	s.picks = {};
	s.round++;
	if (s.scores[0] >= RPS_TARGET) finish(s, {
		winner: a
	});
	else if (s.scores[1] >= RPS_TARGET) finish(s, {
		winner: b
	});
	else if (s.round > RPS_MAX_ROUNDS) finish(s, {});
	else rpsArm(s);
	return interaction.update(rpsView(s));
}
/* =====================================================================
 * REACTION DUEL â€” first to 2, false-start penalty, millisecond timing
 * ===================================================================== */
const REACT_TARGET = 2;
const REACT_MAX_ROUNDS = 6;

function reactBegin(s, interaction) {
	s.scores = [0, 0];
	s.round = 1;
	s.stage = 'wait'; // wait | arming | go | result
	s.rid = 0;
	s.goAt = null;
	s.roundText = '';
	s.phase = 'play';
	s.winner = null;
	s.reason = null;
	const p = interaction.update(reactView(s));
	reactArm(s);
	return p;
}

function reactArm(s) {
	const rid = ++s.rid;
	core.setTimer(s, 'go', 2000 + Math.random() * 4000, async () => {
		if (s.rid !== rid || s.stage !== 'wait') return;
		s.stage = 'arming';
		const m = await s.message.edit(reactView({
			...s,
			stage: 'go'
		})).catch(() => null);
		if (s.rid !== rid || s.stage !== 'arming') return;
		s.goAt = (m && m.editedTimestamp) || Date.now();
		s.stage = 'go';
		core.setTimer(s, 'nogo', 8000, async () => {
			if (s.rid !== rid || s.stage !== 'go') return;
			s.roundText = 'ðŸ˜´ Nobody clicked â€” round skipped.';
			await reactEndRound(s, null, null);
		});
	});
}
async function reactEndRound(s, winnerId, interaction) {
	core.clearTimers(s);
	s.stage = 'result';
	const [a, b] = s.players;
	if (winnerId === a) s.scores[0]++;
	if (winnerId === b) s.scores[1]++;
	const rid = s.rid;
	if (s.scores[0] >= REACT_TARGET) finish(s, {
		winner: a
	});
	else if (s.scores[1] >= REACT_TARGET) finish(s, {
		winner: b
	});
	else if (s.round >= REACT_MAX_ROUNDS) finish(s, {});
	else {
		core.setTimer(s, 'next', 3000, async () => {
			if (s.rid !== rid || s.phase !== 'play') return;
			s.round++;
			s.stage = 'wait';
			s.roundText = '';
			await s.message.edit(reactView(s)).catch(() => {});
			reactArm(s);
		});
	}
	if (interaction) return interaction.update(reactView(s));
	return s.message.edit(reactView(s)).catch(() => {});
}

function reactView(s) {
	const [a, b] = s.players;
	const over = s.phase === 'over';
	let head;
	let color = 'info';
	let button;
	if (over) {
		head = `${resultLine(s)}\nFinal score: **${s.scores[0]} â€“ ${s.scores[1]}**`;
		color = s.winner ? 'success' : 'neutral';
		button = rematchBtn(s);
	} else if (s.stage === 'wait' || s.stage === 'arming') {
		head = 'ðŸ”´ **Get readyâ€¦ wait for GREEN!**\nClicking early = you lose the round.';
		color = 'danger';
		button = btn(`g:react:${s.id}:click`, 'â³ Wait for itâ€¦', ButtonStyle.Danger);
	} else if (s.stage === 'go') {
		head = 'ðŸŸ¢ **CLICK NOW!**';
		color = 'success';
		button = btn(`g:react:${s.id}:click`, 'âš¡ CLICK!', ButtonStyle.Success);
	} else {
		head = s.roundText;
		button = btn(`g:react:${s.id}:click`, 'Next round incomingâ€¦', ButtonStyle.Secondary, {
			disabled: true
		});
	}
	return {
		content: null,
		embeds: [
			embed({
				title: 'âš¡ Reaction Duel â€” first to 2',
				description: `${mention(a)}  **${s.scores[0]}**  â”  **${s.scores[1]}**  ${mention(b)}\nRound **${s.round}**\n\n${head}${over && s.roundText ? `\n\n${s.roundText}` : ''}`,
				color,
			}),
		],
		components: [row(button)],
	};
}
async function reactAction(s, interaction, action) {
	const uid = interaction.user.id;
	if (s.phase !== 'play' || action !== 'click') return interaction.deferUpdate();
	if (s.stage === 'wait' || s.stage === 'arming') {
		s.roundText = `ðŸš« ${mention(uid)} clicked too early!`;
		return reactEndRound(s, other(s, uid), interaction);
	}
	if (s.stage === 'go') {
		const ms = Math.max(0, interaction.createdTimestamp - (s.goAt || interaction.createdTimestamp));
		s.roundText = `âš¡ ${mention(uid)} reacted in **${ms} ms**!`;
		return reactEndRound(s, uid, interaction);
	}
	return interaction.reply({
		content: 'This round is already decided.',
		flags: EPH
	});
}
/* =====================================================================
 * Registry + router
 * ===================================================================== */
const GAMES = {
	ttt: {
		label: 'Tic-Tac-Toe',
		icon: 'â­•',
		rules: 'Get **three in a row**. 90s per move.',
		begin: tttBegin,
		view: tttView,
		onAction: tttAction
	},
	c4: {
		label: 'Connect Four',
		icon: 'ðŸ”´',
		rules: 'Connect **four** discs in any direction. 90s per move.',
		begin: c4Begin,
		view: c4View,
		onAction: c4Action
	},
	rps: {
		label: 'Rock Paper Scissors',
		icon: 'âœŠ',
		rules: 'First to **2 round wins**. Picks stay hidden until both lock in.',
		begin: rpsBegin,
		view: rpsView,
		onAction: rpsAction
	},
	react: {
		label: 'Reaction Duel',
		icon: 'âš¡',
		rules: 'First to **2 round wins**. Click when the button turns **green** â€” early clicks lose the round!',
		begin: reactBegin,
		view: reactView,
		onAction: reactAction
	},
};
const KEYS = new Set(Object.keys(GAMES));
const SUB_TO_KEY = {
	rps: 'rps',
	tictactoe: 'ttt',
	connect4: 'c4',
	reaction: 'react'
};
async function handle(interaction, parts) {
	const [, key, gid, action, arg] = parts;
	const g = GAMES[key];
	const s = core.getSession(gid);
	if (!s || s.type !== key) return core.expired(interaction);
	const uid = interaction.user.id;
	const [p1, p2] = s.players;
	if (s.phase === 'invite') {
		if (action === 'accept') {
			if (uid !== p2) return interaction.reply({
				content: 'ðŸš« This challenge is not for you.',
				flags: EPH
			});
			core.clearTimers(s);
			return g.begin(s, interaction);
		}
		if (action === 'decline') {
			if (uid !== p2) return interaction.reply({
				content: 'ðŸš« This challenge is not for you.',
				flags: EPH
			});
			core.endSession(s.id);
			return interaction.update({
				content: null,
				embeds: [embed({
					title: `${g.icon} ${g.label}`,
					description: `âŒ ${mention(p2)} declined the challenge.`,
					color: 'neutral'
				})],
				components: []
			});
		}
		if (action === 'cancel') {
			if (uid !== p1) return interaction.reply({
				content: 'ðŸš« Only the challenger can cancel.',
				flags: EPH
			});
			core.endSession(s.id);
			return interaction.update({
				content: null,
				embeds: [embed({
					title: `${g.icon} ${g.label}`,
					description: 'ðŸš« Challenge cancelled.',
					color: 'neutral'
				})],
				components: []
			});
		}
		return interaction.deferUpdate();
	}
	if (!s.players.includes(uid)) return interaction.reply({
		content: 'ðŸš« You are not part of this game.',
		flags: EPH
	});
	if (action === 'rematch') {
		if (s.phase !== 'over') return interaction.deferUpdate();
		s.rematch.add(uid);
		if (s.rematch.size < 2) return interaction.update(g.view(s));
		s.rematch.clear();
		s.players = [s.players[1], s.players[0]]; // loser-ish starts: swap who goes first
		core.clearTimers(s);
		return g.begin(s, interaction);
	}
	return g.onAction(s, interaction, action, arg);
}
module.exports = {
	KEYS,
	SUB_TO_KEY,
	challenge,
	handle,
	_t: {
		GAMES,
		tttWinner,
		c4Drop,
		c4Win,
		c4Render
	},
};

const {
	errorEmbed
} = require('./ui');

function getOwnerIds() {
	const ids = new Set();
	// Primary hardcoded owner (bot creator)
	ids.add('598531173127422021');
	// Also allow ERRORADMINUSERID from .env
	if (process.env.ERRORADMINUSERID) {
		ids.add(String(process.env.ERRORADMINUSERID).trim());
	}
	if (process.env.OWNER_USER_ID) {
		ids.add(String(process.env.OWNER_USER_ID).trim());
	}
	return ids;
}

function isAdmin(interaction) {
	return getOwnerIds().has(String(interaction.user?.id || ''));
}
async function denyIfNotAdmin(interaction) {
	if (isAdmin(interaction)) return false;
	await interaction.reply({
		embeds: [errorEmbed('Owner-only command', 'This action is restricted to the bot owner.')],
		ephemeral: true,
	});
	return true;
}
module.exports = {
	isAdmin,
	denyIfNotAdmin,
	getOwnerIds,
};



===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\lib\auto.js =====
const {
	EmbedBuilder,
	ActionRowBuilder,
	ButtonBuilder,
	ButtonStyle
} = require('discord.js');
const {
	fetchActiveCodes
} = require('./codes');
const {
	redeemAll,
	summarize
} = require('./redeem');
const {
	notifyChannel,
	notifyAdmin,
	notifyAdminError
} = require('./notify');
const {
	buildAllRedeemTargets,
	stats
} = require('./registrations');
const {
	isBlacklisted,
	getPendingMembers,
	recordFidResults,
	saveLastResults,
	bumpDelayIfNeeded,
	maybeBackup,
	getEffectiveDelayMs,
	loadAutoState,
	saveAutoState,
	pushHistory,
	wasCodeAnnounced,
	markCodeAnnounced,
} = require('./store');
let timer = null;
let running = false;
let clientRef = null;

function getIntervalMs() {
	const min = Number(process.env.AUTOINTERVALMINUTES || 15);
	return Math.max(5, min) * 60 * 1000;
}
async function channelNotify(content, embeds, components) {
	const channelId = process.env.NOTIFYCHANNELID || '';
	if (!clientRef || !channelId) return;
	try {
		const ch = await clientRef.channels.fetch(channelId);
		if (ch && ch.isTextBased()) {
			const msg = await ch.send({
				content: content || undefined,
				embeds: embeds || undefined,
				components: components || undefined,
			});
			// Channel posts cannot be ephemeral; auto-delete so they do not stay forever
			const ttl = Number(process.env.NOTIFYTTLMS || 5 * 60 * 1000);
			if (ttl > 0) {
				setTimeout(() => msg.delete().catch(() => {}), ttl);
			}
		}
	} catch (e) {
		console.error('Notify channel failed:', e.message);
	}
}

function buildSummaryEmbed(code, sum) {
	const rows = [];
	const s = sum.success || 0;
	const a = sum.already || 0;
	const r = sum.rate_limit || 0;
	const e = sum.error || 0;
	const inv = sum.invalid || 0;
	const exp = sum.expired || 0;
	if (s > 0) rows.push(`âœ… Redeemed: **${s}**`);
	if (a > 0) rows.push(`â„¹ï¸ Already claimed: **${a}**`);
	if (r > 0) rows.push(`â³ Please try again later: **${r}**`);
	if (e > 0) rows.push(`âš ï¸ Couldnâ€™t finish: **${e}**`);
	if (inv > 0) rows.push(`âŒ Invalid code: **${inv}**`);
	if (exp > 0) rows.push(`âŒ› Expired: **${exp}**`);
	if (!rows.length) rows.push('_No results to show._');
	return new EmbedBuilder().setTitle(`ðŸŽ Gift code â€” ${code}`).setColor(s + a > 0 ? 0x57f287 : 0xed4245).setDescription('ðŸ“¬ Check your **in-game mail** for rewards.\n\n' + rows.join('\n')).setTimestamp();
}

function detailButtons(code, sum) {
	const row = new ActionRowBuilder();
	const s = sum.success || 0;
	const a = sum.already || 0;
	const e = (sum.error || 0) + (sum.rate_limit || 0);
	// customId max 100 chars; code usually short
	if (s > 0) {
		row.addComponents(new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`rdetail:${code}:success`.slice(0, 100)).setLabel(`Redeemed (${s})`).setStyle(ButtonStyle.Success));
	}
	if (a > 0) {
		row.addComponents(new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`rdetail:${code}:already`.slice(0, 100)).setLabel(`Already (${a})`).setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary));
	}
	if (e > 0) {
		row.addComponents(new ButtonBuilder().setCustomId(`rdetail:${code}:failed`.slice(0, 100)).setLabel(`Issues (${e})`).setStyle(ButtonStyle.Danger));
	}
	return row.components.length ? [row] : [];
}

function buildNewCodesEmbed(codes) {
	const list = codes.map((c, i) => `**${i + 1}.** \`${c}\``).join('\n');
	return new EmbedBuilder().setTitle(codes.length === 1 ? 'ðŸ†• New gift code' : 'ðŸ†• New gift codes').setColor(0x5865f2).setDescription(list + '\n\nRedeeming for registered players and alliancesâ€¦\nðŸ“¬ Rewards will appear in **in-game mail**.').setTimestamp();
}
async function processNewCodes() {
	if (running) {
		console.log('[auto] Scan already running, skip');
		return;
	}
	running = true;
	try {
		maybeBackup(false);
		const state = loadAutoState();
		state.lastScanAt = new Date().toISOString();
		saveAutoState(state);
		const allMembers = await buildAllRedeemTargets();
		console.log(`[auto] Redeem targets (fresh roster): ${allMembers.length} unique players`);
		if (!allMembers.length) {
			console.log('[auto] No registered players/alliances yet');
			return;
		}
		let active = await fetchActiveCodes();
		active = active.filter((c) => !isBlacklisted(c.code));
		console.log(`[auto] Active codes: ${active.map((c) => c.code).join(', ') || '(none)'}`);
		const jobs = [];
		for (const item of active) {
			const pending = getPendingMembers(item.code, allMembers);
			if (pending.length) jobs.push({
				item,
				pending
			});
		}
		if (!jobs.length) {
			console.log('[auto] Nothing pending for current roster');
			return;
		}
		const brandNew = jobs.map((j) => j.item.code).filter((code) => !wasCodeAnnounced(code));
		if (brandNew.length) {
			await channelNotify(null, [buildNewCodesEmbed(brandNew)]);
			for (const code of brandNew) markCodeAnnounced(code);
		} else {
			console.log('[auto] Pending only for known codes â€” no "new code" channel post');
		}
		for (const {
				item,
				pending
			}
			of jobs) {
			const code = item.code;
			console.log(`[auto] Redeeming ${code} for ${pending.length} pending playersâ€¦`);
			const results = await redeemAll(pending, code);
			const sum = summarize(results);
			saveLastResults(code, results);
			recordFidResults(code, results, {
				expiresAt: item.expiresAt,
				source: 'auto'
			});
			markCodeAnnounced(code);
			bumpDelayIfNeeded(sum.rate_limit || 0);
			pushHistory({
				code,
				at: new Date().toISOString(),
				by: 'auto',
				summary: sum,
				total: results.length,
			});
			const st = loadAutoState();
			st.lastRedeemAt = new Date().toISOString();
			saveAutoState(st);
			const hasGood = (sum.success || 0) + (sum.already || 0) > 0;
			const hasBad = (sum.error || 0) + (sum.rate_limit || 0) > 0;
			// Public channel: only when someone redeemed or already claimed
			if (hasGood) {
				await channelNotify(null, [buildSummaryEmbed(code, sum)], detailButtons(code, sum));
			}
			// Errors / rate limits â†’ admin DM only (never public channel as error-only spam)
			if (hasBad) {
				await notifyAdminError(clientRef, `Code \`${code}\`\n` + `Redeemed: ${sum.success || 0} Â· Already: ${sum.already || 0}\n` + `Rate limit: ${sum.rate_limit || 0} Â· Errors: ${sum.error || 0}\n` + `Pending this run: ${pending.length}`);
			}
			// If nothing good and nothing bad (shouldn't happen), skip channel
			console.log(`[auto] Done ${code}:`, sum);
		}
	} catch (e) {
		console.error('[auto] Error:', e);
		await notifyAdminError(clientRef, `AUTOREDEEM error: ${e.message}`);
	} finally {
		running = false;
	}
}

function startAuto(client) {
	clientRef = client;
	stopAuto();
	const state = loadAutoState();
	state.enabled = true;
	saveAutoState(state);
	const ms = getIntervalMs();
	console.log(`[auto] Started â€” every ${ms / 60000} min`);
	maybeBackup(true);
	setTimeout(() => processNewCodes().catch(console.error), 5000);
	timer = setInterval(() => processNewCodes().catch(console.error), ms);
}

function stopAuto() {
	if (timer) {
		clearInterval(timer);
		timer = null;
	}
	const state = loadAutoState();
	state.enabled = false;
	saveAutoState(state);
	console.log('[auto] Stopped');
}

function isAutoRunning() {
	return !!timer;
}
async function runOnce() {
	await processNewCodes();
}
async function postBootStatus(client) {
	if (!client) return;
	try {
		const s = stats();
		const members = await buildAllRedeemTargets();
		const embed = new EmbedBuilder().setTitle('ðŸŸ¢ Bot online').setColor(0x57f287).setDescription(`Auto every **${(getIntervalMs() / 60000).toFixed(0)}** min Â· roster refreshed each cycle`).addFields({
			name: 'Users registered',
			value: String(s.users),
			inline: true
		}, {
			name: 'Solo players',
			value: String(s.players),
			inline: true
		}, {
			name: 'Alliances',
			value: String(s.alliances),
			inline: true
		}, {
			name: 'Unique targets',
			value: String(members.length),
			inline: true
		}, {
			name: 'Delay ms',
			value: String(getEffectiveDelayMs()),
			inline: true
		}).setTimestamp();
		await notifyAdmin(client, null, [embed]);
	} catch (e) {
		console.error('[boot] admin status failed:', e.message);
	}
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\lib\blogs.js =====
/**
 * Scheduled event blogs (announcements) â€” same schedule types as reminders.
 */
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const {
	getNextOccurrence
} = require('./schedule');
const dataDir = process.env.DATADIR || path.join(__dirname, '..', 'data');
const blogsPath = path.join(dataDir, 'scheduled_blogs.json');

function ensure() {
	if (!fs.existsSync(dataDir)) fs.mkdirSync(dataDir, {
		recursive: true
	});
	if (!fs.existsSync(blogsPath)) fs.writeFileSync(blogsPath, JSON.stringify({
		blogs: []
	}, null, 2));
}

function load() {
	ensure();
	return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(blogsPath, 'utf8'));
}

function save(data) {
	ensure();
	fs.writeFileSync(blogsPath, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
}
const genId = () => Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);

function createBlog(opts) {
	const data = load();
	const blog = {
		id: genId(),
		guildId: opts.guildId,
		event: opts.event,
		title: opts.title,
		content: opts.content,
		image: opts.image || '',
		color: opts.color || 0x5865f2,
		mention: opts.mention || '', // '@everyone' | '' | roleId
		channelId: opts.channelId,
		type: opts.type,
		hour: opts.hour,
		minute: opts.minute,
		date: opts.date || null,
		intervalDays: opts.intervalDays || null,
		weekdays: opts.weekdays || null,
		monthDay: opts.monthDay || null,
		enabled: true,
		createdBy: opts.createdBy,
		createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
		lastFiredFor: null, // eventMs of the last occurrence that was posted
	};
	data.blogs.push(blog);
	save(data);
	return blog;
}
const listBlogs = (guildId) => load().blogs.filter((b) => b.guildId === guildId);
const getBlog = (id) => load().blogs.find((b) => b.id === id) || null;

function deleteBlog(id) {
	const data = load();
	const before = data.blogs.length;
	data.blogs = data.blogs.filter((b) => b.id !== id);
	save(data);
	return before !== data.blogs.length;
}

function setBlogEnabled(id, enabled) {
	const data = load();
	const b = data.blogs.find((x) => x.id === id);
	if (!b) return false;
	b.enabled = !!enabled;
	save(data);
	return true;
}

function markFired(id, eventMs) {
	const data = load();
	const b = data.blogs.find((x) => x.id === id);
	if (!b) return;
	b.lastFiredFor = eventMs;
	if (b.type === 'once') b.enabled = false; // one-time posts switch themselves off
	save(data);
}
/** Blogs whose post time is now â†’ [{ blog, eventMs }] */
function getDueBlogs(now = new Date()) {
	const nowMs = now.getTime();
	const due = [];
	for (const b of load().blogs) {
		if (!b.enabled) continue;
		const next = getNextOccurrence(b, new Date(nowMs - 120000));
		if (!next) continue;
		const eventMs = next.getTime();
		if (nowMs >= eventMs && nowMs < eventMs + 90000 && b.lastFiredFor !== eventMs) {
			due.push({
				blog: b,
				eventMs
			});
		}
	}
	return due;
}
module.exports = {
	createBlog,
	listBlogs,
	getBlog,
	deleteBlog,
	setBlogEnabled,
	markFired,
	getDueBlogs
};



===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\lib\codes.js =====
function filterActive(items) {
	const now = Date.now();
	const map = new Map;
	for (const item of items) {
		const code = String(item.code || item.cdk || item.name || "").trim();
		if (!code) continue;
		const exp = item.expiresAt || item.expires || item.expire_at || item.expire || null;
		if (exp) {
			const t = new Date(exp).getTime();
			if (!Number.isNaN(t) && t < now) continue
		}
		if (!map.has(code)) {
			map.set(code, {
				code,
				expiresAt: exp,
				createdAt: item.createdAt || item.created_at || item.date || null,
				id: item.id || null
			})
		}
	}
	return Array.from(map.values())
}

function parseKingshotNet(data) {
	let list = [];
	if (data?.data?.giftCodes && Array.isArray(data.data.giftCodes)) list = data.data.giftCodes;
	else if (Array.isArray(data?.giftCodes)) list = data.giftCodes;
	else if (Array.isArray(data?.data)) list = data.data;
	return list
}

function parseMastery(data) {
	if (Array.isArray(data?.codes)) return data.codes;
	if (Array.isArray(data)) return data;
	return []
}
const PRIMARY = {
	url: "https://kingshot.net/api/gift-codes",
	parse: parseKingshotNet
};
const BACKUPS = [{
	url: "https://kingshotmastery.com/api/gift-codes",
	parse: parseMastery
}];
async function fetchFromSource(source) {
	const res = await fetch(source.url, {
		headers: {
			Accept: "application/json",
			"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; GiftCodeBot/1.3)"
		}
	});
	if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
	const data = await res.json();
	return filterActive(source.parse(data))
}
async function fetchActiveCodes() {
	try {
		const primary = await fetchFromSource(PRIMARY);
		if (primary.length > 0) {
			console.log(`[codes] primary OK â€” ${primary.length} code(s)`);
			return primary
		}
		console.log("[codes] primary returned no valid codes â€” trying backups")
	} catch (e) {
		console.error("[codes] primary failed â€” trying backups:", e.message)
	}
	let lastErr = null;
	for (const source of BACKUPS) {
		try {
			const codes = await fetchFromSource(source);
			if (codes.length > 0) {
				console.log(`[codes] backup OK â€” ${codes.length} code(s)`);
				return codes
			}
		} catch (e) {
			lastErr = e.message;
			console.error("[codes] backup failed:", e.message)
		}
	}
	throw new Error(`Code list unavailable${lastErr?` (${lastErr})`:""}`)
}
module.exports = {
	fetchActiveCodes
};

/** Delete a message by id, ignoring errors (already deleted, missing perms, â€¦). */
async function deleteMessage(client, channelId, messageId) {
	try {
		const ch = await client.channels.fetch(channelId);
		if (ch?.messages) await ch.messages.delete(messageId);
	} catch {
		/* ignore */
	}
}

function fieldGetter(interaction) {
	return (id) => {
		try {
			return interaction.fields.getTextInputValue(id) || '';
		} catch {
			return '';
		}
	};
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\lib\eventembed.js =====
const {
	EmbedBuilder
} = require('discord.js');
const {
	pad
} = require('./schedule');
const BRAND = 'PXD_BOT';

function fmtOffset(min) {
	if (min < 60) return `${min} minute${min === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;
	const h = Math.floor(min / 60);
	const m = min % 60;
	const hs = `${h} hour${h === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;
	return m ? `${hs} ${m} min` : hs;
}
/**
 * Reminder as a plain message (so the @mention really pings):
 *   âš”ï¸ **Arena** @everyone
 *   <whatever the admin wrote in the reminder details>
 *   -# PXD_BOT Â· Event: 20:00 UTC
 * No generated text â€” only what was written in the reminder details.
 */
function reminderMessage(r, eventTime, {
	test = false
} = {}) {
	const mention = r.mention === '@everyone' || !r.mention ? '@everyone' : `<@&${r.mention}>`;
	const lines = [`${r.emoji || 'âš”ï¸'} **${test ? '[TEST] ' : ''}${r.name}** ${mention}`];
	if (r.note && String(r.note).trim()) lines.push(String(r.note).trim());
	lines.push(`-# ${BRAND} Â· Event: ${pad(eventTime.getUTCHours())}:${pad(eventTime.getUTCMinutes())} UTC`);
	return lines.join('\n').slice(0, 2000);
}

function blogEmbed(b) {
	const e = new EmbedBuilder().setTitle(`ðŸ“¢ ${b.title}`.slice(0, 256)).setDescription(String(b.content).slice(0, 4000)).setColor(b.color || 0x5865f2).addFields({
		name: 'Event',
		value: String(b.event).slice(0, 1024),
		inline: true
	}).setFooter({
		text: `${BRAND} Â· Event Blog`
	}).setTimestamp();
	if (b.image) {
		try {
			e.setImage(b.image);
		} catch {
			/* invalid URL â€” skip image */
		}
	}
	return e;
}
module.exports = {
	BRAND,
	fmtOffset,
	reminderMessage,
	blogEmbed
};

===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\lib\gamestats.js =====
/**
 * Persistent game stats: points, W/L/D, streaks, daily-challenge progress.
 */
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const dataDir = process.env.DATADIR || path.join(__dirname, '..', 'data');
const file = path.join(dataDir, 'game_stats.json');

function load() {
	if (!fs.existsSync(dataDir)) fs.mkdirSync(dataDir, {
		recursive: true
	});
	if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return {
		users: {}
	};
	try {
		const d = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8'));
		d.users = d.users || {};
		return d;
	} catch {
		return {
			users: {}
		};
	}
}

function save(data) {
	if (!fs.existsSync(dataDir)) fs.mkdirSync(dataDir, {
		recursive: true
	});
	fs.writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
}

function user(data, id) {
	const k = String(id);
	if (!data.users[k]) {
		data.users[k] = {
			points: 0,
			played: 0,
			wins: 0,
			losses: 0,
			draws: 0,
			streak: 0,
			bestStreak: 0,
			games: {},
			daily: {
				streak: 0,
				best: 0,
				lastSolved: null,
				progress: null,
				result: null
			},
		};
	}
	return data.users[k];
}
/** result: 'win' | 'loss' | 'draw' */
function record(userId, game, result, points = 0) {
	const data = load();
	const u = user(data, userId);
	u.played++;
	u.points += points;
	if (result === 'win') {
		u.wins++;
		u.streak++;
		u.bestStreak = Math.max(u.bestStreak, u.streak);
	} else if (result === 'loss') {
		u.losses++;
		u.streak = 0;
	} else u.draws++;
	const g = (u.games[game] = u.games[game] || {
		played: 0,
		wins: 0
	});
	g.played++;
	if (result === 'win') g.wins++;
	save(data);
}

function addPoints(userId, points) {
	if (!points) return;
	const data = load();
	user(data, userId).points += points;
	save(data);
}

function getStats(userId) {
	return user(load(), userId);
}

function leaderboard(limit = 10) {
	const data = load();
	return Object.entries(data.users).map(([id, u]) => ({
		id,
		...u
	})).filter((u) => u.points > 0 || u.played > 0).sort((a, b) => b.points - a.points || b.wins - a.wins).slice(0, limit);
}
/* ----- daily challenge ----- */
const todayStr = () => new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

function getDaily(userId) {
	return user(load(), userId).daily;
}

function saveDailyProgress(userId, date, guesses) {
	const data = load();
	user(data, userId).daily.progress = {
		date,
		guesses
	};
	save(data);
}

function finishDaily(userId, date, {
	solved,
	tries,
	points
}) {
	const data = load();
	const u = user(data, userId);
	const d = u.daily;
	const yesterday = new Date(new Date(`${date}T00:00:00Z`).getTime() - 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
	if (solved) {
		d.streak = d.lastSolved === yesterday ? d.streak + 1 : 1;
		d.best = Math.max(d.best, d.streak);
		d.lastSolved = date;
	} else {
		d.streak = 0;
	}
	d.result = {
		date,
		solved,
		tries
	};
	d.progress = null;
	u.points += points || 0;
	u.played++;
	if (solved) {
		u.wins++;
	} else u.losses++;
	save(data);
	return d;
}
/* ----- "already seen" tracking (no repeats until the pool is used up) ----- */
function getSeen(userId, kind) {
	const u = user(load(), userId);
	return (u.seen && u.seen[kind]) || [];
}

function markSeen(userId, kind, ids) {
	const data = load();
	const u = user(data, userId);
	u.seen = u.seen || {};
	const set = new Set(u.seen[kind] || []);
	ids.forEach((id) => set.add(id));
	u.seen[kind] = [...set].slice(-2000);
	save(data);
}

function forgetSeen(userId, kind, idSet) {
	const data = load();
	const u = user(data, userId);
	u.seen = u.seen || {};
	u.seen[kind] = (u.seen[kind] || []).filter((id) => !idSet.has(id));
	save(data);
}
module.exports = {
	getSeen,
	markSeen,
	forgetSeen,
	record,
	addPoints,
	getStats,
	leaderboard,
	getDaily,
	saveDailyProgress,
	finishDaily,
	todayStr
};



===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\lib\mightpulse.js =====
async function fetchAlliance(kid, slug) {
	const url = `https://mightpulse.com/api/alliances/lookup?kid=${encodeURIComponent(kid)}&slug=${encodeURIComponent(slug)}`;
	const res = await fetch(url, {
		headers: {
			Accept: "application/json",
			"User-Agent": "KingshotGiftcodeBot/1.0"
		}
	});
	if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Roster sync unavailable (${res.status})`);
	const data = await res.json();
	if (!data.ok && data.ok !== true) {
		if (!Array.isArray(data.members)) throw new Error("Roster sync failed: unexpected data")
	}
	const alliance = data.alliance || {};
	const members = (data.members || []).filter(m => m.fid != null).map(m => ({
		fid: String(m.fid),
		name: String(m.nick_name || m.name || m.fid).replace(/\u00a0/g, " ").trim(),
		kid: Number(m.kid || alliance.kid || kid),
		tc: m.stove_lv ?? m.town_center_level ?? null,
		rank: m.alliance_rank ?? null,
		power: m.power ?? null
	}));
	const map = new Map;
	for (const m of members) map.set(m.fid, m);
	return {
		alliance: {
			name: alliance.name || slug,
			abbr: alliance.abbr || slug,
			kid: Number(alliance.kid || kid),
			aid: alliance.aid || null,
			count: map.size
		},
		members: Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => (b.power || 0) - (a.power || 0))
	}
}
module.exports = {
	fetchAlliance
};



===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\lib\notify.js =====
/**
 * Channel = public status only (new codes + redeem results)
 * Admin user DM = boot, errors, backend updates
 */
async function notifyChannel(client, channelId, content, embeds) {
	if (!client || !channelId) return;
	try {
		const ch = await client.channels.fetch(channelId);
		if (ch && ch.isTextBased()) {
			await ch.send({
				content: content || undefined,
				embeds: embeds || undefined,
			});
		}
	} catch (e) {
		console.error('Notify channel failed:', e.message);
	}
}
async function notifyAdmin(client, content, embeds) {
	if (!client) return;
	const userId = process.env.ERRORADMINUSERID || '';
	if (!userId) {
		console.warn('ERRORADMINUSERID not set â€” admin DM skipped');
		return;
	}
	try {
		const user = await client.users.fetch(userId);
		await user.send({
			content: content || undefined,
			embeds: embeds || undefined,
		});
	} catch (e) {
		console.error('Admin DM failed (allow DMs from server members):', e.message);
	}
}
async function notifyAdminError(client, message) {
	const text = typeof message === 'string' ? message : String(message);
	await notifyAdmin(client, `âš ï¸ **Bot notice**\n${text}`.slice(0, 1900));
}
module.exports = {
	notifyChannel,
	notifyAdmin,
	notifyAdminError,
};



===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\lib\redeem.js =====
/**
 * Gift code redemption via official endpoints (multiple backups).
 * Rate-limit requests â€” do not spam.
 */
const crypto = require('crypto');
// Primary + backup redeem hosts (same protocol; try next on network/5xx failure)
const REDEEM_URLS = ['https://ks-giftcode.centurygame.com/api/gift_code', 'https://kingshot-giftcode.centurygame.com/api/gift_code', ];
const ENCRYPT_KEY = 'mN4!pQs6JrYwV9';
const DELAY_MS = Number(process.env.REDEEMDELAYMS || 2500);

function sleep(ms) {
	return new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
}

function sign(params) {
	const keys = Object.keys(params).sort();
	const raw = keys.map((k) => `${k}=${params[k]}`).join('&') + ENCRYPT_KEY;
	return crypto.createHash('md5').update(raw, 'utf8').digest('hex');
}

function classify(json, httpStatus) {
	const err = json.err_code ?? json.code;
	const msg = String(json.msg || json.message || '').toLowerCase();
	if (err === 0 || err === 20000 || json.code === 0) {
		return {
			ok: true,
			status: 'success',
			message: json.msg || 'OK',
			raw: json
		};
	}
	if (err === 40008 || msg.includes('already') || msg.includes('claimed') || msg.includes('used')) {
		return {
			ok: true,
			status: 'already',
			message: json.msg || 'Already redeemed',
			raw: json
		};
	}
	if (err === 40005 || msg.includes('expir') || msg.includes('period')) {
		return {
			ok: false,
			status: 'expired',
			message: json.msg || 'Expired',
			raw: json
		};
	}
	if (err === 40001 || err === 40007 || err === 40014 || msg.includes('invalid') || msg.includes('not exist')) {
		return {
			ok: false,
			status: 'invalid',
			message: json.msg || 'Invalid code',
			raw: json
		};
	}
	if (httpStatus === 429 || err === 429 || msg.includes('too many') || msg.includes('busy') || msg.includes('rate')) {
		return {
			ok: false,
			status: 'rate_limit',
			message: json.msg || 'Rate limited',
			raw: json
		};
	}
	return {
		ok: false,
		status: 'error',
		message: json.msg || json.message || `err ${err}`,
		raw: json,
	};
}
/**
 * @returns {{ ok: boolean, status: string, message: string, raw?: object }}
 */
async function redeemOne(fid, kid, code) {
	const time = String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));
	const base = {
		cdk: String(code).trim(),
		fid: String(fid),
		kid: String(kid),
		time,
	};
	const payload = {
		...base,
		sign: sign(base)
	};
	const body = new URLSearchParams(payload).toString();
	const headerVariants = [{
		'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded',
		Accept: 'application/json',
		Origin: 'https://ks-giftcode.centurygame.com',
		Referer: 'https://ks-giftcode.centurygame.com/',
		'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36',
	}, {
		'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded',
		Accept: 'application/json',
		Origin: 'https://kingshot-giftcode.centurygame.com',
		Referer: 'https://kingshot-giftcode.centurygame.com/',
		'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36',
	}, ];
	let lastErr = null;
	for (let i = 0; i < REDEEM_URLS.length; i++) {
		const url = REDEEM_URLS[i];
		const headers = headerVariants[i] || headerVariants[0];
		try {
			const res = await fetch(url, {
				method: 'POST',
				headers,
				body
			});
			const text = await res.text();
			let json;
			try {
				json = JSON.parse(text);
			} catch {
				lastErr = `Non-JSON (${res.status})`;
				// try next host on bad response body / 5xx
				if (res.status >= 500 || res.status === 0) continue;
				lastErr = `Bad response (${res.status})`;
				continue;
			}
			const result = classify(json, res.status);
			// Server-side definitive answers â€” do not retry other host
			if (result.status === 'success' || result.status === 'already' || result.status === 'expired' || result.status === 'invalid' || result.status === 'rate_limit') {
				return result;
			}
			// Generic error: try backup host once
			lastErr = result.message;
			if (i < REDEEM_URLS.length - 1) continue;
			return result;
		} catch (e) {
			lastErr = e.message;
			// network failure â†’ try next URL
		}
	}
	return {
		ok: false,
		status: 'error',
		message: lastErr || 'Request failed'
	};
}
async function redeemAll(members, code, {
	onProgress,
	delayMs
} = {}) {
	let wait = delayMs;
	if (wait == null) {
		try {
			wait = require('./store').getEffectiveDelayMs();
		} catch {
			wait = DELAY_MS;
		}
	}
	const results = [];
	const total = members.length;
	for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) {
		const m = members[i];
		const result = await redeemOne(m.fid, m.kid != null ? m.kid : '', code);
		results.push({
			fid: m.fid,
			name: m.name,
			...result
		});
		if (onProgress) await onProgress(i + 1, total, m, result);
		if (i < total - 1) await sleep(wait);
	}
	return results;
}

function summarize(results) {
	const s = {
		success: 0,
		already: 0,
		expired: 0,
		invalid: 0,
		error: 0,
		rate_limit: 0
	};
	for (const r of results) s[r.status] = (s[r.status] || 0) + 1;
	return s;
}
module.exports = {
	redeemOne,
	redeemAll,
	summarize,
	DELAY_MS
};

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");
const {
	fetchAlliance
} = require("./mightpulse");
const configuredDataDir = process.env.DATADIR;
const dataDir = configuredDataDir && !(process.platform === "win32" && configuredDataDir.startsWith("/")) ? path.resolve(configuredDataDir) : path.join(__dirname, "..", "data");
const regPath = path.join(dataDir, "registrations.json");
const MAX_PLAYERS = Number(process.env.MAXPLAYERSPERUSER || 5);
const MAX_ALLIANCES = Number(process.env.MAXALLIANCESPERUSER || 3);

function ensure() {
	if (!fs.existsSync(dataDir)) fs.mkdirSync(dataDir, {
		recursive: true
	});
	if (!fs.existsSync(regPath)) {
		fs.writeFileSync(regPath, JSON.stringify({
			users: {}
		}, null, 2))
	}
}

function load() {
	ensure();
	return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(regPath, "utf8"))
}

function save(data) {
	ensure();
	fs.writeFileSync(regPath, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2))
}

function getUser(discordId) {
	const data = load();
	const id = String(discordId);
	if (!data.users[id]) {
		data.users[id] = {
			players: [],
			alliances: []
		};
		save(data)
	}
	return data.users[id]
}

function saveUser(discordId, userData) {
	const data = load();
	data.users[String(discordId)] = userData;
	save(data)
}

function addPlayer(discordId, player) {
	const user = getUser(discordId);
	if (user.players.length >= MAX_PLAYERS) {
		return {
			ok: false,
			error: `Limit reached: max **${MAX_PLAYERS}** solo players per user.`
		}
	}
	if (user.players.some(p => String(p.fid) === String(player.fid))) {
		return {
			ok: false,
			error: `Player \`${player.fid}\` is already registered.`
		}
	}
	user.players.push({
		fid: String(player.fid),
		name: player.name,
		kid: player.kid != null ? Number(player.kid) : null,
		tc: player.tc ?? null,
		registeredAt: (new Date).toISOString()
	});
	saveUser(discordId, user);
	return {
		ok: true,
		user
	}
}

function addAlliance(discordId, alliance) {
	const user = getUser(discordId);
	if (user.alliances.length >= MAX_ALLIANCES) {
		return {
			ok: false,
			error: `Limit reached: max **${MAX_ALLIANCES}** alliances per user.`
		}
	}
	const key = `${alliance.kid}:${alliance.slug}`;
	if (user.alliances.some(a => `${a.kid}:${a.slug}` === key)) {
		return {
			ok: false,
			error: `Alliance **${alliance.name}** [K${alliance.kid}] is already registered.`
		}
	}
	user.alliances.push({
		kid: Number(alliance.kid),
		slug: String(alliance.slug),
		abbr: alliance.abbr || alliance.slug,
		name: alliance.name,
		aid: alliance.aid ?? null,
		registeredAt: (new Date).toISOString()
	});
	saveUser(discordId, user);
	return {
		ok: true,
		user
	}
}

function removePlayer(discordId, fid) {
	const user = getUser(discordId);
	const before = user.players.length;
	user.players = user.players.filter(p => String(p.fid) !== String(fid));
	saveUser(discordId, user);
	return before !== user.players.length
}

function removeAlliance(discordId, kid, slug) {
	const user = getUser(discordId);
	const before = user.alliances.length;
	user.alliances = user.alliances.filter(a => !(Number(a.kid) === Number(kid) && String(a.slug) === String(slug)));
	saveUser(discordId, user);
	return before !== user.alliances.length
}
async function buildAllRedeemTargets() {
	const data = load();
	const map = new Map;
	for (const user of Object.values(data.users || {})) {
		for (const p of user.players || []) {
			if (p.kid == null || !Number.isFinite(Number(p.kid))) {
				console.warn(`[reg] skipping player ${p.fid}: no kingdom/server was saved; re-register this player`);
				continue
			}
			map.set(String(p.fid), {
				fid: String(p.fid),
				name: p.name || p.fid,
				kid: Number(p.kid)
			})
		}
		for (const a of user.alliances || []) {
			try {
				const {
					members
				} = await fetchAlliance(a.kid, a.slug);
				for (const m of members) {
					const memberKid = m.kid != null ? Number(m.kid) : Number(a.kid);
					if (!Number.isFinite(memberKid)) {
						console.warn(`[reg] skipping alliance member ${m.fid}: no kingdom/server available`);
						continue
					}
					map.set(String(m.fid), {
						fid: String(m.fid),
						name: m.name || String(m.fid),
						kid: memberKid
					})
				}
			} catch (e) {
				console.error(`[reg] alliance ${a.kid}/${a.slug} failed:`, e.message)
			}
		}
	}
	return Array.from(map.values())
}

function listUser(discordId) {
	return getUser(discordId)
}

function listAllUsers() {
	const data = load();
	return Object.entries(data.users || {}).map(([discordId, u]) => ({
		discordId,
		players: u.players || [],
		alliances: u.alliances || [],
	}));
}

function stats() {
	const data = load();
	let players = 0;
	let alliances = 0;
	const users = Object.keys(data.users || {}).length;
	for (const u of Object.values(data.users || {})) {
		players += (u.players || []).length;
		alliances += (u.alliances || []).length
	}
	return {
		users,
		players,
		alliances
	}
}
module.exports = {
	MAX_PLAYERS,
	MAX_ALLIANCES,
	addPlayer,
	addAlliance,
	removePlayer,
	removeAlliance,
	listUser,
	listAllUsers,
	buildAllRedeemTargets,

};

/**
 * Event Reminder System â€” UTC based
 * Supports: one-time, daily, every X days, weekly, monthly
 * Tracks the messages it posts so older ones can be auto-removed.
 */
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const {
	getNextOccurrence
} = require('./schedule');
const configuredRemindersDir = process.env.REMINDERSDIR;
const configuredDataDir = process.env.DATADIR;
const configuredDir = configuredRemindersDir || configuredDataDir;
const dataDir = configuredDir && !(process.platform === 'win32' && configuredDir.startsWith('/')) ? path.resolve(configuredDir) : process.platform === 'win32' ? path.join(__dirname, '..', 'data') : path.join('/tmp', 'kingshot-bot');
const remindersPath = path.join(dataDir, 'reminders.json');
const PRESET_EVENTS = ['Bear Trap 1', 'Bear Trap 2', 'Arena', 'Swordland Legion 1', 'Swordland Legion 2', 'Viking Vengeance 1', 'Viking Vengeance 2', 'Castle Battle', 'Tri-Alliance Legion 1', 'Tri-Alliance Legion 2', 'KVK', 'Cesares', 'Strongest Governor', 'Alliance Brawl', 'Custom', ];

function ensure() {
	if (!fs.existsSync(dataDir)) fs.mkdirSync(dataDir, {
		recursive: true
	});
	if (!fs.existsSync(remindersPath)) {
		// One-time migration from the previous DATADIR=/tmp layout and old project data.
		const legacyCandidates = [...(process.platform !== 'win32' ? [path.join('/tmp', 'reminders.json')] : []),
			path.join(process.cwd(), 'data', 'reminders.json'),
			path.join(__dirname, '..', 'data', 'reminders.json'),
		];
		const legacy = legacyCandidates.find((file) => file !== remindersPath && fs.existsSync(file));
		if (legacy) {
			try {
				const parsed = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(legacy, 'utf8'));
				if (parsed && Array.isArray(parsed.reminders)) {
					fs.writeFileSync(remindersPath, JSON.stringify(parsed, null, 2));
					console.log(`[reminders] Migrated ${parsed.reminders.length} reminder(s) from ${legacy}`);
					return;
				}
			} catch (e) {
				console.error(`[reminders] Could not migrate ${legacy}:`, e.message);
			}
		}
		fs.writeFileSync(remindersPath, JSON.stringify({
			reminders: []
		}, null, 2));
	}
}

function load() {
	ensure();
	return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(remindersPath, 'utf8'));
}

function save(data) {
	ensure();
	fs.writeFileSync(remindersPath, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
}

function generateId() {
	return Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);
}

function createReminder(opts) {
	const data = load();
	const reminder = {
		id: generateId(),
		guildId: opts.guildId,
		name: opts.name,
		emoji: opts.emoji || 'âš”ï¸',
		note: opts.note || '', // event rules / extra info shown in the reminder
		type: opts.type, // 'once' | 'daily' | 'every_x_days' | 'weekly' | 'monthly'
		hour: opts.hour,
		minute: opts.minute,
		date: opts.date || null,
		intervalDays: opts.intervalDays || null,
		weekdays: opts.weekdays || null,
		monthDay: opts.monthDay || null,
		channelId: opts.channelId,
		mention: opts.mention || '@everyone',
		offsets: opts.offsets || [60, 10],
		enabled: true,
		createdBy: opts.createdBy,
		createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
		lastFired: {},
		sent: [], // [{ offset, messageId, channelId, eventMs }]
	};
	data.reminders.push(reminder);
	save(data);
	return reminder;
}

function listReminders(guildId) {
	return load().reminders.filter((r) => r.guildId === guildId);
}

function getReminder(id) {
	return load().reminders.find((r) => r.id === id) || null;
}

function updateReminder(id, patch) {
	const data = load();
	const r = data.reminders.find((x) => x.id === id);
	if (!r) return null;
	// Only update explicitly supplied fields; preserve sent-message history and firing state.
	const allowed = ['name', 'emoji', 'note', 'hour', 'minute', 'offsets', 'channelId', 'mention', 'type', 'date', 'intervalDays', 'weekdays', 'monthDay', 'enabled'];
	for (const key of allowed) {
		if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(patch, key)) r[key] = patch[key];
	}
	r.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
	save(data);
	return r;
}

function deleteReminder(id) {
	const data = load();
	const before = data.reminders.length;
	data.reminders = data.reminders.filter((r) => r.id !== id);
	save(data);
	return before !== data.reminders.length;
}

function setEnabled(id, enabled) {
	const data = load();
	const r = data.reminders.find((x) => x.id === id);
	if (!r) return false;
	r.enabled = !!enabled;
	save(data);
	return true;
}

function updateLastFired(id, offset, iso) {
	const data = load();
	const r = data.reminders.find((x) => x.id === id);
	if (!r) return;
	r.lastFired = r.lastFired || {};
	r.lastFired[String(offset)] = iso;
	save(data);
}
/* ---------- Sent-message tracking (auto-remove) ---------- */
function addSent(id, entry) {
	const data = load();
	const r = data.reminders.find((x) => x.id === id);
	if (!r) return;
	r.sent = r.sent || [];
	r.sent.push(entry);
	save(data);
}
/** Earlier reminders (bigger offset) for the SAME event occurrence â€” removed once a newer one posts. */
function takeSuperseded(id, eventMs, offset) {
	const data = load();
	const r = data.reminders.find((x) => x.id === id);
	if (!r || !Array.isArray(r.sent)) return [];
	const old = r.sent.filter((s) => s.eventMs === eventMs && s.offset > offset);
	if (!old.length) return [];
	r.sent = r.sent.filter((s) => !old.includes(s));
	save(data);
	return old;
}
/**
 * Messages that should be deleted now:
 *  - offset > 0 â†’ at event start
 *  - offset = 0 ("starting now") â†’ 10 min after start
 */
function takeDueDeletions(nowMs = Date.now()) {
	const data = load();
	const out = [];
	let changed = false;
	for (const r of data.reminders) {
		if (!Array.isArray(r.sent) || !r.sent.length) continue;
		const keep = [];
		for (const s of r.sent) {
			const deleteAt = s.offset > 0 ? s.eventMs : s.eventMs + 10 * 60 * 1000;
			if (nowMs >= deleteAt) {
				out.push({
					reminderId: r.id,
					...s
				});
				changed = true;
			} else keep.push(s);
		}
		r.sent = keep;
	}
	if (changed) save(data);
	return out;
}
/** Reminders that should fire now â†’ [{ reminder, offset, eventTime }] */
function getDueReminders(now = new Date()) {
	const data = load();
	const due = [];
	const nowMs = now.getTime();
	for (const r of data.reminders) {
		if (!r.enabled) continue;
		const next = getNextOccurrence(r, new Date(nowMs - 120000));
		if (!next) continue;
		const eventMs = next.getTime();
		for (const offset of r.offsets || [60]) {
			const fireAt = eventMs - offset * 60 * 1000;
			if (nowMs >= fireAt && nowMs < fireAt + 90000) {
				const last = r.lastFired?.[String(offset)];
				if (last && Math.abs(new Date(last).getTime() - fireAt) < 5 * 60 * 1000) continue;
				due.push({
					reminder: r,
					offset,
					eventTime: next
				});
			}
		}
	}
	return due;
}
module.exports = {
	PRESET_EVENTS,
	createReminder,
	listReminders,
	getReminder,
	updateReminder,
	deleteReminder,
	setEnabled,
	updateLastFired,
	addSent,
	takeSuperseded,
	takeDueDeletions,
	getNextOccurrence,
	getDueReminders,
};

const {
	EmbedBuilder
} = require("discord.js");
const STATUS_LABEL = {
	success: "âœ… Success",
	already: "â„¹ï¸ Already redeemed",
	expired: "â° Expired",
	invalid: "âŒ Invalid code",
	error: "âš ï¸ Error",
	rate_limit: "ðŸš¦ Rate limited"
};
const STATUS_ORDER = ["success", "already", "rate_limit", "error", "invalid", "expired"];

function buildDetailedReport(code, results, summary, footer) {
	const groups = {};
	for (const r of results) {
		const st = r.status || "error";
		if (!groups[st]) groups[st] = [];
		groups[st].push(r)
	}
	const embeds = [];
	const main = (new EmbedBuilder).setTitle(`ðŸŽ Redeem â€” ${code}`).setColor((summary.success || 0) + (summary.already || 0) > 0 ? 5763719 : 15548997).addFields({
		name: "âœ… Success",
		value: String(summary.success || 0),
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: "â„¹ï¸ Already",
		value: String(summary.already || 0),
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: "ðŸš¦ Rate limit",
		value: String(summary.rate_limit || 0),
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: "âš ï¸ Errors",
		value: String(summary.error || 0),
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: "âŒ Invalid",
		value: String(summary.invalid || 0),
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: "â° Expired",
		value: String(summary.expired || 0),
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: "ðŸ“Š Total targets",
		value: String(results.length),
		inline: true
	}, {
		name: "ðŸ“ˆ Success rate",
		value: `${results.length?Math.round(((summary.success||0)+(summary.already||0))/results.length*100):0}%`,
		inline: true
	}).setFooter({
		text: footer || `${results.length} players Â· rewards â†’ in-game mail`
	}).setTimestamp();
	embeds.push(main);
	for (const st of STATUS_ORDER) {
		const list = groups[st];
		if (!list || !list.length) continue;
		const lines = list.map(r => {
			const name = (r.name || "Unknown").slice(0, 40);
			const extra = st === "error" || st === "invalid" || st === "rate_limit" ? ` â€” ${(r.message||"").slice(0,60)}` : "";
			return `â€¢ **${name}** \`${r.fid}\`${extra}`
		});
		let buf = "";
		let part = 1;
		for (const line of lines) {
			if ((buf + line + "\n").length > 3800) {
				embeds.push((new EmbedBuilder).setTitle(`${STATUS_LABEL[st]||st}${part>1?` (${part})`:""} â€” ${list.length} players`).setDescription(buf).setColor(st === "success" ? 5763719 : st === "already" ? 5793266 : 15548997));
				buf = "";
				part++
			}
			buf += line + "\n"
		}
		if (buf) {
			embeds.push((new EmbedBuilder).setTitle(`${STATUS_LABEL[st]||st}${part>1?` (${part})`:""} â€” ${list.length} players`).setDescription(buf).setColor(st === "success" ? 5763719 : st === "already" ? 5793266 : 15548997))
		}
	}
	return embeds.slice(0, 10)
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Asif\Downloads\kingshot-bot-2\lib\schedule.js =====
/**
 * Shared schedule helpers (UTC) â€” used by event reminders AND scheduled blogs.
 * Types: once | daily | every_x_days | weekly | monthly
 */
const DAY_NAMES = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'];
const DAY_MS = 86400000;
const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
/** "20:30", "20.30", "20" â†’ { hour, minute } | null */
function parseTime(raw) {
	const m = String(raw || '').trim().match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:\s*[:.]\s*(\d{1,2}))?$/);
	if (!m) return null;
	const hour = Number(m[1]);
	const minute = m[2] != null ? Number(m[2]) : 0;
	if (hour > 23 || minute > 59) return null;
	return {
		hour,
		minute
	};
}
/** "60,10" â†’ [60,10] (unique, descending, max 5) */
function parseOffsets(raw, fallback = [60, 10]) {
	const list = String(raw || '').split(',').map((x) => parseInt(x.trim(), 10)).filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= 10080);
	const uniq = [...new Set(list)].sort((a, b) => b - a).slice(0, 5);
	return uniq.length ? uniq : fallback;
}
/**
 * Validate the type-specific modal field.
 * @param {string} type
 * @param {(id:string)=>string} get  safe text-input getter ('' when missing)
 * @returns {{error:string}|{data:object}}
 */
function parseDetails(type, get) {
	if (type === 'once') {
		const date = get('date').trim();
		const d = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date) ? new Date(`${date}T00:00:00Z`) : null;
		if (!d || Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) || d.toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== date) {
			return {
				error: 'Date must be a real date like `2026-11-20`.'
			};
		}
		return {
			data: {
				date
			}
		};
	}
	if (type === 'every_x_days') {
		const n = parseInt(get('interval'), 10);
		if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 1 || n > 365) return {
			error: 'Interval must be 1â€“365 days.'
		};
		return {
			data: {
				intervalDays: n
			}
		};
	}
	if (type === 'weekly') {
		const days = [...new Set(get('weekdays').split(',').map((x) => parseInt(x.trim(), 10)).filter((n) => n >= 0 && n <= 6)), ].sort();
		if (!days.length) return {
			error: 'Weekdays must be numbers 0â€“6 (0=Sun â€¦ 6=Sat), e.g. `1,3,5`.'
		};
		return {
			data: {
				weekdays: days
			}
		};
	}
	if (type === 'monthly') {
		const n = parseInt(get('monthday'), 10);
		if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 1 || n > 31) return {
			error: 'Day of month must be 1â€“31.'
		};
		return {
			data: {
				monthDay: n
			}
		};
	}
	return {
		data: {}
	}; // daily
}

function formatSchedule(r) {
	const time = `${pad(r.hour)}:${pad(r.minute)} UTC`;
	switch (r.type) {
		case 'once':
			return `One-time Â· ${r.date} at ${time}`;
		case 'daily':
			return `Daily at ${time}`;
		case 'every_x_days':
			return `Every ${r.intervalDays} day(s) at ${time}`;
		case 'weekly':
			return `Weekly (${(r.weekdays || []).map((d) => DAY_NAMES[d]).join(', ')}) at ${time}`;
		case 'monthly':
			return `Monthly on day ${r.monthDay} at ${time}`;
		default:
			return time;
	}
}
/** Next occurrence (UTC) strictly after `from`, or null. */
function getNextOccurrence(r, from = new Date()) {
	const now = from.getTime();
	const h = r.hour;
	const m = r.minute;
	if (r.type === 'once') {
		if (!r.date) return null;
		const d = new Date(`${r.date}T${pad(h)}:${pad(m)}:00Z`);
		return d.getTime() > now ? d : null;
	}
	if (r.type === 'daily') {
		let d = new Date(Date.UTC(from.getUTCFullYear(), from.getUTCMonth(), from.getUTCDate(), h, m, 0));
		if (d.getTime() <= now) d = new Date(d.getTime() + DAY_MS);
		return d;
	}
	if (r.type === 'every_x_days') {
		const interval = Math.max(1, Number(r.intervalDays) || 1);
		const anchor = r.date ? new Date(`${r.date}T00:00:00Z`) : new Date(r.createdAt || now);
		let d = new Date(Date.UTC(anchor.getUTCFullYear(), anchor.getUTCMonth(), anchor.getUTCDate(), h, m, 0));
		if (d.getTime() <= now) {
			const steps = Math.floor((now - d.getTime()) / (interval * DAY_MS)) + 1;
			d = new Date(d.getTime() + steps * interval * DAY_MS);
		}
		return d;
	}
	if (r.type === 'weekly') {
		const days = (r.weekdays || []).map(Number);
		if (!days.length) return null;
		for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			const d = new Date(Date.UTC(from.getUTCFullYear(), from.getUTCMonth(), from.getUTCDate() + i, h, m, 0));
			if (d.getTime() <= now) continue;
			if (days.includes(d.getUTCDay())) return d;
		}
		return null;
	}
	if (r.type === 'monthly') {
		const day = Number(r.monthDay) || 1;
		const y = from.getUTCFullYear();
		const mo = from.getUTCMonth();
		// Skip months that don't have this day (e.g. 31st) instead of rolling into the next month
		for (let i = 0; i < 24; i++) {
			const d = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo + i, day, h, m, 0));
			if (d.getUTCDate() === day && d.getUTCMonth() === (mo + i) % 12 && d.getTime() > now) return d;
		}
		return null;
	}
	return null;
}
module.exports = {
	DAY_NAMES,
	pad,
	parseTime,
	parseOffsets,
	parseDetails,
	formatSchedule,
	getNextOccurrence
};

async function searchPlayers(query) {
	const url = `https://mightpulse.com/api/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&limit=40&live=0`;
	const res = await fetch(url, {
		headers: {
			Accept: "application/json",
			"User-Agent": "GiftCodeBot/2.0"
		}
	});
	if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Player search unavailable (${res.status})`);
	const data = await res.json();
	const results = Array.isArray(data.results) ? data.results : [];
	const mapped = results.map(r => {
		const fid = r.fid != null ? String(r.fid) : r.uid != null && r.nick_name ? String(r.uid) : null;
		const playerId = r.fid != null ? String(r.fid) : null;
		if (!playerId) return null;
		const name = String(r.nick_name || r.name || playerId).replace(/\u00a0/g, " ").trim();
		return {
			fid: playerId,
			uid: r.uid != null ? String(r.uid) : null,
			name,
			kid: r.kid != null ? Number(r.kid) : null,
			tc: r.stove_lv ?? r.town_center_level ?? null,
			power: r.power ?? null,
			alliance: r.alliance_abbr || r.alliance_name || null,
			allianceName: r.alliance_name || null
		}
	}).filter(Boolean);
	const map = new Map;
	for (const p of mapped) {
		if (!map.has(p.fid)) map.set(p.fid, p)
	}
	return Array.from(map.values())
}
async function searchAlliances(query, kidFilter = null) {
	const url = `https://mightpulse.com/api/search/alliances?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&limit=40`;
	const res = await fetch(url, {
		headers: {
			Accept: "application/json",
			"User-Agent": "GiftCodeBot/2.0"
		}
	});
	if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Alliance search unavailable (${res.status})`);
	const data = await res.json();
	let results = Array.isArray(data.results) ? data.results : [];
	if (kidFilter != null && kidFilter !== "") {
		const kid = Number(kidFilter);
		results = results.filter(r => Number(r.kid) === kid)
	}
	return results.map(r => ({
		aid: r.aid,
		kid: Number(r.kid),
		name: String(r.name || "").replace(/\u00a0/g, " ").trim(),
		abbr: String(r.abbr || r.slug || "").trim(),
		slug: String(r.slug || r.abbr || "").trim(),
		leader: r.leader_name || null,
		members: r.member_count ?? r.count ?? null,
		power: r.power ?? null
	}))
}
module.exports = {
	searchPlayers,
	searchAlliances
};

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
// Use a project-local data folder by default.
// On Windows, ignore Unix-style DATADIR values such as /tmp/kingshot-bot.
const configuredDataDir = process.env.DATADIR;
const dataDir = configuredDataDir && !(process.platform === 'win32' && configuredDataDir.startsWith('/')) ? path.resolve(configuredDataDir) : path.join(__dirname, '..', 'data');
const membersPath = path.join(dataDir, 'members.json');
const historyPath = path.join(dataDir, 'history.json');
const seenPath = path.join(dataDir, 'seen_codes.json');
const autoPath = path.join(dataDir, 'auto_state.json');
const blacklistPath = path.join(dataDir, 'blacklist.json');
const lastResultsPath = path.join(dataDir, 'last_results.json');
const backupsDir = path.join(dataDir, 'backups');

function ensure() {
	if (!fs.existsSync(dataDir)) fs.mkdirSync(dataDir, {
		recursive: true
	});
	if (!fs.existsSync(membersPath)) {
		fs.writeFileSync(membersPath, JSON.stringify({
			members: [],
			syncedAt: null
		}, null, 2));
	}
	if (!fs.existsSync(historyPath)) {
		fs.writeFileSync(historyPath, JSON.stringify({
			runs: []
		}, null, 2));
	}
	if (!fs.existsSync(seenPath)) {
		fs.writeFileSync(seenPath, JSON.stringify({
			codes: {}
		}, null, 2));
	}
	if (!fs.existsSync(autoPath)) {
		fs.writeFileSync(autoPath, JSON.stringify({
			enabled: false,
			lastScanAt: null,
			lastRedeemAt: null,
			extraDelayMs: 0,
			lastBackupAt: null,
		}, null, 2));
	}
	if (!fs.existsSync(blacklistPath)) {
		fs.writeFileSync(blacklistPath, JSON.stringify({
			codes: []
		}, null, 2));
	}
	if (!fs.existsSync(lastResultsPath)) {
		fs.writeFileSync(lastResultsPath, JSON.stringify({
			byCode: {}
		}, null, 2));
	}
	if (!fs.existsSync(backupsDir)) fs.mkdirSync(backupsDir, {
		recursive: true
	});
}

function loadMembers() {
	ensure();
	return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(membersPath, 'utf8'));
}

function saveMembers(data) {
	ensure();
	fs.writeFileSync(membersPath, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));
}

function loadHistory() {
	ensure();
	return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(historyPath, 'utf8'));
}

function pushHistory(entry) {
	const h = loadHistory();
	h.runs.unshift(entry);
	h.runs = h.runs.slice(0, 50);
	fs.writeFileSync(historyPath, JSON.stringify(h, null, 2));
}

function loadSeen() {
	ensure();
	return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(seenPath, 'utf8'));
}

function saveSeen(seen) {
	ensure();
	fs.writeFileSync(seenPath, JSON.stringify(seen, null, 2));
}
/** Legacy: whole-code done (old data). Prefer per-fid. */
function isCodeDone(code) {
	const entry = loadSeen().codes?.[code];
	if (!entry) return false;
	if (entry.fids && typeof entry.fids === 'object') {
		return false;
	}
	return true;
}

function getCodeFidStatus(code, fid) {
	const entry = loadSeen().codes?.[code];
	if (!entry?.fids) return null;
	return entry.fids[String(fid)] || null;
}
/**
 * Members who still need this code:
 * - not success / already
 * - rate_limit / error / missing â†’ include
 */
function getPendingMembers(code, allMembers) {
	const entry = loadSeen().codes?.[code];
	if (!entry) return allMembers;
	const fids = entry.fids && typeof entry.fids === 'object' ? entry.fids : null;
	// Legacy record: code was processed but no per-player map was saved.
	// Do NOT re-redeem the whole roster (avoids spam after restart).
	// New members after this will need a one-time /redeem run or a later cycle
	// once fids exist â€” prefer migrating by treating legacy as complete.
	if (!fids || Object.keys(fids).length === 0) {
		if (entry.summary || entry.lastSummary || entry.source || entry.at) {
			console.log(`[seen] ${code}: legacy complete record (no fids) â€” skip full re-run`);
			return [];
		}
		return allMembers;
	}
	// Per-player: new players + rate-limited only.
	// Do not auto-retry hard errors every cycle (avoids channel spam).
	return allMembers.filter((m) => {
		const st = fids[String(m.fid)];
		if (st === 'success' || st === 'already' || st === 'invalid' || st === 'expired') return false;
		if (st === 'error') return false;
		return true;
	});
}

function countFidStatuses(code) {
	const entry = loadSeen().codes?.[code];
	const fids = entry?.fids || {};
	const counts = {
		success: 0,
		already: 0,
		rate_limit: 0,
		error: 0,
		expired: 0,
		invalid: 0,
		other: 0
	};
	for (const st of Object.values(fids)) {
		if (counts[st] != null) counts[st]++;
		else counts.other++;
	}
	return {
		total: Object.keys(fids).length,
		...counts
	};
}
/** Merge redeem results into per-fid map for this code. */
function recordFidResults(code, results, meta = {}) {
	const seen = loadSeen();
	const prev = seen.codes[code] || {};
	const fids = {
		...(prev.fids || {})
	};
	for (const r of results) {
		if (r.fid == null) continue;
		fids[String(r.fid)] = r.status || 'error';
	}
	const rateLimitCount = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'rate_limit').length;
	const total = results.length || 1;
	const highRateLimit = rateLimitCount / total > 0.2;
	seen.codes[code] = {
		...prev,
		...meta,
		at: new Date().toISOString(),
		fids,
		lastSummary: results.reduce((acc, r) => {
			acc[r.status] = (acc[r.status] || 0) + 1;
			return acc;
		}, {}),
		// Keep processing next cycles if many rate limits or incomplete
		complete: !highRateLimit && results.every((r) => r.status === 'success' || r.status === 'already' || r.status === 'invalid' || r.status === 'expired'),
	};
	saveSeen(seen);
	return seen.codes[code];
}
/** @deprecated prefer recordFidResults â€” kept for compatibility */
function markCodeDone(code, meta = {}) {
	const seen = loadSeen();
	const prev = seen.codes[code] || {};
	seen.codes[code] = {
		...prev,
		at: new Date().toISOString(),
		...meta,
	};
	saveSeen(seen);
}

	ensure();
	fs.writeFileSync(autoPath, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2));
